EnterpriseLab provides enterprises a practical path to deploying capable, privacy-preserving agents without compromising operational capability.

</details>


### [72] [Silicon Bureaucracy and AI Test-Oriented Education: Contamination Sensitivity and Score Confidence in LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21636)
*Yiliang Song,Hongjun An,Jiangan Chen,Xuanchen Yan,Huan Song,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对LLM基准测试污染敏感性的审计框架，发现基准测试分数可能无法真实反映模型泛化能力，建议引入置信度审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前公共基准测试主导着大语言模型的排名、选择和部署，形成了一种“硅基官僚体系”和“AI应试教育”。然而，这种体系基于一个脆弱假设：基准测试分数直接反映真实的泛化能力。实际上，由于现代训练管道中难以排除数据污染和语义泄漏，分数可能混淆了应试能力与真正的能力。

Method: 提出一个审计框架，使用router-worker设置，将干净的对照组与噪声条件进行对比。在噪声条件下，基准测试问题在被传递下游之前会被系统性删除、重写和扰动。对于真正干净的基准测试，噪声条件不应持续优于干净对照组基线。

Result: 在多个模型中，发现噪声条件下存在广泛但异质的超基线增益，表明基准测试相关线索可能被重组并能重新激活污染相关的记忆。这意味着相似的基准测试分数可能承载着截然不同的置信度水平。

Conclusion: 不应完全拒绝基准测试，而应在基于基准测试的评估中补充明确的污染敏感性审计和分数置信度评估，以提高评估的可靠性和有效性。

Abstract: Public benchmarks increasingly govern how large language models (LLMs) are ranked, selected, and deployed. We frame this benchmark-centered regime as Silicon Bureaucracy and AI Test-Oriented Education, and argue that it rests on a fragile assumption: that benchmark scores directly reflect genuine generalization. In practice, however, such scores may conflate exam-oriented competence with principled capability, especially when contamination and semantic leakage are difficult to exclude from modern training pipelines. We therefore propose an audit framework for analyzing contamination sensitivity and score confidence in LLM benchmarks. Using a router-worker setup, we compare a clean-control condition with noisy conditions in which benchmark problems are systematically deleted, rewritten, and perturbed before being passed downstream. For a genuinely clean benchmark, noisy conditions should not consistently outperform the clean-control baseline. Yet across multiple models, we find widespread but heterogeneous above-baseline gains under noisy conditions, indicating that benchmark-related cues may be reassembled and can reactivate contamination-related memory. These results suggest that similar benchmark scores may carry substantially different levels of confidence. Rather than rejecting benchmarks altogether, we argue that benchmark-based evaluation should be supplemented with explicit audits of contamination sensitivity and score confidence.

</details>


### [73] [Mirage The Illusion of Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21687)
*Mohammad Asadi,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Fang Cao,Tahoura Nedaee,Kamyar Fardi,Fei-Fei Li,Ehsan Adeli,Euan Ashley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模态AI模型在没有图像输入的情况下仍能生成详细描述并在基准测试中获得高分，这种"幻影推理"现象揭示了现有评估方法的严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态AI系统在现实任务中表现出色，但其视觉-语言推理的潜在机制仍知之甚少。现有假设认为模型通过处理和整合视觉信息进行推理，本文旨在挑战这一主流假设，探究模型是否真正依赖视觉输入。

Method: 研究者进行了三组关键实验：1）测试前沿模型在没有提供图像时是否会生成详细的图像描述和推理路径；2）在没有任何图像输入的情况下评估模型在通用和医学多模态基准上的表现；3）对比显式指令（明确告知猜测答案）与隐式提示（暗示图像存在）下的模型表现差异。同时提出B-Clean方法用于公平的、基于视觉的评估。

Result: 发现三个核心结果：1）前沿模型会为从未提供的图像生成详细描述和临床发现，称为"幻影推理"；2）无图像输入时模型在通用和医学基准上得分惊人地高，极端案例中模型在无图像访问情况下获得胸部X光问答基准榜首；3）显式猜测指令下性能显著下降，表明存在两种不同响应模式：保守猜测模式与幻影模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了视觉-语言模型推理和评估的根本性脆弱，暴露了现有基准测试可通过文本线索实现非视觉推理的缺陷。在医学等高风险领域，AI系统的错误校准可能带来严重后果，亟需消除文本线索的私有基准。B-Clean被提出作为多模态AI系统公平、基于视觉评估的原则性解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal AI systems have achieved remarkable performance across a broad range of real-world tasks, yet the mechanisms underlying visual-language reasoning remain surprisingly poorly understood. We report three findings that challenge prevailing assumptions about how these systems process and integrate visual information. First, Frontier models readily generate detailed image descriptions and elaborate reasoning traces, including pathology-biased clinical findings, for images never provided; we term this phenomenon mirage reasoning. Second, without any image input, models also attain strikingly high scores across general and medical multimodal benchmarks, bringing into question their utility and design. In the most extreme case, our model achieved the top rank on a standard chest X-ray question-answering benchmark without access to any images. Third, when models were explicitly instructed to guess answers without image access, rather than being implicitly prompted to assume images were present, performance declined markedly. Explicit guessing appears to engage a more conservative response regime, in contrast to the mirage regime in which models behave as though images have been provided. These findings expose fundamental vulnerabilities in how visual-language models reason and are evaluated, pointing to an urgent need for private benchmarks that eliminate textual cues enabling non-visual inference, particularly in medical contexts where miscalibrated AI carries the greatest consequence. We introduce B-Clean as a principled solution for fair, vision-grounded evaluation of multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [74] [AI Token Futures Market: Commoditization of Compute and Derivatives Contract Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21690)
*Yicai Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI推理tokens视为新兴大宗商品，设计了标准化的tokens期货合约，并通过仿真证明其能显著降低企业算力成本波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和视觉-语言-动作模型的广泛应用，AI推理所消耗的tokens正演变为一种新型商品。为了解决应用层企业面临的算力成本波动风险，作者主张将tokens从智能服务输出转化为算力基础设施原材料，并借鉴传统大宗商品（如电力、碳排放配额、带宽）的经验，推动其金融化进程。

Method: 论文基于电力期货市场历史经验和大宗商品金融化理论，首先定义了标准推理代币（SIT），并设计了完整的tokens期货合约方案（包括合约规格、结算机制、保证金制度和做市商机制）。随后，构建了均值回复跳跃扩散随机过程模型，采用蒙特卡洛模拟方法对期货合约的套期保值效率进行评估。

Result: 仿真结果显示，在应用层需求爆发场景下，tokens期货合约能够将企业算力成本波动降低62%-78%。研究还分析了GPU算力期货的可行性，并探讨了tokens期货市场的监管框架。

Conclusion: 本文为算力资源的金融化提供了理论基础与实践路线图，论证了tokens期货在管理算力成本风险方面的有效性，为未来AI算力市场的规范化发展指明了方向。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) and vision-language-action models (VLAs) become widely deployed, the tokens consumed by AI inference are evolving into a new type of commodity. This paper systematically analyzes the commodity attributes of tokens, arguing for their transition from intelligent service outputs to compute infrastructure raw materials, and draws comparisons with established commodities such as electricity, carbon emission allowances, and bandwidth. Building on the historical experience of electricity futures markets and the theory of commodity financialization, we propose a complete design for standardized token futures contracts, including the definition of a Standard Inference Token (SIT), contract specifications, settlement mechanisms, margin systems, and market-maker regimes. By constructing a mean-reverting jump-diffusion stochastic process model and conducting Monte Carlo simulations, we evaluate the hedging efficiency of the proposed futures contracts for application-layer enterprises. Simulation results show that, under an application-layer demand explosion scenario, token futures can reduce enterprise compute cost volatility by 62%-78%. We also explore the feasibility of GPU compute futures and discuss the regulatory framework for token futures markets, providing a theoretical foundation and practical roadmap for the financialization of compute resources.

</details>


### [75] [Deterministic Hallucination Detection in Medical VQA via Confidence-Evidence Bayesian Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21693)
*Mohammad Asadi,Tahoura Nedaee,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CEBaG，一种确定性、无需外部模型的自包含幻觉检测方法，在16个实验设置中13个取得最高AUC，平均优于VASE 8个AUC点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法（如SE和VASE）需要大量随机采样和外部NLI模型，计算成本高且难以部署；幻觉响应在模型自身对数概率中表现出独特特征。

Method: 提出Confidence-Evidence Bayesian Gain (CEBaG)，结合两个互补信号：token级预测方差（捕获不一致置信度）和证据幅度（测量图像相对于纯文本推理的预测偏移），无需随机采样、外部模型或任务特定超参数。

Result: 在4个医学MLLM和3个VQA基准（16个实验设置）上评估，CEBaG在13/16设置中取得最高AUC，平均比VASE提升8个AUC点，且完全确定性和自包含。

Conclusion: CEBaG为医学VQA中的MLLM幻觉检测提供了一种高效、实用的解决方案，显著降低了计算成本同时提升了检测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for medical Visual Question Answering (VQA), yet they remain prone to hallucinations, defined as generating responses that contradict the input image, posing serious risks in clinical settings. Current hallucination detection methods, such as Semantic Entropy (SE) and Vision-Amplified Semantic Entropy (VASE), require 10 to 20 stochastic generations per sample together with an external natural language inference model for semantic clustering, making them computationally expensive and difficult to deploy in practice. We observe that hallucinated responses exhibit a distinctive signature directly in the model's own log-probabilities: inconsistent token-level confidence and weak sensitivity to visual evidence. Based on this observation, we propose Confidence-Evidence Bayesian Gain (CEBaG), a deterministic hallucination detection method that requires no stochastic sampling, no external models, and no task-specific hyperparameters. CEBaG combines two complementary signals: token-level predictive variance, which captures inconsistent confidence across response tokens, and evidence magnitude, which measures how much the image shifts per-token predictions relative to text-only inference. Evaluated across four medical MLLMs and three VQA benchmarks (16 experimental settings), CEBaG achieves the highest AUC in 13 of 16 settings and improves over VASE by 8 AUC points on average, while being fully deterministic and self-contained. The code will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [76] [MIND: Multi-agent inference for negotiation dialogue in travel planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21696)
*Hunmin Do,Taejun Yoon,Kiyong Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MIND框架，通过引入基于心理理论(ToM)的战略评估阶段来推断谈判对手的意愿，在旅行规划等多利益相关者场景中显著优于传统多智能体辩论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体辩论(MAD)研究在协调复杂利益相关者（如旅行规划）方面的有效性尚未得到充分探索，缺乏能够模拟现实中不同偏好旅行者之间共识构建的框架。

Method: 提出MIND（Multi-agent Inference for Negotiation Dialogue）框架，基于心理理论(Theory of Mind)引入战略评估阶段，通过语言细微差别推断对手意愿(w)，准确率达90.2%，从而模拟现实的共识构建过程。

Result: MIND在High-w Hit指标上提升20.5%，在Debate Hit-Rate上提高30.7%，有效优先处理高风险约束；LLM-as-a-Judge定性评估显示，在理性(68.8%)和流畅性(72.4%)方面超越基线，总体胜率达68.3%。

Conclusion: MIND有效建模人类谈判动态，能够推导出具有说服力的共识，验证了该方法在多利益相关者协调场景中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: While Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) research has advanced, its efficacy in coordinating complex stakeholder interests such as travel planning remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose MIND (Multi-agent Inference for Negotiation Dialogue), a framework designed to simulate realistic consensus-building among travelers with heterogeneous preferences. Grounded in the Theory of Mind (ToM), MIND introduces a Strategic Appraisal phase that infers opponent willingness (w) from linguistic nuances with 90.2% accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that MIND outperforms traditional MAD frameworks, achieving a 20.5% improvement in High-w Hit and a 30.7% increase in Debate Hit-Rate, effectively prioritizing high-stakes constraints. Furthermore, qualitative evaluations via LLM-as-a-Judge confirm that MIND surpasses baselines in Rationality (68.8%) and Fluency (72.4%), securing an overall win rate of 68.3%. These findings validate that MIND effectively models human negotiation dynamics to derive persuasive consensus.

</details>


### [77] [A Blueprint for Self-Evolving Coding Agents in Vehicle Aerodynamic Drag Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21698)
*Jinhui Ren,Huaiming Li,Yabin Liu,Tao Li,Zhaokun Liu,Yujia Liang,Zengle Ge,Chufan Wu,Xiaomin Yuan,Danyu Liu,Annan Li,Jianmin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于合约的自演化代码代理框架，用于自动发现可执行的车辆阻力系数预测代理管道，解决了工业设计中的工作流摩擦问题，实现了高吞吐量设计探索与高保真CFD验证的协同工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真车辆阻力评估的主要瓶颈不在于求解器运行时间，而在于工作流摩擦：几何清理、网格重试、队列竞争和跨团队的可重复性失败。现有方法缺乏自动化、可重复和可审计的解决方案，亟需一种能够在工业约束下自动发现和优化代理模型的方法。

Method: 将代理发现问题建模为对程序的约束优化问题而非静态模型实例。结合Famou-Agent风格的评估器反馈、基于种群的海岛进化、结构化变异（涵盖数据、模型、损失和分割策略）以及多目标选择机制（平衡排序质量、稳定性和成本）。通过严格的评估合约强制执行泄漏预防、确定性重放、多种子鲁棒性和资源预算，构建"筛选-升级"的部署模式。

Result: 在八个匿名进化操作符测试中，最佳系统达到综合得分0.9335，符号准确率0.9180。轨迹分析和消融研究表明，自适应采样和海岛迁移是收敛质量的主要驱动因素。系统成功实现了高通量设计排序，同时保持决策级可靠性。

Conclusion: 建立了一套可审计、可重用的工作流程，在保持决策级可靠性、治理可追溯性和安全边界的前提下，有效加速了气动设计迭代。该框架为工业设计提供了从代理模型快速筛选到高保真CFD验证的完整解决方案路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity vehicle drag evaluation is constrained less by solver runtime than by workflow friction: geometry cleanup, meshing retries, queue contention, and reproducibility failures across teams. We present a contract-centric blueprint for self-evolving coding agents that discover executable surrogate pipelines for predicting drag coefficient $C_d$ under industrial constraints. The method formulates surrogate discovery as constrained optimization over programs, not static model instances, and combines Famou-Agent-style evaluator feedback with population-based island evolution, structured mutations (data, model, loss, and split policies), and multi-objective selection balancing ranking quality, stability, and cost. A hard evaluation contract enforces leakage prevention, deterministic replay, multi-seed robustness, and resource budgets before any candidate is admitted. Across eight anonymized evolutionary operators, the best system reaches a Combined Score of 0.9335 with sign-accuracy 0.9180, while trajectory and ablation analyses show that adaptive sampling and island migration are primary drivers of convergence quality. The deployment model is explicitly ``screen-and-escalate'': surrogates provide high-throughput ranking for design exploration, but low-confidence or out-of-distribution cases are automatically escalated to high-fidelity CFD. The resulting contribution is an auditable, reusable workflow for accelerating aerodynamic design iteration while preserving decision-grade reliability, governance traceability, and safety boundaries.

</details>


### [78] [Compensating Visual Insufficiency with Stratified Language Guidance for Long-Tail Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21708)
*Xi Wang,Xu Yang,Donghao Sun,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用语言知识的分层语言树结构，通过分层自适应语言指导和分层对齐语言指导，有效解决了长尾类增量学习中的数据不平衡和灾难性遗忘问题，实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾类增量学习（LT CIL）面临巨大挑战：尾部类别的样本稀缺不仅阻碍了这些类别的学习，还在持续演变和不平衡的数据分布下加剧了灾难性遗忘问题。现有方法难以有效处理数据不平衡与知识保留之间的矛盾。

Method: 1. 分析LT CIL数据分布，指导大型语言模型（LLMs）生成分层语言树，从粗到细粒度层级化组织语义信息；2. 提出分层自适应语言指导，利用可学习权重融合多尺度语义表示，实现对尾部类别的动态监督调整；3. 引入分层对齐语言指导，利用语言树的结构稳定性约束优化过程，增强语义视觉对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，所提方法实现了最先进的性能，有效证明了该方法在处理长尾类增量学习问题上的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过引入语言知识的分层结构，本文方法成功地在解决数据不平衡和缓解灾难性遗忘之间取得了平衡，为长尾类增量学习提供了一个新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Long-tail class incremental learning (LT CIL) remains highly challenging because the scarcity of samples in tail classes not only hampers their learning but also exacerbates catastrophic forgetting under continuously evolving and imbalanced data distributions. To tackle these issues, we exploit the informativeness and scalability of language knowledge. Specifically, we analyze the LT CIL data distribution to guide large language models (LLMs) in generating a stratified language tree that hierarchically organizes semantic information from coarse to fine grained granularity. Building upon this structure, we introduce stratified adaptive language guidance, which leverages learnable weights to merge multi-scale semantic representations, thereby enabling dynamic supervisory adjustment for tail classes and alleviating the impact of data imbalance. Furthermore, we introduce stratified alignment language guidance, which exploits the structural stability of the language tree to constrain optimization and reinforce semantic visual alignment, thereby alleviating catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state of the art performance.

</details>


### [79] [CurvZO: Adaptive Curvature-Guided Sparse Zeroth-Order Optimization for Efficient LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21725)
*Shuo Wang,Ziyu Chen,Ming Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CurvZO方法，通过在线追踪曲率信号引导稀疏零阶优化，在保持内存高效的同时显著提升大模型微调性能与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反向传播微调大语言模型虽性能高但内存开销大；零阶优化虽内存高效但存在梯度估计方差大、收敛慢且不稳定的问题；现有稀疏零阶更新方法依赖参数选择，而在零阶优化中仅有的标量反馈使得选择信息丰富的参数具有挑战性。

Method: 提出自适应曲率引导的稀疏零阶优化（CurvZO）：1）从标量零阶反馈中在线追踪曲率信号；2）利用曲率信号构建参数级采样分布，用于选择每次更新的坐标，从而降低稀疏零阶梯度估计器的方差；3）动态调整扰动预算以适应演化的曲率信号分布，使稀疏更新既专注又具探索性。

Result: 在OPT和Llama模型及多种NLP任务上的广泛实验表明：相比零阶基线方法，CurvZO持续提升微调性能并减少训练时间；准确率提升最高达4.4个点，训练速度提升最高达2倍，同时保持内存高效。

Conclusion: CurvZO成功解决了零阶优化中的高方差与收敛问题，为资源受限场景下的大语言模型微调提供了一种高效、实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) with backpropagation achieves high performance but incurs substantial memory overhead, limiting scalability on resource-constrained hardware. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization provides a memory-efficient alternative by relying solely on forward passes, yet it typically suffers from slow or unstable convergence due to high-variance gradient estimates. Sparse ZO updates partially address this issue by perturbing only a subset of parameters, but their effectiveness hinges on selecting informative parameters, which is challenging in ZO optimization because each query yields only scalar feedback. We propose \textbf{Adaptive Curvature-Guided Sparse Zeroth-Order Optimization (CurvZO)}, which tracks curvature signals online from scalar ZO feedback and leverages these signals to construct a parameter-wise sampling distribution for selecting coordinates at each update, reducing the variance of the sparse ZO gradient estimator. Moreover, CurvZO dynamically adapts the perturbation budget to the evolving curvature signal distribution, yielding sparse ZO updates that remain both focused and sufficiently exploratory. Extensive experiments on OPT and Llama across diverse NLP tasks show that CurvZO consistently improves fine-tuning performance and reduces training time over ZO baselines. It improves accuracy by up to 4.4 points and achieves up to a $2\times$ speedup, while preserving memory efficiency.

</details>


### [80] [EvoIdeator: Evolving Scientific Ideas through Checklist-Grounded Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21728)
*Andreas Sauter,Yuyue Zhao,Jacopo Urbani,Wenxiang Hu,Zaiqiao Meng,Lun Zhou,Xiaohui Yan,Yougang Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoIdeator框架，通过将强化学习训练目标与基于检查清单的反馈对齐，实现科学想法的迭代进化，显著提升想法质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习范式依赖基于评分标准的标量奖励，仅提供全局质量分数而缺乏可操作的粒度；基于语言的优化方法通常局限于推理时的提示，未能显式优化模型以内化这些批评。

Method: 利用结构化评判模型生成两类协同信号：(1)用于多维优化的字典序奖励；(2)提供关于基础、可行性和方法论严谨性的细粒度语言反馈；将这些信号整合到强化学习循环中。

Result: 基于Qwen3-4B的EvoIdeator在关键科学指标上显著优于更大规模的前沿模型；学习到的策略能够强泛化到多样化的外部反馈源，无需进一步微调。

Conclusion: EvoIdeator为自精炼的自主构想提供了一条可扩展且严谨的路径，展示了将细粒度反馈融入强化学习训练的有效性。

Abstract: Scientific idea generation is a cornerstone of autonomous knowledge discovery, yet the iterative evolution required to transform initial concepts into high-quality research proposals remains a formidable challenge for Large Language Models (LLMs). Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL) paradigms often rely on rubric-based scalar rewards that provide global quality scores but lack actionable granularity. Conversely, language-based refinement methods are typically confined to inference-time prompting, targeting models that are not explicitly optimized to internalize such critiques. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{EvoIdeator}, a framework that facilitates the evolution of scientific ideas by aligning the RL training objective with \textbf{checklist-grounded feedback}. EvoIdeator leverages a structured judge model to generate two synergistic signals: (1) \emph{lexicographic rewards} for multi-dimensional optimization, and (2) \emph{fine-grained language feedback} that offers span-level critiques regarding grounding, feasibility, and methodological rigor. By integrating these signals into the RL loop, we condition the policy to systematically utilize precise feedback during both optimization and inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoIdeator, built on Qwen3-4B, significantly outperforms much larger frontier models across key scientific metrics. Crucially, the learned policy exhibits strong generalization to diverse external feedback sources without further fine-tuning, offering a scalable and rigorous path toward self-refining autonomous ideation.

</details>


### [81] [The Reasoning Error About Reasoning: Why Different Types of Reasoning Require Different Representational Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21736)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含可操作性、一致性、结构保持性和组合性四个属性的结构化框架，揭示了推理类型的结构性边界：边界以下的推理可在联想/概率表征上运行，边界以上的推理（如演绎）需完全满足四属性且无法通过概率方法逼近或仅靠统计学习规模扩展跨越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同类型的推理对表征系统施加不同的结构性需求，但心理学、AI和心智哲学领域缺乏对这些需求的系统性解释。现有研究未能清晰界定不同推理类型所需的结构性前提，也未能说明为何某些推理（如演绎）无法通过概率性或统计学习实现。

Method: 提出一个理论框架，识别表征系统的四个结构性属性：可操作性、一致性、结构保持性和组合性。分析不同推理类型（归纳、类比、因果推理、演绎、形式逻辑）对这些属性的需求程度，并界定一个主要的结构性边界。采用跨学科方法，整合AI评估、发展心理学和认知神经科学的证据。

Result: 发现一个主要的结构性边界：边界以下的推理类型可以在联想性和概率性表征上运行；边界以上的推理类型需要完全满足全部四个属性。统计学习的规模扩展若无结构性重组，不足以跨越此边界，因为演绎推理所需的结构性保证无法通过概率手段逼近。框架得到来自AI评估、发展心理学和认知神经科学不同直接性程度的证据支持。

Conclusion: 该框架是一个必要条件说明，对表征格式持不可知论，旨在重组现有辩论而非终结它们。研究提出了三个可检验预测：复合退化、对靶向结构破坏的选择性脆弱性、以及在规模扩展下的不可还原性，为未来研究提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Different types of reasoning impose different structural demands on representational systems, yet no systematic account of these demands exists across psychology, AI, and philosophy of mind. I propose a framework identifying four structural properties of representational systems: operability, consistency, structural preservation, and compositionality. These properties are demanded to different degrees by different forms of reasoning, from induction through analogy and causal inference to deduction and formal logic. Each property excludes a distinct class of reasoning failure. The analysis reveals a principal structural boundary: reasoning types below it can operate on associative, probabilistic representations, while those above it require all four properties to be fully satisfied. Scaling statistical learning without structural reorganization is insufficient to cross this boundary, because the structural guarantees required by deductive reasoning cannot be approximated through probabilistic means. Converging evidence from AI evaluation, developmental psychology, and cognitive neuroscience supports the framework at different levels of directness. Three testable predictions are derived, including compounding degradation, selective vulnerability to targeted structural disruption, and irreducibility under scaling. The framework is a necessary-condition account, agnostic about representational format, that aims to reorganize existing debates rather than close them.

</details>


### [82] [The Presupposition Problem in Representation Genesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21745)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLMs未经历从非表征系统到表征系统的“表征起源”过程，使起源问题变得紧迫；现有主要哲学框架在解释起源时普遍存在“表征预设”结构，导致“表征回归”困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型首次在未明确经历表征起源的情况下展现出高认知表现，这使表征起源问题变得紧迫：如果起源未发生，哪些认知能力会受影响？现有概念资源无法回答此问题。

Method: 对哲学心灵主要框架（思维语言假说、目的语义学、预测处理、生成主义、发生现象学）进行结构性分析，识别其在解释表征起源时的共同特征——表征预设结构，并论证其导致的表征回归问题。

Result: 发现现有框架在解释表征起源时都存在系统性解释延宕：在某个解释步骤上，所使用的概念的解释力依赖于系统已被组织为表征者。确定了任何避免此模式的解释理论必须满足的两个最低充分条件。

Conclusion: 这是对问题的概念诊断而非新理论，建立了问题的结构；LLMs使缺乏此类理论变得具有实质性后果，而不仅仅是理论问题。

Abstract: Large language models are the first systems to achieve high cognitive performance without clearly undergoing representation genesis: the transition from a non-representing physical system to one whose states guide behavior in a content-sensitive way. Prior cognitive systems had already made this transition before we could examine it, and philosophy of mind treated genesis as a background condition rather than an explanatory target. LLMs provide a case that does not clearly involve this transition, making the genesis question newly urgent: if genesis did not occur, which cognitive capacities are affected, and why? We currently lack the conceptual resources to answer this. The reason, this paper argues, is structural. Major frameworks in philosophy of mind, including the Language of Thought hypothesis, teleosemantics, predictive processing, enactivism, and genetic phenomenology, share a common feature when applied to the genesis question: at some explanatory step, each deploys concepts whose explanatory purchase depends on the system already being organized as a representer. This pattern, which we call the Representation Presupposition structure, generates systematic explanatory deferral. Attempts to explain the first acquisition of content-manipulable representation within the existing categorical vocabulary import resources from the representational side of the transition itself. We call this the Representation Regress. The paper offers a conceptual diagnosis rather than a new theory, establishing the structure of the problem and deriving two minimum adequacy conditions for any account that avoids this pattern. LLMs make the absence of such a theory consequential rather than merely theoretical.

</details>


### [83] [Agentic Personas for Adaptive Scientific Explanations with Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21846)
*Susana Nunes,Tiago Guerreiro,Catia Pesquita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于智能体角色的强化学习方法，通过模拟专家推理策略生成自适应知识图谱解释，在药物发现任务中实现了最先进的预测性能，大幅降低了对人类反馈的需求（减少两个数量级），且用户更偏好自适应解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI解释方法（包括知识图谱解释）通常假设静态用户模型，无法根据专家目标、推理策略或决策上下文生成自适应解释。在科学发现等复杂领域中，这种假设无法捕捉专家之间多样化的认知策略和认识论立场，阻碍了深入理解和知情决策。同时，人类专家的稀缺性限制了对人类反馈的直接使用。

Method: 提出一种用于科学解释生成的强化学习方法，该方法引入"智能体角色"（agentic personas）——即专家推理策略的结构化表示——引导解释智能体朝向特定的认识论偏好。在药物发现的知识图谱解释任务中，构建并测试了两个基于专家反馈的、反映不同认识论立场的角色。

Result: 在药物发现领域的评估中，角色驱动的解释达到了最先进的预测性能；角色偏好与其对应的专家偏好高度一致；在22名参与者的测试中，自适应解释持续优于非自适应基线；基于角色的训练将反馈需求降低了两个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究表明，智能体角色能够在复杂、高风险领域的AI系统中实现可扩展的自适应可解释性，有效解决了专家稀缺和解释适应性之间的矛盾。

Abstract: AI explanation methods often assume a static user model, producing non-adaptive explanations regardless of expert goals, reasoning strategies, or decision contexts. Knowledge graph-based explanations, despite their capacity for grounded, path-based reasoning, inherit this limitation. In complex domains such as scientific discovery, this assumption fails to capture the diversity of cognitive strategies and epistemic stances among experts, preventing explanations that foster deeper understanding and informed decision-making. However, the scarcity of human experts limits the use of direct human feedback to produce adaptive explanations.
  We present a reinforcement learning approach for scientific explanation generation that incorporates agentic personas, structured representations of expert reasoning strategies, that guide the explanation agent towards specific epistemic preferences. In an evaluation of knowledge graph-based explanations for drug discovery, we tested two personas that capture distinct epistemic stances derived from expert feedback.
  Results show that persona-driven explanations match state-of-the-art predictive performance while persona preferences closely align with those of their corresponding experts. Adaptive explanations were consistently preferred over non-adaptive baselines (n = 22), and persona-based training reduces feedback requirements by two orders of magnitude. These findings demonstrate how agentic personas enable scalable adaptive explainability for AI systems in complex and high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [84] [Reasoning or Rhetoric? An Empirical Analysis of Moral Reasoning Explanations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21854)
*Aryan Kasat,Smriti Singh,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在道德困境中表现出“道德腹语术”：模型通过人类反馈学习掌握了成熟道德推理的修辞技巧，却缺乏真实的人类道德发展轨迹，且存在推理逻辑不一致的严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型面对道德困境时，是展现出真实的道德发展进程（遵循科尔伯格道德发展阶段），还是仅通过人类偏好对齐训练产生表面上类似成熟道德判断的输出。

Method: 使用经过验证的LLM-as-judge评分管道，对13个不同架构、参数规模和训练机制的大语言模型在6个经典道德困境中的600多个回答进行评分与分类，并开展十项补充分析以刻画结果模式的性质与内在一致性。

Result: 发现惊人的“倒置”现象：模型回答压倒性地对应后习俗水平（第5-6阶段），与人类发展常态（第4阶段主导）截然相反。部分模型表现出“道德解耦”，即陈述的道德理由与行动选择之间存在系统性不一致。模型规模影响微弱，训练类型无显著独立主效应，且模型在不同道德困境间表现出近乎机械的逻辑一致性。

Conclusion: 提出“道德腹语术”概念：大语言模型通过对齐训练习得了成熟道德推理的修辞惯例，却缺乏其所代表的潜在发展轨迹，仅是表面上的道德推理。

Abstract: Do large language models reason morally, or do they merely sound like they do? We investigate whether LLM responses to moral dilemmas exhibit genuine developmental progression through Kohlberg's stages of moral development, or whether alignment training instead produces reasoning-like outputs that superficially resemble mature moral judgment without the underlying developmental trajectory. Using an LLM-as-judge scoring pipeline validated across three judge models, we classify more than 600 responses from 13 LLMs spanning a range of architectures, parameter scales, and training regimes across six classical moral dilemmas, and conduct ten complementary analyses to characterize the nature and internal coherence of the resulting patterns. Our results reveal a striking inversion: responses overwhelmingly correspond to post-conventional reasoning (Stages 5-6) regardless of model size, architecture, or prompting strategy, the effective inverse of human developmental norms, where Stage 4 dominates. Most strikingly, a subset of models exhibit moral decoupling: systematic inconsistency between stated moral justification and action choice, a form of logical incoherence that persists across scale and prompting strategy and represents a direct reasoning consistency failure independent of rhetorical sophistication. Model scale carries a statistically significant but practically small effect; training type has no significant independent main effect; and models exhibit near-robotic cross-dilemma consistency producing logically indistinguishable responses across semantically distinct moral problems. We posit that these patterns constitute evidence for moral ventriloquism: the acquisition, through alignment training, of the rhetorical conventions of mature moral reasoning without the underlying developmental trajectory those conventions are meant to represent.

</details>


### [85] [Tacit Knowledge Management with Generative AI: Proposal of the GenAI SECI Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21866)
*Naoshi Uchihira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"GenAI SECI"模型，通过引入"数字碎片化知识"概念，实现了显性与隐性知识的整合管理，为生成式AI在知识管理领域的应用提供了理论框架和系统架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI在知识管理领域的研究主要集中在显性知识管理，尽管对隐性知识管理有零星探索，但缺乏能够整合处理显性和隐性知识的系统化建模。传统知识管理系统存在局限性，需要利用生成式AI的能力来突破这些限制。

Method: 提出"GenAI SECI"模型，这是对知识创造过程（SECI）模型的更新版本，专门设计以发挥生成式AI的能力。核心创新是引入"数字碎片化知识"（Digital Fragmented Knowledge）概念，在网络空间内整合显性和隐性知识。同时提出了具体的系统架构，并与具有相似问题意识和目标的现有研究模型进行对比分析。

Result: 成功构建了能够整合显性和隐性知识的理论模型，提供了"GenAI SECI"模型的完整框架，包括数字碎片化知识概念的定义和具体的系统架构设计。通过与相关研究模型的对比，验证了该模型的独特价值和优势。

Conclusion: "GenAI SECI"模型为生成式AI时代的知识管理提供了新的理论框架和实践指导，数字碎片化知识的引入为解决显性与隐性知识整合这一长期难题提供了创新方案，有望推动知识管理系统在生成式AI支持下实现质的飞跃。

Abstract: The emergence of generative AI is bringing about a significant transformation in knowledge management. Generative AI has the potential to address the limitations of conventional knowledge management systems, and it is increasingly being deployed in real-world settings with promising results. Related research is also expanding rapidly. However, much of this work focuses on research and practice related to the management of explicit knowledge. While fragmentary efforts have been made regarding the management of tacit knowledge using generative AI, the modeling and systematization that handle both tacit and explicit knowledge in an integrated manner remain insufficient. In this paper, we propose the "GenAI SECI" model as an updated version of the knowledge creation process (SECI) model, redesigned to leverage the capabilities of generative AI. A defining feature of the "GenAI SECI" model is the introduction of "Digital Fragmented Knowledge", a new concept that integrates explicit and tacit knowledge within cyberspace. Furthermore, a concrete system architecture for the proposed model is presented, along with a comparison with prior research models that share a similar problem awareness and objectives.

</details>


### [86] [Guideline-grounded retrieval-augmented generation for ophthalmic clinical decision support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21925)
*Shuying Chen,Sen Cui,Zhong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Oph-Guid-RAG，一个面向眼科临床问答与决策支持的多模态视觉RAG系统，通过直接检索指南页面图像保留表格与流程图等信息，在HealthBench困难子集上相比GPT-5.2整体得分提升30.0%，准确率提升10.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼科临床问答需要基于权威指南证据进行精确推理，但现有文本检索方法会丢失表格、流程图和版面信息；同时检索引入的噪声会影响临床决策的可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 将每页指南作为独立证据单元，直接检索页面图像以保留多模态信息；设计可控检索框架，包含路由与过滤机制以选择性引入外部证据并降低噪声；集成查询分解、查询重写、检索、重排序与多模态推理，输出可追溯到指南页面的结果。

Result: 在HealthBench困难子集上，相比GPT-5.2整体得分从0.2969提升至0.3861（+30.0%），准确率从0.5956提升至0.6576（+10.4%）；相比GPT-5.4准确率提升24.4%；消融实验表明重排序、路由与检索设计对困难场景下的稳定性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 将视觉检索与可控推理结合可有效提升临床AI的证据依据性与鲁棒性，尤其在需要精确证据推理的困难案例上效果显著，但仍有进一步完善的空间。

Abstract: In this work, we propose Oph-Guid-RAG, a multimodal visual RAG system for ophthalmology clinical question answering and decision support. We treat each guideline page as an independent evidence unit and directly retrieve page images, preserving tables, flowcharts, and layout information. We further design a controllable retrieval framework with routing and filtering, which selectively introduces external evidence and reduces noise. The system integrates query decomposition, query rewriting, retrieval, reranking, and multimodal reasoning, and provides traceable outputs with guideline page references. We evaluate our method on HealthBench using a doctor-based scoring protocol. On the hard subset, our approach improves the overall score from 0.2969 to 0.3861 (+0.0892, +30.0%) compared to GPT-5.2, and achieves higher accuracy, improving from 0.5956 to 0.6576 (+0.0620, +10.4%). Compared to GPT-5.4, our method achieves a larger accuracy gain of +0.1289 (+24.4%). These results show that our method is more effective on challenging cases that require precise, evidence-based reasoning. Ablation studies further show that reranking, routing, and retrieval design are critical for stable performance, especially under difficult settings. Overall, we show how combining visionbased retrieval with controllable reasoning can improve evidence grounding and robustness in clinical AI applications,while pointing out that further work is needed to be more complete.

</details>


### [87] [Future-Interactions-Aware Trajectory Prediction via Braid Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22035)
*Caio Azevedo,Stefano Sabatini,Sascha Hornauer,Fabien Moutarde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于辫理论的辅助任务（辫预测），通过预测智能体间的交叉类型来增强多智能体轨迹预测的社会感知能力，在三个数据集上显著提升了联合预测性能，且几乎不增加计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要预测周围大量交互智能体的未来行为。现有方法在建模社会交互时存在计算量大或依赖启发式标签的问题。辫理论能提供多智能体行为的精确描述，但过去仅被轻量使用。本文旨在充分利用辫表示的表达能力来改善联合轨迹预测。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的辅助任务——辫预测（braid prediction），与轨迹预测任务并行进行。该方法通过将智能体间的边分类为辫表示中的正确交叉类型，使模型获得更好的社会感知能力。该辅助任务在训练和推理时几乎不增加复杂度。

Result: 在三个独立数据集上，联合预测指标获得显著提升。辫预测任务使模型具备未来意图感知能力，从而产生更准确的联合预测，预测结果更贴近实际的多智能体行为。

Conclusion: 辫预测作为简单有效的辅助任务，能够将辫表示的表达能力充分融入轨迹预测模型，显著提升多智能体联合预测性能，证明了辫理论在轨迹预测任务中的重要价值。

Abstract: To safely operate, an autonomous vehicle must know the future behavior of a potentially high number of interacting agents around it, a task often posed as multi-agent trajectory prediction. Many previous attempts to model social interactions and solve the joint prediction task either add extensive computational requirements or rely on heuristics to label multi-agent behavior types. Braid theory, in contrast, provides a powerful exact descriptor of multi-agent behavior by projecting future trajectories into braids that express how trajectories cross with each other over time; a braid then corresponds to a specific mode of coordination between the multiple agents in the future. In past work, braids have been used lightly to reason about interacting agents and restrict the attention window of predicted agents. We show that leveraging more fully the expressivity of the braid representation and using it to condition the trajectories themselves leads to even further gains in joint prediction performance, with negligible added complexity either in training or at inference time. We do so by proposing a novel auxiliary task, braid prediction, done in parallel with the trajectory prediction task. By classifying edges between agents into their correct crossing types in the braid representation, the braid prediction task is able to imbue the model with improved social awareness, which is reflected in joint predictions that more closely adhere to the actual multi-agent behavior. This simple auxiliary task allowed us to obtain significant improvements in joint metrics on three separate datasets. We show how the braid prediction task infuses the model with future intention awareness, leading to more accurate joint predictions. Code is available at github.com/caiocj1/traj-pred-braid-theory.

</details>


### [88] [A Context Engineering Framework for Improving Enterprise AI Agents based on Digital-Twin MDP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22083)
*Xi Yang,Aurelie Lozano,Naoki Abe,Bhavya,Saurabh Jha,Noah Zheutlin,Rohan R. Arora,Yu Deng,Daby M. Sow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个轻量级、模型无关的离线强化学习框架DT-MDP-CE，通过构建数字孪生MDP、鲁棒对比逆强化学习和RL引导的上下文工程三个核心组件，有效提升了企业级LLM智能体的决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业自动化AI智能体在实际部署中面临数据质量与数量有限、复杂现实推理需求高、自我博弈困难以及缺乏可靠反馈信号等关键挑战，严重制约了其性能提升和实际应用。

Method: 提出DT-MDP-CE框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)数字孪生马尔可夫决策过程(DT-MDP)，将智能体推理行为抽象为有限MDP；(2)鲁棒对比逆强化学习，基于DT-MDP高效估计奖励函数并从混合质量离线轨迹中诱导策略；(3)RL引导的上下文工程，利用集成策略改进智能体决策行为。

Result: 在IT自动化领域代表性任务上进行案例研究，实验结果表明该框架在广泛评估设置下相比基线智能体获得一致且显著的性能提升，证明其具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效解决企业级LLM智能体的核心挑战，在IT自动化任务中表现优异，并具备推广至其他具有相似特征的企业环境智能体的潜力。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in AI agents for enterprise automation and decision-making, their real-world deployment and further performance gains remain constrained by limited data quality and quantity, complex real-world reasoning demands, difficulties with self-play, and the lack of reliable feedback signals. To address these challenges, we propose a lightweight, model-agnostic framework for improving LLM-based enterprise agents via offline reinforcement learning (RL). The proposed Context Engineering via DT-MDP (DT-MDP-CE) framework comprises three key components: (1) A Digital-Twin Markov Decision Process (DT-MDP), which abstracts the agent's reasoning behavior as a finite MDP; (2) A robust contrastive inverse RL, which, armed with the DT-MDP, to efficiently estimate a well-founded reward function and induces policies from mixed-quality offline trajectories; and (3) RL-guided context engineering, which uses the policy obtained from the integrated process of (1) and (2), to improve the agent's decision-making behavior. As a case study, we apply the framework to a representative task in the enterprise-oriented domain of IT automation. Extensive experimental results demonstrate consistent and significant improvements over baseline agents across a wide range of evaluation settings, suggesting that the framework can generalize to other agents sharing similar characteristics in enterprise environments.

</details>


### [89] [GSEM: Graph-based Self-Evolving Memory for Experience Augmented Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22096)
*Xiao Han,Yuzheng Fan,Sendong Zhao,Haochun Wang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GSEM框架，通过双层记忆图组织临床经验，解决了传统记忆增强方法因缺乏关系结构导致的检索噪声问题，在MedR-Bench和MedAgentsBench基准上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强方法将经验存储为独立记录，缺乏显式关系结构，导致检索噪声、不可靠的复用，甚至可能损害模型性能。

Method: 提出GSEM（基于图的自我进化记忆）框架，将临床经验组织为双层记忆图，同时捕获单个经验内部的决策结构和跨经验的关系依赖，并支持应用感知检索和基于在线反馈的节点质量与边权重的校准。

Result: 在MedR-Bench和MedAgentsBench两个基准上，使用DeepSeek-V3.2和Qwen3.5-35B两种LLM骨干网络，GSEM在所有基线方法中取得了最高平均准确率，分别达到70.90%和69.24%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模经验内部决策结构和跨经验关系依赖，GSEM有效提升了临床决策智能体的性能，验证了结构化记忆在临床场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making agents can benefit from reusing prior decision experience. However, many memory-augmented methods store experiences as independent records without explicit relational structure, which may introduce noisy retrieval, unreliable reuse, and in some cases even hurt performance compared to direct LLM inference. We propose GSEM (Graph-based Self-Evolving Memory), a clinical memory framework that organizes clinical experiences into a dual-layer memory graph, capturing both the decision structure within each experience and the relational dependencies across experiences, and supporting applicability-aware retrieval and online feedback-driven calibration of node quality and edge weights. Across MedR-Bench and MedAgentsBench with two LLM backbones, GSEM achieves the highest average accuracy among all baselines, reaching 70.90\% and 69.24\% with DeepSeek-V3.2 and Qwen3.5-35B, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/xhan1022/gsem.

</details>


### [90] [SpecTM: Spectral Targeted Masking for Trustworthy Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22097)
*Syed Usama Imtiaz,Mitra Nasr Azadani,Nasrin Alamdari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpecTM（光谱目标掩码），一种基于物理信息引导的掩码设计方法。通过多任务自监督学习框架，在Lake Erie的NASA PACE高光谱图像上预测微囊藻毒素浓度，当前周R²达到0.695，8天预测R²为0.620，分别超越基线模型34%和99%，并在标签稀缺条件下实现了2.2倍的标签效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地球观测基础模型通常依赖随机掩码策略，未能显式引入物理约束，这对于指导公共卫生决策的预测模型而言是一个关键的可信度限制。因此需要开发能够融合物理先验知识的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出SpecTM（光谱目标掩码）方法，通过跨光谱上下文重建目标波段来引入物理约束；开发了自适应多任务自监督学习框架，联合优化波段重建、生物光学指数推断和8天时序预测三个任务，从而编码光谱内在表征。

Result: 在Lake Erie的NASA PACE高光谱图像微囊藻毒素浓度回归任务中，当前周预测R²=0.695，8天预测R²=0.620，分别超越Ridge基线34%和SVR基线99%；目标掩码比随机掩码提升+0.037 R²；在极端标签稀缺条件下，标签效率比强基线高2.2倍。

Conclusion: SpecTM成功实现了跨地球观测领域的物理信息引导表示学习，显著提升了基础模型的可解释性和预测性能，为需要物理一致性的地球观测应用提供了新的建模范式。

Abstract: Foundation models are now increasingly being developed for Earth observation (EO), yet they often rely on stochastic masking that do not explicitly enforce physics constraints; a critical trustworthiness limitation, in particular for predictive models that guide public health decisions. In this work, we propose SpecTM (Spectral Targeted Masking), a physics-informed masking design that encourages the reconstruction of targeted bands from cross-spectral context during pretraining. To achieve this, we developed an adaptable multi-task (band reconstruction, bio-optical index inference, and 8-day-ahead temporal prediction) self-supervised learning (SSL) framework that encodes spectrally intrinsic representations via joint optimization, and evaluated it on a downstream microcystin concentration regression model using NASA PACE hyperspectral imagery over Lake Erie. SpecTM achieves R^2 = 0.695 (current week) and R^2 = 0.620 (8-day-ahead) predictions surpassing all baseline models by (+34% (0.51 Ridge) and +99% (SVR 0.31)) respectively. Our ablation experiments show targeted masking improves predictions by +0.037 R^2 over random masking. Furthermore, it outperforms strong baselines with 2.2x superior label efficiency under extreme scarcity. SpecTM enables physics-informed representation learning across EO domains and improves the interpretability of foundation models.

</details>


### [91] [MARCUS: An agentic, multimodal vision-language model for cardiac diagnosis and management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22179)
*Jack W O'Sullivan,Mohammad Asadi,Lennart Elbe,Akshay Chaudhari,Tahoura Nedaee,Francois Haddad,Michael Salerno,Li Fe-Fei,Ehsan Adeli,Rima Arnaout,Euan A Ashley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MARCUS，一个用于ECG、超声心动图和心脏磁共振影像端到端解释的多模态智能体视觉-语言系统，在单模态和多模态任务上均大幅超越GPT-5、Gemini 2.5等前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球首要死因，而复杂心脏检查的解释依赖于人工，限制了诊疗进展。现有的AI视觉-语言模型仅支持单模态输入且缺乏交互能力，无法满足临床多模态心脏检查的综合解读需求。

Method: 采用分层智能体架构：包含针对ECG、超声心动图和CMR的特定模态视觉-语言专家模型（结合领域训练的视觉编码器与多阶段语言模型优化），由多模态编排器协调工作。基于1350万张图像和160万个专家标注问答数据集进行训练。

Result: 在斯坦福内部和UCSF外部测试队列中，单模态准确率达ECG 87-91%、超声心动图67-86%、CMR 85-88%，超越前沿模型34-45%（P<0.001）；多模态病例准确率达70%，接近前沿模型（22-28%）的三倍；自由文本质量评分提升1.7-3.0倍；并具备抗幻觉推理能力。

Conclusion: 领域专用视觉编码器结合智能体编排架构可实现高效的多模态心脏解读，证明了垂直领域多模态AI系统的临床应用潜力。模型、代码和基准测试已开源。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease remains the leading cause of global mortality, with progress hindered by human interpretation of complex cardiac tests. Current AI vision-language models are limited to single-modality inputs and are non-interactive. We present MARCUS (Multimodal Autonomous Reasoning and Chat for Ultrasound and Signals), an agentic vision-language system for end-to-end interpretation of electrocardiograms (ECGs), echocardiograms, and cardiac magnetic resonance imaging (CMR) independently and as multimodal input. MARCUS employs a hierarchical agentic architecture comprising modality-specific vision-language expert models, each integrating domain-trained visual encoders with multi-stage language model optimization, coordinated by a multimodal orchestrator. Trained on 13.5 million images (0.25M ECGs, 1.3M echocardiogram images, 12M CMR images) and our novel expert-curated dataset spanning 1.6 million questions, MARCUS achieves state-of-the-art performance surpassing frontier models (GPT-5 Thinking, Gemini 2.5 Pro Deep Think). Across internal (Stanford) and external (UCSF) test cohorts, MARCUS achieves accuracies of 87-91% for ECG, 67-86% for echocardiography, and 85-88% for CMR, outperforming frontier models by 34-45% (P<0.001). On multimodal cases, MARCUS achieved 70% accuracy, nearly triple that of frontier models (22-28%), with 1.7-3.0x higher free-text quality scores. Our agentic architecture also confers resistance to mirage reasoning, whereby vision-language models derive reasoning from unintended textual signals or hallucinated visual content. MARCUS demonstrates that domain-specific visual encoders with an agentic orchestrator enable multimodal cardiac interpretation. We release our models, code, and benchmark open-source.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [DGNNFlow: A Streaming Dataflow Architecture for Real-Time Edge-based Dynamic GNN Inference in HL-LHC Trigger Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20364)
*Davendra Maharaj,Tu Pham,Peter Meiring,Kyungmin Park,Sena Durgut,Cong Hao,Matteo Cremonesi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DGNNFlow，一种面向高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）触发系统的基于边缘的动态图神经网络（GNN）推理数据流架构，在FPGA上实现了显著的延迟降低和能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）的高能物理实验中，未来探测器产生的碰撞数据量将增加10倍。由于离线计算和存储的限制，触发系统需要在FPGA上运行超低延迟的机器学习模型以低功耗进行实时在线过滤。现有的GNN加速器依赖静态图结构，无法满足HL-LHC触发系统中边嵌入基于邻居节点嵌入实时变化的动态GNN需求。

Method: 提出DGNNFlow数据流架构，包含三个核心贡献：1）引入硬件支持实现边嵌入的动态计算；2）解决基于边缘的动态GNN数据流中的数据依赖问题，边嵌入由其源节点和目标节点共同构建；3）执行输入动态图构建辅助设置，以完整支持无预定义边嵌入的模型。在AMD Alveo U50 FPGA上以200MHz频率部署实现。

Result: DGNNFlow在批处理大小为1到4时，相比NVIDIA RTX A6000 GPU实现了1.6x-6.3x加速，功耗仅为GPU的0.22倍；相比Intel Xeon Gold 6226R CPU实现了3.2x-5.1x加速，功耗仅为CPU的0.25倍。完整实现已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: DGNNFlow成功解决了HL-LHC触发系统中动态GNN推理的硬件加速挑战，通过创新的边嵌入动态计算和数据流设计，在FPGA上实现了比GPU和CPU更高的性能和能效，为未来高数据量的高能物理实验提供了有效的实时事件过滤解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic GNN inference has exhibited effectiveness in High Energy Physics (HEP) experiments at High Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC) due to strong capability to model complex particle interactions in collision events. Future HEP experiments will involve detectors that produce 10x more collision data to help unlocking physics discoveries. Due to limitations in offline compute capacity and storage, revamped trigger systems require FPGAs to run ultra-low-latency Machine Learning models for online filtering of useful events with low power consumption. State-of-the-art GNN accelerators relied on static graph structures, but this assumption breaks down in real-time HL-LHC trigger systems and edge-based dynamic GNN models where edge embeddings change in-place based on neighbor node embeddings at runtime. We propose DGNNFlow, a novel dataflow architecture for real-time edge-based dynamic GNN inference applications, especially HL-LHC trigger systems, with three key contributions. First, we introduce hardware support for dynamic computation of edge embeddings. Second, we resolve data dependencies in edge-based dynamic GNN dataflow, where edge embedding is formulated using its source and target nodes. Third, we perform input dynamic graph construction auxiliary setup for complete support of models without pre-defined edge embeddings. We deployed DGNNFlow using AMD Alveo U50 FPGA to evaluate end-to-end latency on-board at 200 MHz clock frequency. DGNNFlow achieved 1.6x-6.3x speedup and 0.22x power consumption compared to GPU (NVIDIA RTX A6000) with batch sizes from 1 to 4, 3.2x-5.1x speedup and 0.25x power consumption compared to CPU (Intel Xeon Gold 6226R). Our complete implementation is publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [93] [SkyHOST: A Unified Architecture for Cross-Cloud Hybrid Object and Stream Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20512)
*Muhammad Arslan Tariq,Grégoire Danoy,Pascal Bouvry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SkyHOST是一种统一数据传输架构，可同时支持批量对象传输和流式传输工作负载，通过单一控制平面实现操作简便性并保持竞争性吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据传输工具通常专门针对单一范式（流复制或批量传输）进行设计，迫使用户为每种传输模式部署和管理具有不同配置的独立系统，增加了运营复杂性。随着云和大数据工作负载在多个云提供商和区域间分布数据的增加，亟需一种能够同时处理批量对象传输和流式传输的统一解决方案。

Method: 基于Skyplane框架构建SkyHOST（混合对象和流传输）统一数据移动架构，通过单一控制平面和CLI弥合批量对象传输和流式工作负载之间的差距。采用基于URI的路由机制自动选择适当的传输方式，同时支持用于记录级摄取的结构化数据传输和用于大型二进制对象的基于块的传输。

Result: 通过环境监测用例和实证评估验证，SkyHOST通过将异构数据移动模式整合到单一控制平面下实现了操作简便性，同时在跨区域传输中实现了具有竞争性的吞吐量表现。

Conclusion: SkyHOST成功解决了传统传输工具的专一性问题，提供了一个统一的解决方案来处理批量对象传输和流式传输两种不同的数据移动模式，在保证传输性能的同时显著简化了系统部署和管理。

Abstract: Cloud and big data workloads are increasingly distributing data across multiple cloud providers and regions for rapid decision-making and analytics. Traditional transfer tools are typically specialized for a single paradigm, either stream replication or bulk transfer. This specialization forces users to deploy and manage separate systems with different configurations for each transfer pattern. This paper presents SkyHOST (Hybrid Object and Stream Transfer), a unified data movement architecture built upon the Skyplane framework to bridge the gap between bulk object transfer and streaming workloads through a single control plane and CLI. SkyHOST manages URI-based routing to automatically select the appropriate transfer mechanism, supporting both structured data for record-level ingestion and chunk-based transfer for large binary objects. We demonstrate, through an environmental monitoring use case and empirical evaluation, that SkyHOST provides operational simplicity by consolidating heterogeneous data movement patterns under a single control plane while achieving competitive throughput for cross-region transfers.

</details>


### [94] [Epistemic Observability in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20531)
*Tony Mason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 大语言模型在编造事实时反而报告最高置信度；在纯文本观察下，无法可靠区分诚实输出与编造输出；通过输出token熵值可有效检测编造行为（AUC 0.757）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现模型在编造时置信度最高（AUC 0.28-0.36），这种反常现象促使研究者探究其根本原因，并寻求可靠的编造检测方法。

Method: 1）在形式化假设下证明纯文本观察无法区分诚实与编造输出的不可能定理；2）构建张量接口输出计算副产品（token熵和对数概率分布）；3）构建验证预算与检测准确率的成本曲面图。

Result: token熵检测方法达到AUC 0.757，在各预算水平上超越文本基线2.5-3.9个百分点，跨架构泛化性良好（Spearman ρ=0.762）。

Conclusion: 模型编造行为的不可检测性是观测局限性而非能力差距；通过输出计算副产品可突破此限制，为系统设计者提供实用的验证资源配置决策工具。

Abstract: We find that models report highest confidence precisely when they are fabricating. Across four model families (OLMo-3, Llama-3.1, Qwen3, Mistral), self-reported confidence inversely correlates with accuracy, with AUC ranging from 0.28 to 0.36 where 0.5 is random guessing.
  We prove, under explicit formal assumptions, that this is not a capability gap but an observational one. Under text-only observation, where a supervisor sees only the model's output text, no monitoring system can reliably distinguish honest model outputs from plausible fabrications. We prove two results: first, that any policy conditioning only on the query cannot satisfy epistemic honesty across ambiguous world states; second, that no learning algorithm optimizing reward from a text-only supervisor can converge to honest behavior when the supervisor's observations are identical for both grounded and fabricated responses. Within our formal model, these impossibilities hold regardless of model scale or training procedure, including RLHF and instruction tuning.
  We construct a tensor interface that escapes the impossibility by exporting computational byproducts (per-token entropy and log-probability distributions) that are structurally coupled to correctness under standard training. Per-token entropy achieves pooled AUC 0.757, outperforming all text baselines by 2.5--3.9 percentage points at every budget level tested (10\%, 20\%, 30\%). The entropy signal generalizes across architectures (Spearman $ρ= 0.762$).
  The core contribution is a cost surface where the empirical mapping from verification budget (fraction of queries receiving expensive checks) to detection accuracy for each judge strategy is a practical lookup for system builders deciding how to allocate verification resources. The contribution is the map. The territory is the system you are building.

</details>


### [95] [Incremental GNN Embedding Computation on Streaming Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20622)
*Qiange Wang,Haoran Lv,Yanfeng Zhang,Weng-Fai Wong,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对流式图GNN推理的高效增量计算框架，通过解耦和重排序算子将计算限制在受影响的子图上，并利用GPU-CPU协同处理大规模历史数据，实现了最高145.8倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式图上的图神经网络（GNN）在实际部署中面临巨大挑战。其推理过程依赖运行时嵌入计算（RTEC）来捕获图的最新变化，该过程会产生沉重的多跳图遍历开销，严重损害计算效率。然而，作者观察到在图演化过程中，大部分节点的中间结果保持不变，存在大量冗余计算，这为增量优化提供了动机。

Method: 作者提出了一种增量化的RTEC框架。核心方法是将GNN计算解耦为一组广义的、细粒度的算子，并安全地对它们进行重排序，从而将昂贵的全邻居GNN计算转换为在受影响子图上的高效计算形式。此外，为了扩展到具有海量历史结果的图，开发了一个GPU-CPU协同处理系统，通过通信优化的调度策略将嵌入卸载到CPU内存中。

Result: 该框架在保留原始全邻居计算的语义和准确性的同时，支持多种具有复杂消息传递模式的GNN模型。实验表明，该方法减少了64%-99%的计算量，相比现有解决方案实现了1.7倍至145.8倍的加速。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度的算子解耦与重排序以及GPU-CPU协同处理机制，本文提出的增量计算框架有效解决了流式图GNN推理中的计算冗余问题，大幅提升了计算效率且具有良好的模型通用性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Network (GNN) on streaming graphs has gained increasing popularity. However, its practical deployment remains challenging, as the inference process relies on Runtime Embedding Computation (RTEC) to capture recent graph changes. This process incurs heavyweight multi-hop graph traversal overhead, which significantly undermines computation efficiency. We observe that the intermediate results for large portions of the graph remain unchanged during graph evolution, and thus redundant computations can be effectively eliminated through carefully designed incremental methods. In this work, we propose an efficient framework for incrementalizing RTEC on streaming graphs.The key idea is to decouple GNN computation into a set of generalized, fine-grained operators and safely reorder them, transforming the expensive full-neighbor GNN computation into a more efficient form over the affected subgraph. With this design, our framework preserves the semantics and accuracy of the original full-neighbor computation while supporting a wide range of GNN models with complex message-passing patterns. To further scale to graphs with massive historical results, we develop a GPU-CPU co-processing system that offloads embeddings to CPU memory with communication-optimized scheduling. Experiments across diverse graph sizes and GNN models show that our method reduces computation by 64%-99% and achieves 1.7x-145.8x speedups over existing solutions.

</details>


### [96] [Modernizing Amdahl's Law: How AI Scaling Laws Shape Computer Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20654)
*Chien-Ping Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对现代异构系统重新表述了阿姆达尔定律，揭示了资源分配存在有限坍缩阈值：当可扩展任务比例超过临界值时，无论专用硬件效率优势如何，专用化投资的最优值都将归零，呈现相变特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典阿姆达尔定律基于串行与并行任务的固定分解及同构复制假设，已无法准确描述结合专用加速器、可编程计算单元、张量数据路径的现代异构系统。核心矛盾已转变为在效率差异和负载结构约束下，如何将额外计算资源有效转化为价值。

Method: 针对具有可扩展工作负载的现代异构系统，重新构建并推导了阿姆达尔定律的理论框架，将资源分配问题建模为在异构硬件间的优化问题，分析专用硬件效率优势与可扩展任务比例之间的相互作用关系。

Result: 分析发现存在一个有限的坍缩阈值，表现为相变现象而非渐近尾部：当可扩展任务比例超过某一临界值时，无论专用硬件相对于可编程计算具有何种效率优势，专用化策略都会变得次优，最优专用投资降至零。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解释了GPU可编程性不断增强以及领域专用AI加速器未能取代GPU的现象：专用加速器的适用范围存在根本性边界，可编程性在现代多样化负载场景中具有不可替代的价值。

Abstract: Classical Amdahl's Law assumes a fixed decomposition between serial and parallel work and homogeneous replication; historically, it bounds how much parallel speedup is attainable. Modern systems instead combine specialized accelerators with programmable compute, tensor datapaths, and evolving pipelines, while empirical scaling laws shift which stages absorb marginal compute. The central tension is therefore not the serial-versus-parallel split alone, but resource allocation across heterogeneous hardware, given efficiency differences, and workload structures that determine how effectively additional compute can be converted into value. We reformulate Amdahl's Law for modern heterogeneous systems with scalable workloads. The analysis yields a finite collapse threshold: beyond a critical scalable fraction, specialization becomes suboptimal for any efficiency advantage of specialized hardware over programmable compute, and optimal specialized investment falls to zero, a phase transition rather than an asymptotic tail. We use this framework to interpret increasing GPU programmability and why domain-specific AI accelerators have not displaced GPUs.

</details>


### [97] [WWW.Serve: Interconnecting Global LLM Services through Decentralization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20661)
*Huanyu Wang,Ziyu Xia,Zhuoming Chen,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了WWW.Serve，一个去中心化的大语言模型服务框架，通过允许参与者灵活制定参与策略和支持自组织请求调度，在保留去中心化优势的同时，将SLO达成率提升高达1.5倍，延迟降低27.6%，性能接近甚至超越中心化调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型服务大多采用中心化架构，导致可扩展性瓶颈和大量分散GPU资源利用率不足。而现有的去中心化框架主要关注GPU提供商之间的合作，忽略了其固有的竞争动态，并施加了过多限制（如过度平台监管或要求使用固定软硬件配置执行请求），这些假设在现实去中心化环境中并不现实。

Method: 提出WWW.Serve去中心化框架，包含两个核心设计：1）允许参与者灵活决定参与策略和资源承诺；2）支持自组织请求分发机制，使网络能够在没有中心化协调的情况下自主分配请求。

Result: 实验表明，WWW.Serve将全局SLO（服务级别目标）达成率提高了1.5倍，降低了27.6%的延迟，性能接近甚至在某些情况下超越了中心化调度方案，同时完全保留了去中心化的优势。

Conclusion: WWW.Serve为现实世界中去中心化大语言模型服务提供了一个有前景的基础框架，证明了在去中心化环境下实现高效LLM服务的可行性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) services are mostly centralized, leading to scalability bottlenecks and underutilization of substantial scattered GPU resources. While decentralization offers a promising alternative, existing frameworks primarily focus on cooperation among GPU providers while overlooking their inherent competitive dynamics, imposing substantial constraints such as excessive platform-level oversight or rigid requirements to execute all assigned requests using fixed software stacks on fixed hardware configurations. We argue that such assumptions are unrealistic in real-world decentralized environments. To this end, we propose WWW.Serve, a decentralized framework for interconnecting LLM services worldwide. It allows participants to flexibly determine their participation policies and resource commitments, and supports self-organizing request dispatch, enabling the network to autonomously allocate requests without centralized coordination. Empirically, we show that WWW.Serve improves global SLO (service-level-objective) attainment by up to 1.5x and lowers latency by 27.6%. Its performance approaches, and in some cases surpasses, centralized scheduling, while fully preserving the benefits of decentralization. These results highlight WWW.Serve as a promising foundation for real-world, decentralized LLM serving.

</details>


### [98] [RoboECC: Multi-Factor-Aware Edge-Cloud Collaborative Deployment for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20711)
*Zihao Zheng,Hangyu Cao,Jiayu Chen,Sicheng Tian,Chenyue Li,Maoliang Li,Xinhao Sun,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboECC框架，通过模型-硬件协同感知分割策略和网络感知部署调整方法，解决视觉-语言-动作模型在边云协同部署中的分割点识别和网络波动问题，实现最高3.28倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型是具身智能的主流方法，但面临高推理成本问题。虽然边云协同(ECC)部署可以缓解边缘设备计算压力以满足实时需求，但现有ECC框架对VLA模型效果不佳，主要面临两个挑战：(1)多样的模型结构阻碍了最优ECC分割点的识别；(2)即使确定了最优分割点，网络带宽变化也会导致性能漂移。

Method: 提出RoboECC框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)模型-硬件协同感知分割策略，帮助为各种VLA模型找到最优分割点；(2)网络感知部署调整方法，适应网络波动以维持最优性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，RoboECC实现了高达3.28倍的加速，仅有2.55x~2.62x的开销。

Conclusion: RoboECC框架有效解决了VLA模型在边云协同部署中的关键挑战，在保证性能的同时显著提升了推理速度，为具身智能场景下的模型部署提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are mainstream in embodied intelligence but face high inference costs. Edge-Cloud Collaborative (ECC) deployment offers an effective fix by easing edge-device computing pressure to meet real-time needs. However, existing ECC frameworks are suboptimal for VLA models due to two challenges: (1) Diverse model structures hinder optimal ECC segmentation point identification; (2) Even if the optimal split point is determined, changes in network bandwidth can cause performance drift. To address these issues, we propose a novel ECC deployment framework for various VLA models, termed RoboECC. Specifically, we propose a model-hardware co-aware segmentation strategy to help find the optimal segmentation point for various VLA models. Moreover, we propose a network-aware deployment adjustment approach to adapt to the network fluctuations for maintaining optimal performance. Experiments demonstrate that RoboECC achieves a speedup of up to 3.28x with only 2.55x~2.62x overhead.

</details>


### [99] [Compass: Optimizing Compound AI Workflows for Dynamic Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20821)
*Milos Gravara,Juan Luis Herrera,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Compass框架，通过离线优化和在线自适应实现复合AI系统的动态配置切换，在固定基础设施下平衡准确率、延迟和成本，显著提升SLO合规率和资源利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的复合AI系统部署优化方法仅关注准确率，忽略了工作负载条件的变化；在无法水平扩展的固定基础设施上，需要在变化负载下同时满足准确率、延迟和成本目标。

Method: Compass框架包含三个组件：1) COMPASS-V算法，使用有限差分引导搜索和爬山与横向扩展相结合的方法发现帕累托最优配置；2) Planner，在目标硬件上分析配置并基于排队理论模型推导切换策略；3) Elastico控制器，监控队列深度并根据阈值进行运行时配置切换。

Result: 在两个复合AI工作流上，COMPASS-V实现了100%召回率，相比穷举搜索平均减少57.5%的配置评估，在严格的准确率阈值下效率提升达95.3%。运行时自适应在动态负载模式下实现90-98%的SLO合规率，相比静态高准确率基线提升71.6%，同时相比静态快速基线提升3-5%的准确率。

Conclusion: Compass成功实现了复合AI系统的动态配置切换，在固定基础设施约束下有效平衡了准确率与延迟的权衡，为生产部署提供了实用的优化解决方案。

Abstract: Compound AI is a distributed intelligence approach that represents a unified system orchestrating specialized AI/ML models with engineered software components into AI workflows. Compound AI production deployments must satisfy accuracy, latency, and cost objectives under varying loads. However, many deployments operate on fixed infrastructure where horizontal scaling is not viable. Existing approaches optimize solely for accuracy and do not consider changes in workload conditions. We observe that compound AI systems can switch between configurations to fit infrastructure capacity, trading accuracy for latency based on current load. This requires discovering multiple Pareto-optimal configurations from a combinatorial search space and determining when to switch between them at runtime. We present Compass, a novel framework that enables dynamic configuration switching through offline optimization and online adaptation. Compass consists of three components: COMPASS-V algorithm for configuration discovery, Planner for switching policy derivation, and Elastico Controller for runtime adaptation. COMPASS-V discovers accuracy-feasible configurations using finite-difference guided search and a combination of hill-climbing and lateral expansion. Planner profiles these configurations on target hardware and derives switching policies using a queuing theory based model. Elastico monitors queue depth and switches configurations based on derived thresholds. Across two compound AI workflows, COMPASS-V achieves 100% recall while reducing configuration evaluations by 57.5% on average compared to exhaustive search, with efficiency gains reaching 95.3% at tight accuracy thresholds. Runtime adaptation achieves 90-98% SLO compliance under dynamic load patterns, improving SLO compliance by 71.6% over static high-accuracy baselines, while simultaneously improving accuracy by 3-5% over static fast baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Error-resilient Distributed Local Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20831)
*Paweł Garncarek,Tomasz Jurdzinski,Dariusz Kowalski,Subhajit Pramanick*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在局部可检标记框架下研究了分布式网络图属性的验证问题，提出了仅需2个标签的环路检测方案，并开发了可容忍对抗性标签错误的通用“refix”算法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分布式网络中图属性的验证问题，重点解决两个挑战：一是降低环路检测所需的标签数量，二是应对节点标签可能遭受对抗性错误（篡改）的场景，提升验证算法的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了两种技术：1) 一种新型装置（gadget），仅使用2个标签和3跳邻域即可编码路径方向，从而实现高效的环路检测；2) 一个名为“refix”的通用算法框架，可将基于d跳邻域的基础验证算法转化为能容忍d+2i跳邻域内最多i个错误标签的鲁棒版本。

Result: 实现了仅需2个标签的环路检测标记方案（此前需要3个）；证明了refix框架可将任意属性验证算法转化为容错版本，并成功应用于环路检测、环路缺失检测和二分图检测；给出了错误数量与所需邻域大小之间的下界关系。

Conclusion: 通过引入新型编码装置和refix框架，显著降低了环路检测的标签复杂度，并为分布式验证算法提供了通用的容错转换机制，增强了算法在对抗环境下的适用性。

Abstract: We study verification (decision) problems for graph properties in distributed networks under the locally checkable labeling framework, where nodes use labels (proofs) and local neighborhoods to decide acceptance or rejection.
  Our focus is twofold. First, we study cycle detection. While it is known that this can be verified using 3 labels with access to the 1-hop neighborhood, we introduce a novel gadget that encodes direction along a path using only 2 labels and access to a 3-hop neighborhood. This yields a cycle-detection labeling scheme with just 2 labels and may be of independent interest.
  Second, we consider adversarially corrupted labelings, where each node has access to a local neighborhood within which a fraction of nodes may receive erroneous labels. We introduce a general algorithmic framework, called refix, that transforms a base verification algorithm for a property P operating on labels within a d-hop neighborhood into one that tolerates up to i erroneous labels within a radius d+2i, by accessing a d+2i-hop neighborhood. We demonstrate applications to cycle detection, cycle absence, and bipartiteness, and provide lower bounds relating the number of errors to the required neighborhood size.

</details>


### [101] [Adviser: An Intuitive Multi-Cloud Platform for Scientific and ML Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20941)
*Shihan Cheng,Michael A. Laurenzano,Brian Strauch,Timothy A. Ellis,Krish Wadhwani,David A. B. Hyde*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Adviser平台，通过工作流抽象封装专业知识，使科研人员无需云或HPC专业知识即可在多云环境中高效运行科学和ML应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效利用现代云环境的海量计算资源需要跨越多个技术领域的专业知识（软件配置、实例类型选择、并行执行管理等）。研究显示，缺乏这些专业知识是阻碍云计算在科学计算领域发挥潜力的持续挑战。

Method: 引入Adviser直观多云平台，以工作流抽象为核心。工作流是可复用的、专家构建的制品，封装了环境设置、数据处理、模拟、结果捕获和可视化步骤。用户只需指定高级意图，Adviser负责资源供应、运行时配置和数据移动。

Result: 使用两个计算冰川学代码（Icepack和PISM）进行验证，展示了如何通过Adviser获得科学洞察，并在无需云或高性能计算专业知识的情况下，快速探索成本-性能权衡和扩展行为。

Conclusion: 通过工作流抽象封装专业知识的方式，Adviser成功降低了云计算在科学计算领域的使用门槛，使科学家能够专注于研究本身而非基础设施复杂性。

Abstract: Effectively leveraging the vast computational resources of modern cloud environments requires expertise spanning multiple technical domains: configuring scientific software with correct parameters and dependencies, navigating thousands of provider-specific instance types and pricing options, and managing parallel or distributed execution. We conduct a study indicating that the absence of these categories of expertise poses an ongoing challenge to unlocking the potential of cloud-enabled computational science. To address this challenge, we introduce Adviser, an intuitive multi-cloud platform centered on a workflow abstraction. Workflows are reusable, expert-crafted artifacts encapsulating environment setup, data processing, simulation, result capture, and visualization steps needed to execute scientific and ML applications. This approach allows users to specify high-level intent, while Adviser handles resource provisioning, runtime configuration, and data movement. Using two computational glaciology codes, Icepack and PISM, we show how to use Adviser to gain scientific insight and perform rapid exploration of cost-performance tradeoffs and scaling behavior without specialized expertise in cloud or high-performance computing.

</details>


### [102] [Communication Lower Bounds and Algorithms for Sketching with Random Dense Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20966)
*Hussam Al Daas,Grey Ballard,Laura Grigori,Md Taufique Hussain,Suraj Kumar,Mohammad Marufur Rahman,Kathryn Rouse*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文建立了分布式环境下矩阵素描（sketching）及Nyström近似的通信下界，并提出了通信最优或接近最优的并行算法，在现代超算平台上验证了其可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵素描在随机线性代数和机器学习中应用广泛，但在分布式内存环境下通信开销成为瓶颈，而分布式并行素描策略的研究尚属空白。

Method: 推导了使用稠密矩阵进行素描时的通信下界理论，据此设计通信最优的并行算法；将该理论扩展至Nyström近似（涉及两次素描），并设计了接近下界的并行算法。

Result: 证明了所提通信下界是紧的，并提供了匹配该下界的通信最优算法；Nyström近似算法的通信成本接近理论下界；在现代CPU和GPU超算系统上实现并展示了良好的并行可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了分布式并行素描算法的理论空白，为大规模机器学习与线性代数计算中的通信瓶颈提供了理论指导与高效实践方案。

Abstract: Sketching is widely used in randomized linear algebra for low-rank matrix approximation, column subset selection, and many other problems, and it has gained significant traction in machine learning applications. However, sketching large matrices often necessitates distributed memory algorithms, where communication overhead becomes a critical bottleneck on modern supercomputing clusters. Despite its growing relevance, distributed-memory parallel strategies for sketching remain largely unexplored. In this work, we establish communication lower bounds for sketching using dense matrices that determine how much data movement is required to perform it in parallel. One important observation of our lower bounds is that no communication is required for a small number of processors. We show that our lower bounds are tight by presenting communication optimal algorithms. Furthermore, we extend our approach to determine communication lower bounds for computations of Nyström approximation where sketching is applied twice. We also introduce novel parallel algorithms whose communication costs are close to the lower bounds. Finally, we implement our algorithms on modern state-of-the-art supercomputing infrastructures which have both CPU- and GPU-equipped systems and demonstrate their parallel scalability.

</details>


### [103] [NeSy-Edge: Neuro-Symbolic Trustworthy Self-Healing in the Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21145)
*Peihan Ye,Alfreds Lapkovskis,Alaa Saleh,Qiyang Zhang,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeSy-Edge，一个面向计算连续体的神经符号自愈框架，通过边缘优先设计在资源受限环境下实现鲁棒的故障诊断与恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI服务的计算需求正从集中式云转向跨越边缘和终端设备的计算连续体。然而，这些环境的规模、异构性和跨层依赖性使得韧性难以维持。现有的故障管理方法过于静态、碎片化或笨重，难以支持及时的自愈，特别是在噪声日志和边缘资源约束条件下。

Method: 提出NeSy-Edge神经符号框架，采用边缘优先设计：资源受限的边缘节点执行本地感知和推理，云模型仅在最终诊断阶段调用。具体方法包括：将原始运行时日志转换为结构化事件表示，构建先验约束的稀疏符号因果图，并整合因果证据与历史故障排查知识进行根因分析和恢复推荐。

Result: 在代表性Loghub数据集上，在多级语义噪声条件下评估了解析质量、因果推理、端到端诊断和边缘资源使用。结果表明，NeSy-Edge即使在最高噪声水平下仍保持鲁棒性，根因分析准确率高达75%，端到端准确率达65%，同时本地内存占用约1500MB。

Conclusion: NeSy-Edge框架成功解决了计算连续体环境下的故障自愈难题，在噪声干扰和资源约束下实现了高效可靠的故障诊断与恢复，为边缘计算环境的可信自愈提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The computational demands of modern AI services are increasingly shifting execution beyond centralized clouds toward a computing continuum spanning edge and end devices. However, the scale, heterogeneity, and cross-layer dependencies of these environments make resilience difficult to maintain. Existing fault-management methods are often too static, fragmented, or heavy to support timely self-healing, especially under noisy logs and edge resource constraints. To address these limitations, this paper presents NeSy-Edge, a neuro-symbolic framework for trustworthy self-healing in the computing continuum. The framework follows an edge-first design, where a resource-constrained edge node performs local perception and reasoning, while a cloud model is invoked only at the final diagnosis stage. Specifically, NeSy-Edge converts raw runtime logs into structured event representations, builds a prior-constrained sparse symbolic causal graph, and integrates causal evidence with historical troubleshooting knowledge for root-cause analysis and recovery recommendation. We evaluate our work on representative Loghub datasets under multiple levels of semantic noise, considering parsing quality, causal reasoning, end-to-end diagnosis, and edge-side resource usage. The results show that NeSy-Edge remains robust even at the highest noise level, achieving up to 75% root-cause analysis accuracy and 65% end-to-end accuracy while operating within about 1500 MB of local memory.

</details>


### [104] [CALVO: Improve Serving Efficiency for LLM Inferences with Intense Network Demands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21257)
*Weiye Wang,Chen Chen,Junxue Zhang,Zhusheng Wang,Hui Yuan,Zixuan Guan,Xiaolong Zheng,Qizhen Weng,Yin Chen,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CALVO，一个将KVCache加载视为一等公民的LLM推理服务引擎，通过解耦KVCache加载与GPU计算并显式建模加载延迟，显著提升了网络密集型长上下文推理的效率，SLO达成率最高提升61.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式前缀缓存已成为高效LLM服务的核心技术，但在长上下文请求的高缓存命中率场景下，从远程服务器检索可复用的KVCache块已成为新的性能瓶颈。随着代理式AI工作负载的增长，这种网络密集型推理将愈发普遍。然而，现有LLM推理引擎仍然以计算为中心，将KVCache加载视为GPU执行的从属阶段，在调度时往往未能显式考虑其延迟。

Method: CALVO将KVCache加载与GPU计算解耦为独立管理、异步推进的阶段，实现了网络、PCIe和计算资源的更好利用。此外，CALVO将KVCache加载延迟作为每请求服务成本的显式组成部分，从而做出更准确的调度决策。

Result: 在真实测试平台上针对多样化长上下文工作负载的实验表明，CALVO显著提升了网络密集型LLM推理的效率，SLO达成率相比基线最高提升61.67%。

Conclusion: 通过将KVCache加载作为一等关注点进行管理，CALVO有效解决了现有系统在网络密集型推理场景下的不足，为未来代理式AI工作负载的高效服务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed prefix caching has become a core technique for efficient LLM serving. However, for long-context requests with high cache hit ratios, retrieving reusable KVCache blocks from remote servers has emerged as a new performance bottleneck. Such network-intensive LLM inference is expected to become increasingly common as agentic AI workloads continue to grow. However, existing LLM inference engines remain largely compute-centric: they treat KVCache loading as a subordinate phase to GPU execution and often fail to account for its delay explicitly during scheduling.
  We present CALVO, an LLM serving engine that treats KVCache loading as a first-class concern. CALVO decouples KVCache loading and GPU computation into independently managed, asynchronously progressing stages, enabling better utilization of network, PCIe, and computation resources. In addition, CALVO incorporates KVCache loading delay as an explicit component of per-request service cost, leading to more accurate scheduling decisions. Experiments on a real testbed with diverse long-context workloads show that CALVO substantially improves the efficiency of network-intensive LLM inference, achieving up to 61.67% higher SLO attainment than the baseline.

</details>


### [105] [Decidability of Livelock Detection for Parameterized Self-Disabling Unidirectional Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21443)
*Aly Farahat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了参数化对称单向环中活锁检测问题可在多项式时间内判定，算法时间复杂度为O(|T|^3)，与环大小无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化分布式系统中活锁检测的可判定性是一个重要问题。Farahat的工作给出了活锁存在的代数特征，但未解决可判定性问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，为有界域上的自禁用进程组成的参数化对称单向环提供多项式时间的活锁检测算法。

Method: 算法基于有限转移集合T上的递减单调算子计算最大不动点。通过不动点的非空性判定活锁存在性：若不动点非空则存在活锁。该方法的正确性基于极大性论证——不动点表示能够在每个进程上维持伪活锁的最大转移集合。算法还扩展到(1,1)-非对称情形（一个特殊进程P₀与其余K-1个相同进程不同）。

Result: 证明了活锁检测问题在多项式时间内可判定，算法复杂度为O(|T|^3)，且与环大小K无关。最大不动点为空即证明对所有K值均无活锁，无需遍历搜索环大小。方法适用于对称情形和(1,1)-非对称情形。

Conclusion: 本文首次证明了参数化对称单向环上活锁检测问题的多项式时间可判定性，通过代数方法实现了无需搜索环大小的高效检测，为分布式协议验证提供了新的理论工具和实用算法。

Abstract: We prove that livelock detection is \emph{decidable in polynomial time} for parameterized symmetric unidirectional rings of self-disabling processes with bounded domain $\mathbb{Z}_m$. Given a protocol specified by its set of local transitions $T$, the algorithm decides whether a livelock exists for \emph{some} ring size $K\!\geq\!2$, running in $O(|T|^3)$ time independent of $K$. The algorithm computes the greatest fixed point of a deflationary monotone operator on the finite set $T$ and returns \emph{livelock} iff the fixed point is non-empty. The livelock freedom argument rests on maximality: the fix-point is the largest set of transitions that can together sustain a pseudolivelock at every process; its emptiness certifies freedom for all $K$ without any search over ring sizes. The work is grounded in the algebraic characterization of livelocks from Farahat~\citep{farahat2012}, which establishes necessary and sufficient conditions for livelock existence but does not address decidability. We also handle the $(1,1)$-asymmetric case in which one distinguished process $P_0$ differs from the remaining $K\!-\!1$ identical processes. Code and algebraic foundation are at the URL: https://github.com/cosmoparadox/mathematical-tools.

</details>


### [106] [Communication-Avoiding SpGEMM via Trident Partitioning on Hierarchical GPU Interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21444)
*Julian Bellavita,Lorenzo Pichetti,Thomas Pasquali,Flavio Vella,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Trident，一种层次感知的2D分布式稀疏矩阵乘法算法，通过利用现代超算互连的层次结构特性，显著降低了节点间通信量，性能提升高达2.38倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏矩阵乘法（SpGEMM）是科学计算和大规模数据分析的核心算子，但其非结构化稀疏性导致内存效率和可扩展性受限。在分布式内存环境中，节点间交换和合并部分积的开销进一步制约性能。现代异构超算具有深层、层次化的GPU互连结构，现有SpGEMM实现忽视了节点内与节点间带宽差距，导致不必要的数据移动和同步，未能充分利用节点内高速互连。

Method: 提出Trident算法，这是一种层次感知的2D分布式SpGEMM算法。核心创新是"trident划分方案"，该方案实现层次感知分解，利用节点内GPU间带宽高于节点间带宽的特性，结合通信规避技术和异步通信，减少节点间通信量，充分挖掘现代超算层次化异构架构的潜力。

Result: 在非结构化矩阵上评估Trident，相比传统2D SpGEMM实现最高加速2.38倍，几何平均加速1.54倍。在NERSC的Perlmutter超算上，节点间通信量减少最高达2倍。在Markov聚类应用中，相比竞争策略实现高达2倍加速。

Conclusion: Trident成功解决了现代异构超算层次化互连架构下SpGEMM的性能瓶颈，通过层次感知划分有效减少节点间通信，显著提升性能，证明了层次感知算法设计对分布式稀疏计算的重要性。

Abstract: The multiplication of two sparse matrices, known as SpGEMM, is a key kernel in scientific computing and large-scale data analytics, underpinning graph algorithms, machine learning, simulations, and computational biology, where sparsity is often highly unstructured. The unstructured sparsity makes achieving high performance challenging because it limits both memory efficiency and scalability. In distributed memory, the cost of exchanging and merging partial products across nodes further constrains performance. These issues are exacerbated on modern heterogeneous supercomputers with deep, hierarchical GPU interconnects. Current SpGEMM implementations overlook the gap between intra-node and inter-node bandwidth, resulting in unnecessary data movement and synchronization not fully exploiting the fast intra-node interconnect. To address these challenges, we introduce Trident, a hierarchy-aware 2D distributed SpGEMM algorithm that uses communication-avoiding techniques and asynchronous communication to exploit the hierarchical and heterogeneous architecture of modern supercomputing interconnect. Central to Trident is the novel trident partitioning scheme, which enables hierarchy-aware decomposition and reduces internode communication by leveraging the higher bandwidth between GPUs within a node compared to across nodes. Here, we evaluate Trident on unstructured matrices, achieving up to $2.38\times$ speedup over a 2D SpGEMM with a corresponding geometric mean speedup of $1.54\times$. Trident reduces internode communication volume by up to $2\times$ on NERSC's Perlmutter supercomputer. Furthermore, we demonstrate the effectiveness of Trident in speeding up Markov Clustering, achieving up to $2\times$ speedup compared to competing strategies.

</details>


### [107] [Benchmarking Message Brokers for IoT Edge Computing: A Comprehensive Performance Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21600)
*Tapajit Chandra Paul,Pawissanutt Lertpongrujikorn,Hai Duc Nguyen,Mohsen Amini Salehi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了mq-bench基准测试框架，对8种主流消息代理进行了系统性能评估，发现轻量级原生代理延迟更低，多线程架构扩展性更好，而Java代理内存消耗显著更高，为边缘计算场景提供了选型指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有消息队列评估主要关注固定硬件上的吞吐量和延迟，忽视了CPU和内存占用及资源限制的影响，而这些因素对边缘和IoT部署至关重要，导致选择合适的MQ系统仍然困难。

Method: 提出mq-bench统一基准测试框架，在三种代表边缘硬件的虚拟机配置下，对Mosquitto、EMQX、HiveMQ、RabbitMQ、ActiveMQ Artemis、NATS Server、Redis和Zenoh Router八个消息代理进行评估，扩展到10,000个并发客户端对。
