Result: 轻量级原生代理实现亚毫秒级延迟，功能丰富的企业平台开销高2-3倍；高连接负载下，NATS和Zenoh等多线程代理扩展高效，而广泛部署的Mosquitto因单线程架构较早饱和；Java代理比原生实现消耗显著更多内存。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，为遥测、流分析和IoT用例提供了实用的部署指南，将工作负载需求和资源约束映射到适当的代理选择，帮助在边缘和IoT环境中做出合理的MQ系统选型决策。

Abstract: Asynchronous messaging is a cornerstone of modern distributed systems, enabling decoupled communication for scalable and resilient applications. Today's message queue (MQ) ecosystem spans a wide range of designs, from high-throughput streaming platforms to lightweight protocols tailored for edge and IoT environments. Despite this diversity, choosing an appropriate MQ system remains difficult. Existing evaluations largely focus on throughput and latency on fixed hardware, while overlooking CPU and memory footprint and the effects of resource constraints, factors that are critical for edge and IoT deployments. In this paper, we present a systematic performance study of eight prominent message brokers: Mosquitto, EMQX, HiveMQ, RabbitMQ, ActiveMQ Artemis, NATS Server, Redis (Pub/Sub), and Zenoh Router. We introduce mq-bench, a unified benchmarking framework to evaluate these systems under identical conditions, scaling up to 10,000 concurrent client pairs across three VM configurations representative of edge hardware. This study reveals several interesting and sometimes counter-intuitive insights. Lightweight native brokers achieve sub-millisecond latency, while feature-rich enterprise platforms incur 2-3X higher overhead. Under high connection loads, multi-threaded brokers like NATS and Zenoh scale efficiently, whereas the widely-deployed Mosquitto saturates earlier due to its single-threaded architecture. We also find that Java-based brokers consume significantly more memory than native implementations, which has important implications for memory-constrained edge deployments. Based on these findings, we provide practical deployment guidelines that map workload requirements and resource constraints to appropriate broker choices for telemetry, streaming analytics, and IoT use cases.

</details>


### [108] [exaCB: Reproducible Continuous Benchmark Collections at Scale Leveraging an Incremental Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22251)
*Jayesh Badwaik,Mathis Bode,Michal Rajski,Andreas Herten*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了exaCB，一个面向百亿亿次级HPC系统的持续基准测试框架，已成功应用于JUPITER系统，支持超过70个应用的性能和能效评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着HPC系统异构性增加和向E级计算架构演进，需要系统化、可重复的性能评估。传统性能测试在CI流程外进行，缺乏持续性和可重复性，因此需要将持续基准测试(CB)作为CI的补充方法集成到软件开发生命周期中。

Method: 提出exaCB框架，通过可复用的CI/CD组件、成熟的测试工具链和共享报告协议，支持将基准测试集成到工作流中。框架支持渐进式采用，允许基准测试从基本运行性验证逐步演进到高级仪表化和可重复性保证。

Result: 在JUPITER百亿亿次系统的早期访问项目JUREAP中部署应用，成功对70多个不同成熟度的应用进行持续基准测试，支持跨应用分析、性能追踪和能耗研究。

Conclusion: exaCB框架证明了在百亿亿次HPC系统上对大规模、多样化科学应用集合进行持续基准测试的可行性，为应用团队提供了有效的性能和能效评估解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing heterogeneity of high-performance computing (HPC) systems and the transition to exascale architectures require systematic and reproducible performance evaluation across diverse workloads. While continuous integration (CI) ensures functional correctness in software engineering, performance and energy efficiency in HPC are typically evaluated outside CI workflows, motivating continuous benchmarking (CB) as a complementary approach. Integrating benchmarking into CI workflows enables reproducible evaluation, early detection of regressions, and continuous validation throughout the software development lifecycle.
  We present exaCB, a framework for continuous benchmarking developed in the context of the JUPITER exascale system. exaCB enables application teams to integrate benchmarking into their workflows while supporting large-scale, system-wide studies through reusable CI/CD components, established harnesses, and a shared reporting protocol. The framework supports incremental adoption, allowing benchmarks to be onboarded easily and to evolve from basic runnability to more advanced instrumentation and reproducibility. The approach is demonstrated in JUREAP, the early-access program for JUPITER, where exaCB enabled continuous benchmarking of over 70 applications at varying maturity levels, supporting cross-application analysis, performance tracking, and energy-aware studies. These results illustrate the practicality using exaCB for continuous benchmarking for exascale HPC systems across large, diverse collections of scientific applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Reason-to-Transmit: Deliberative Adaptive Communication for Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20308)
*Aayam Bansal,Ishaan Gangwani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Reason-to-Transmit (R2T) 框架，通过基于推理的传输决策机制解决V2X网络中协同感知的带宽限制问题，在高遮挡场景下性能显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知方法依赖置信度图、门控学习或稀疏掩码等反应式机制进行传输决策，缺乏对消息为何有益于接收者的推理能力，无法有效利用有限的带宽资源。

Method: 提出R2T框架，为每个智能体配备轻量级Transformer模块，基于局部场景上下文、邻居信息缺口估计和带宽预算进行推理，实现逐区域的传输决策，并采用带宽感知目标进行端到端训练。

Result: 在多智能体鸟瞰图感知环境中对九个基线进行评估：相比无通信提升约58% AP；低带宽下各选择性方法性能相近，但在高遮挡（信息不对称最大）场景下R2T接近Oracle性能，优势明显；所有方法在50%丢包率下性能平缓下降，表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 融合设计主导整体性能，但在高遮挡等挑战场景中，深思熟虑的通信策略能带来额外增益。R2T引入了基于推理的通信方法，实现了更高效、上下文感知的协同感知信息共享。

Abstract: Cooperative perception among autonomous agents overcomes the limitations of single-agent sensing, but bandwidth constraints in vehicle-to-everything (V2X) networks require efficient communication policies. Existing approaches rely on reactive mechanisms, such as confidence maps, learned gating, or sparse masks, to decide what to transmit, without reasoning about why a message benefits the receiver. We introduce Reason-to-Transmit (R2T), a framework that equips each agent with a lightweight transformer-based module that reasons over local scene context, estimated neighbor information gaps, and bandwidth budget to make per-region transmission decisions. Trained end-to-end with a bandwidth-aware objective, R2T is evaluated against nine baselines in a multi-agent bird's-eye-view perception environment. Any communication improves performance by about 58% AP over no communication. At low bandwidth, all selective methods perform similarly, but R2T shows clear gains under high occlusion, where information asymmetry is greatest, approaching oracle performance. All methods degrade gracefully under packet drops up to 50%, showing robustness to communication failures. These results indicate that while fusion design dominates performance, deliberative communication provides additional gains in challenging scenarios. R2T introduces a reasoning-based approach to communication, enabling more efficient and context-aware information sharing in cooperative perception.

</details>


### [110] [When Agents Disagree: The Selection Bottleneck in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20324)
*Artem Maryanskyy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究揭示了团队多样性在多智能体LLM中的双重作用：当使用高质量的judge选择机制时，多样性团队显著优于单一模型（胜率0.810 vs 0.512）；但当采用合成聚合时，同质团队更优。关键在于选择瓶颈阈值$s^*$——跨过此阈值，多样性有益；否则有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在团队多样性是否提升输出质量上存在矛盾证据：异构MoA团队优于单模型，但同构Self-MoA团队在合成聚合下表现更佳。作者旨在解决这一悖论，明确何时多样性有益或有害。

Method: 1) 提出选择瓶颈理论模型，推导封闭形式阈值$s^*$划分多样性有益/有害区域；
2) 设计对照实验：42任务×7类别（N=210），比较多样性团队+judge选择 vs 同质团队 vs 单模型基线；
3) 采用独立judge进行解耦评估验证结论稳健性。

Result: 1) 多样性团队+judge选择胜率0.810，同质团队仅0.512（Glass's Δ=2.07）；
2) Judge选择比MoA合成优Δ_WR=+0.631，合成方法在42任务中0次胜出；
3) 独立评估确认发现（Spearman ρ=0.90）；
4) 探索性发现：加入弱模型可提升性能并降低成本（p<10⁻⁴）。

Conclusion: 选择器质量是单轮生成-选择管道中比生成器多样性更关键的设计杠杆。跨过选择质量阈值后多样性才发挥作用，否则反而有害。这为多智能体LLM系统设计提供了明确的理论指导。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines produce contradictory evidence on whether team diversity improves output quality: heterogeneous Mixture-of-Agents teams outperform single models, yet homogeneous Self-MoA teams consistently win under synthesis-based aggregation. We propose a resolution by identifying the selection bottleneck -- a crossover threshold in aggregation quality that determines whether diversity helps or hurts. Under this model, we obtain a closed-form crossover threshold $s^*$ (Proposition 1) that separates the regimes where diversity helps and hurts. In a targeted experiment spanning 42 tasks across 7 categories ($N=210$), a diverse team with judge-based selection achieves a win rate of 0.810 against a single-model baseline, while a homogeneous team scores 0.512 -- near chance (Glass's $Δ= 2.07$). Judge-based selection outperforms MoA-style synthesis by $Δ_{\mathrm{WR}} = +0.631$ -- the synthesis approach is preferred over the baseline in zero of 42 tasks by the judge panel. A decoupled evaluation with independent judges confirms all directional findings (Spearman $ρ= 0.90$). Exploratory evidence suggests that including a weaker model improves performance while reducing cost ($p < 10^{-4}$, not pre-registered). Our results suggest that selector quality may be a more impactful design lever than generator diversity in single-round generate-then-select pipelines.

</details>


### [111] [ALARA for Agents: Least-Privilege Context Engineering Through Portable Composable Multi-Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20380)
*Christopher J. Agostino,Nayan D'Souza*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出CAT数据层和npcsh命令行工具，通过声明式文件管理多智能体系统的工具访问权限和上下文范围，确保修改工具列表能产生确定的行为改变，并在22个模型上进行了全面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架缺乏统一、可扩展的管理机制，行为规范分散在指令文件、框架配置和MCP服务器中，难以共享、版本控制和协作维护，影响人机交互质量和团队协作能力。

Method: 借鉴辐射安全的ALARA原则，设计声明式的context-agent-tool（CAT）数据层，通过相互关联的文件限定每个智能体工具访问权限和上下文的最小需求范围，并开发npcsh命令行shell来执行和管理这些文件。

Result: 在115个实际任务（包括文件操作、网络搜索、多步骤脚本、工具链式调用和多智能体委托）中，对22个本地部署模型（0.6B-35B参数）进行约2500次执行评估，揭示了不同模型系列在各任务类别的成功率和失败点。

Conclusion: 结构化解析和强制执行配置文件可确保修改工具列表产生确定的行为改变而非模型可能忽略的建议，为多智能体系统提供了统一、可扩展且可协作维护的行为规范管理方案。

Abstract: Industry practitioners and academic researchers regularly use multi-agent systems to accelerate their work, yet the frameworks through which these systems operate do not provide a simple, unified mechanism for scalably managing the critical aspects of the agent harness, impacting both the quality of individual human-agent interactions and the capacity for practitioners to coordinate toward common goals through shared agent infrastructure. Agent frameworks have enabled increasingly sophisticated multi-agent systems, but the behavioral specifications that define what these agents can do remain fragmented across prose instruction files, framework-internal configuration, and mechanisms like MCP servers that operate separately from individual agent definitions, making these specifications difficult to share, version, or collaboratively maintain across teams and projects. Applying the ALARA principle from radiation safety (exposures kept as low as reasonably achievable) to agent context, we introduce a declarative context-agent-tool (CAT) data layer expressed through interrelated files that scope each agent's tool access and context to the minimum its role requires, and \texttt{npcsh}, a command-line shell for executing it. Because the system parses and enforces these files structurally, modifying an agent's tool list produces a guaranteed behavioral change rather than a suggestion the model may or may not follow. We evaluate 22 locally-hosted models from 0.6B to 35B parameters across 115 practical tasks spanning file operations, web search, multi-step scripting, tool chaining, and multi-agent delegation, characterizing which model families succeed at which task categories and where they break down across $\sim$2500 total executions.

</details>


### [112] [Measuring Reasoning Trace Legibility: Can Those Who Understand Teach?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20508)
*Dani Roytburg,Shreya Sridhar,Daphne Ippolito*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文评估了12个推理语言模型的9万条推理轨迹，发现性能最高的模型推理可读性最低，且奖励模型不会内在奖励可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理语言模型被训练在回答前输出推理过程，主要目标是提高答案正确性。然而作者认为除了答案正确性，还应评估推理轨迹的可读性（legibility），即推理过程是否易于理解和利用。

Method: 引入转移效用（transfer utility）概念，评估推理语言模型的推理轨迹对引导较弱非推理模型得出正确答案的有用程度。对12个推理语言模型的9万条推理轨迹进行了质量评估，分析了基于效率的可读性测量（如轨迹长度）与转移效用之间的关系。

Result: 发现性能最高的模型其推理可读性排名最低。基于效率的可读性测量（如轨迹长度）与转移效用之间存在张力，形成可读性帕累托前沿。推理语言模型输出高可读性轨迹的能力是任务和受众相关的目标。用于训练推理语言模型的奖励模型不会内在奖励可读性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了推理轨迹质量评估的重要维度，为多智能体未来中推理轨迹的搭建指明了方向，表明需要在模型训练中显式考虑推理可读性。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly being trained to "reason" before answering users' queries, outputting hundreds or even thousands of tokens worth of deliberation before their final answer. While the main intention of reasoning is to improve models' ability to arrive at a correct answer, we argue that these models should be assessed for the legibility of their reasoning traces in addition to the correctness of their final answers. In this paper, we evaluate 90k traces from 12 Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) for the quality of their reasoning traces. We introduce the concept of transfer utility, which assesses how useful an RLM's reasoning traces are for guiding a weaker, non-reasoning model toward arriving at the correct answer. We find that the reasoning traces of the highest-performing models rank among the lowest for legibility. Furthermore, we uncover tensions between efficiency-based measurements of legibility (such as trace length) and transfer utility. These tensions establish a legibility Pareto frontier, and we demonstrate that an RLM's ability to output highly legible traces can be a task- and audience-dependent goal. Crucially, we find that reward models used to train RLMs do not intrinsically reward legibility. Together, these metrics and the findings they surface chart a path towards scaffolding reasoning traces for a multi-agent future.

</details>


### [113] [Personality-Driven Student Agent-Based Modeling in Mathematics Education: How Well Do Student Agents Align with Human Learners?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21358)
*Bushi Xiao,Qian Shen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究构建了基于大五人格的学生智能体模型，通过14项准则评估其行为可信度，发现71.4%的行为与人类学习者一致，验证了LLM智能体模拟学生行为的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育研究中真人教学实验面临伦理约束，无法对同一学生重复进行实验。LLM生成式智能体为模拟学生行为提供了新途径，但在大规模应用前需验证学生智能体的可信度及其能否真实模拟人类学习。

Method: 构建基于大五人格的学生智能体模型，包含师生互动、自主学习和考试等完整流程；收集13项关于大五人格与学习的实证研究，提炼出14项评估准则，用于检验智能体行为与人类学习者的匹配程度。

Result: 学生智能体的行为中有71.4%与人类学习者保持一致，表明智能体能够在较大程度上忠实模拟人类学习行为。

Conclusion: 基于大五人格的LLM学生智能体具有较高的行为保真度，可作为教育研究中有价值的模拟工具，为后续大规模教学实验奠定了基础。

Abstract: It is crucial to explore the impact of different teaching methods on student learning in educational research. However, real-person experiments face significant ethical constraints, and we cannot conduct repeated teaching experiments on the same student. LLM-based generative agents offer a promising avenue for simulating student behavior. Before large-scale experiments, a fundamental question must be addressed: are student agents truly credible, and can they faithfully simulate human learning? In this study, we built a Big Five Personality-based student agent model with a full pipeline of student-teacher interaction, self-study, and examination. To evaluate behavioral fidelity, we collected 13 empirical studies on Big Five traits and learning, and distilled them into 14 criteria. We found that the 71.4% of the student agents' behavior was aligned with human learners.

</details>


### [114] [Is AI Ready for Multimodal Hate Speech Detection? A Comprehensive Dataset and Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21686)
*Rui Xing,Qi Chai,Jie Ma,Jing Tao,Pinghui Wang,Shuming Zhang,Xinping Wang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个智能体标注框架，构建了一个包含2,455个表情包的多平台、多语言、多模态仇恨言论数据集M³，发现现有最先进的多模态大语言模型在利用上下文语境时表现不佳，甚至导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态仇恨言论数据集存在标签粒度粗糙且缺乏对周边话语语境整合的问题，导致仇恨言论检测不够精确和完整。表情包作为传播仇恨言论的载体，往往依赖文化知识进行解读，需要更精细的标注和语境分析。

Method: 提出了一个协调七个专门智能体的智能体标注框架，用于生成分层标签和理由说明。基于该框架，从X、4chan和微博三个平台收集了2,455个表情包，构建了M³数据集，包含细粒度的仇恨标签和人工验证的理由说明。

Result: 对最先进的多模态大语言模型进行基准测试发现，这些模型难以有效利用周边帖子语境，语境信息往往无法提升甚至降低了检测性能，揭示了这些模型在面对嵌入真实话语语境中的表情包推理时面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了现有多模态模型在真实话语语境中进行表情包推理的困难，突显了开发具有语境感知能力的多模态架构的必要性。

Abstract: Hate speech online targets individuals or groups based on identity attributes and spreads rapidly, posing serious social risks. Memes, which combine images and text, have emerged as a nuanced vehicle for disseminating hate speech, often relying on cultural knowledge for interpretation. However, existing multimodal hate speech datasets suffer from coarse-grained labeling and a lack of integration with surrounding discourse, leading to imprecise and incomplete assessments. To bridge this gap, we propose an agentic annotation framework that coordinates seven specialized agents to generate hierarchical labels and rationales. Based on this framework, we construct M^3 (Multi-platform, Multi-lingual, and Multimodal Meme), a dataset of 2,455 memes collected from X, 4chan, and Weibo, featuring fine-grained hate labels and human-verified rationales. Benchmarking state-of-the-art Multimodal Large Language Models reveals that these models struggle to effectively utilize surrounding post context, which often fails to improve or even degrades detection performance. Our finding highlights the challenges these models face in reasoning over memes embedded in real-world discourse and underscores the need for a context-aware multimodal architecture. Our dataset and code are available at https://github.com/mira-ai-lab/M3.

</details>


### [115] [Strategic Infrastructure Design via Multi-Agent Congestion Games with Joint Placement and Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21691)
*Niloofar Aminikalibar,Farzaneh Farhadi,Maria Chli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多智能体场景的设施选址与定价联合优化框架（ABO-MPN），通过双层优化建模，在满足预算和盈利约束下显著降低社会成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的基础设施规划（如电动汽车充电、应急响应、智能交通）日益涉及自利智能体之间的战略交互和竞争有限的拥堵资源。规划者需要在预判去中心化智能体自适应行为的同时，做出协调的资源选址和定价决策。

Method: 提出一个双层优化模型：上层代表中央规划者，下层通过耦合的非原子拥堵博弈捕捉智能体响应。针对NP难问题，设计了ABO-MPN双层近似框架，该框架解耦智能体类型，应用整数调整和舍入技术，并针对高影响力的选址和定价决策进行优化。

Result: 在基准网络上的实验表明，与仅进行选址或仅进行定价的基线方法相比，该模型将社会成本降低了高达40%，并能泛化至其他多智能体相关领域。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合选址与定价多智能体框架能够有效解决资源竞争场景下的基础设施规划问题，在降低社会成本方面显著优于传统单一决策方法，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Real-world infrastructure planning increasingly involves strategic interactions among autonomous agents competing over congestible, limited resources. Applications such as Electric Vehicle (EV) charging, emergency response, and intelligent transportation require coordinated resource placement and pricing decisions, while anticipating the adaptive behaviour of decentralised, self-interested agents. We propose a novel multi-agent framework for joint placement and pricing under such interactions, formalised as a bi-level optimisation model. The upper level represents a central planner, while the lower level captures agent responses via coupled non-atomic congestion games. Motivated by the EV charging domain, we study a setting where a central planner provisions chargers and road capacity under budget and profitability constraints. The agent population includes both EV drivers and non-charging drivers (NCDs), who respond to congestion, delays, and costs. To solve the resulting NP-hard problem, we introduce ABO-MPN, a double-layer approximation framework that decouples agent types, applies integer adjustment and rounding, and targets high-impact placement and pricing decisions. Experiments on benchmark networks show that our model reduces social cost by up to 40% compared to placement- or pricing-only baselines, and generalises to other MAS-relevant domains.

</details>


### [116] [A Game-Theoretic Framework for Intelligent EV Charging Network Optimisation in Smart Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21715)
*Niloofar Aminikalibar,Farzaneh Farhadi,Maria Chli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对电动汽车充电站选址与定价的联合优化框架（JPPO-DE），通过建模驾驶员策略行为与交通均衡，在保证盈利的前提下最小化社会成本。实验表明该方法在多种场景下较现有技术至少提升16%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车的普及需要智能且具备拥堵感知的基础设施规划，以在用户便利性、经济可行性和交通效率之间取得平衡。现有研究往往忽略驾驶员的策略性行为或仅单独考虑选址与定价问题，难以实现系统最优规划。

Method: 提出了一种双层近似求解方法——驾驶员均衡下的联合选址定价优化（JPPO-DE）。该框架建立了混合整数非线性规划模型，通过道路网络和充电设施上的耦合非原子拥堵博弈显式捕捉驾驶员的策略行为，结合驾驶员行为分解与整数松弛技术进行高效求解，并提供了扩展至更大规模网络的泛化程序。

Result: 在Sioux Falls交通网络基准测试中，该方法在各种预算、电动汽车渗透率和充电站容量设置下均优于单参数基线，相较于最先进方法至少实现了16%的性能提升，并具备良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过精确建模交通均衡并实现自适应、高效的基础设施设计，该框架推进了可持续城市移动性中智能交通系统的关键目标，为电动汽车基础设施规划提供了有效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: The transition to Electric Vehicles (EVs) demands intelligent, congestion-aware infrastructure planning to balance user convenience, economic viability, and traffic efficiency. We present a joint optimisation framework for EV Charging Station (CS) placement and pricing, explicitly capturing strategic driver behaviour through coupled non-atomic congestion games over road networks and charging facilities. From a Public Authority (PA) perspective, the model minimises social cost, travel times, queuing delays and charging expenses, while ensuring infrastructure profitability. To solve the resulting Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Programme, we propose a scalable two-level approximation method, Joint Placement and Pricing Optimisation under Driver Equilibrium (JPPO-DE), combining driver behaviour decomposition with integer relaxation. Experiments on the benchmark Sioux Falls Transportation Network (TN) demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms single-parameter baselines, effectively adapting to varying budgets, EV penetration levels, and station capacities. It achieves performance improvements of at least 16% over state-of-the-art approaches. A generalisation procedure further extends scalability to larger networks. By accurately modelling traffic equilibria and enabling adaptive, efficient infrastructure design, our framework advances key intelligent transportation system goals for sustainable urban mobility.

</details>


### [117] [Human-Inspired Pavlovian and Instrumental Learning for Autonomous Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22170)
*Jingfeng Shan,Francesco Guidi,Mehrdad Saeidi,Enrico Testi,Elia Favarelli,Andrea Giorgetti,Davide Dardari,Alberto Zanella,Giorgio Li Pira,Francesca Starita,Anna Guerra*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种受人类认知启发的混合强化学习架构，通过整合巴甫洛夫条件反射、无模型和有模型组件，在不确定环境中实现了更安全、更高效的学习与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在不确定环境中需平衡快速响应与目标规划。经典的无模型（MF）强化学习收敛慢且探索不安全，而有模型（MB）方法计算成本高且对模型失配敏感，现有方法难以兼顾效率与安全性。

Method: 受神经科学中巴甫洛夫与工具性学习启发，构建混合架构。框架将地理环境特征作为条件刺激（CS）塑造内在价值信号，并引入动机信号调节学习；通过贝叶斯仲裁机制根据预测可靠性自适应融合MF与MB估计。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该混合方法相比标准RL基线加快了学习速度，提升了操作安全性，并减少了对高不确定性区域的导航。巴甫洛夫条件反射促进了更安全的探索和更快的收敛，仲裁机制实现了从探索到高效规划利用的平滑过渡。

Conclusion: 研究结果突显了生物启发的模块化设计对于在不确定性下构建鲁棒、自适应自主系统的优势。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating in uncertain environments must balance fast responses with goal-directed planning. Classical MF RL often converges slowly and may induce unsafe exploration, whereas MB methods are computationally expensive and sensitive to model mismatch. This paper presents a human-inspired hybrid RL architecture integrating Pavlovian, Instrumental MF, and Instrumental MB components. Inspired by Pavlovian and Instrumental learning from neuroscience, the framework considers contextual radio cues, here intended as georeferenced environmental features acting as CS, to shape intrinsic value signals and bias decision-making. Learning is further modulated by internal motivational drives through a dedicated motivational signal. A Bayesian arbitration mechanism adaptively blends MF and MB estimates based on predicted reliability. Simulation results show that the hybrid approach accelerates learning, improves operational safety, and reduces navigation in high-uncertainty regions compared to standard RL baselines. Pavlovian conditioning promotes safer exploration and faster convergence, while arbitration enables a smooth transition from exploration to efficient, plan-driven exploitation. Overall, the results highlight the benefits of biologically inspired modularity for robust and adaptive autonomous systems under uncertainty.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Hetero-Net: An Energy-Efficient Resource Allocation and 3D Placement in Heterogeneous LoRa Networks via Multi-Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20404)
*Abdullahi Isa Ahmed,Ana Maria Drăgulinescu,El Mehdi Amhoud*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hetero-Net，一种统一的异构LoRa框架，通过MAPPO算法联合优化扩频因子、发射功率和UAV三维位置，实现地面WSN与地下WUSN的集成，相比独立部署方案能效提升高达198.49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRa的网络设计通常将地面无线传感器网络(WSN)和地下无线传感器网络(WUSN)视为独立系统，导致跨多样化环境的连接效率低下且缺乏集成性。

Method: 提出Hetero-Net统一异构LoRa框架，集成多种LoRa终端设备与多无人机(UAV)载LoRa网关；将问题建模为部分可观测随机博弈(POSG)，采用多智能体近端策略优化(MAPPO)框架联合优化扩频因子、发射功率和UAV三维位置。

Result: 消融研究表明，所提MAPPO Hetero-Net显著优于传统独立网络设计，相比独立WSN部署能效提升55.81%，相比独立WUSN部署能效提升198.49%。

Conclusion: Hetero-Net通过统一框架成功整合了地面与地下传感器网络，有效解决了跨环境连接问题，为多层IoT环境下的异构LoRa网络部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The evolution of Internet of Things (IoT) into multi-layered environments has positioned Low-Power Wide Area Networks (LPWANs), particularly Long Range (LoRa), as the backbone for connectivity across both surface and subterranean landscapes. However, existing LoRa-based network designs often treat ground-based wireless sensor networks (WSNs) and wireless underground sensor networks (WUSNs) as separate systems, resulting in inefficient and non-integrated connectivity across diverse environments. To address this, we propose Hetero-Net, a unified heterogeneous LoRa framework that integrates diverse LoRa end devices with multiple unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-mounted LoRa gateways. Our objective is to maximize system energy efficiency through the joint optimization of the spreading factor, transmission power, and three-dimensional (3D) placement of the UAVs. To manage the dynamic and partially observable nature of this system, we model the problem as a partially observable stochastic game (POSG) and address it using a multi-agent proximal policy optimization (MAPPO) framework. An ablation study shows that our proposed MAPPO Hetero-Net significantly outperforms traditional, isolated network designs, achieving energy efficiency improvements of 55.81\% and 198.49\% over isolated WSN-only and WUSN-only deployments, respectively.

</details>


### [119] [RISE: Real-time Image Processing for Spectral Energy Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20481)
*Chung-Hsuan Tung,Zhenzhou Qi,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RISE，一种基于图像处理技术的实时频谱感知系统，通过将时频谱图视为图像并应用自适应阈值、形态学操作和连通分量标记，实现了高效信号检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量检测广泛应用于频谱感知，但在实时条件下准确定位信号的时间和频率占用以实现高效频谱共享仍面临挑战。现有方法在实时性、准确性和定位精度之间存在权衡，亟需一种能够满足实时性要求同时保持高检测精度的解决方案。

Method: RISE将时频谱图视为图像，采用自适应阈值、形态学操作和连通分量标记等图像处理技术进行信号检测与定位。系统采用多线程架构设计，实现了对原始I/Q数据的实时处理能力，支持100 MHz带宽下的3.2 Gbps输入速率，时间和频率分辨率分别达到10.24 μs和97.6 kHz。

Result: 使用合成数据和受控空中（OTA）实验进行评估，RISE在IoU阈值为0.4时检测概率达80.42%，满足实时延迟约束。与Searchlight相比，延迟降低20.51倍，IoU提升22.31%；与DeepRadar相比，IoU提升56.02%；与GPU加速的U-Net相比，实时性能优于其213.38倍的延迟超标。

Conclusion: RISE成功实现了实时频谱感知与信号定位，在保证实时性的同时显著提升了检测精度和定位准确度，为动态频谱共享提供了高效的软件化解决方案。

Abstract: Energy detection is widely used for spectrum sensing, but accurately localizing the time and frequency occupation of signals in real-time for efficient spectrum sharing remains challenging. To address this challenge, we present RISE, a software-based spectrum sensing system designed for real-time signal detection and localization. RISE treats time-frequency spectrum plots as images and applies adaptive thresholding, morphological operations, and connected component labeling with a multi-threaded architecture. We evaluate RISE using both synthetic data and controlled over-the-air (OTA) experiments across diverse signal types. Results show that RISE satisfies real-time latency constraints while achieving a probability of detection of 80.42% at an intersection-over-union (IoU) threshold of 0.4. RISE sustains a raw I/Q input rate of 3.2 Gbps for 100 MHz bandwidth sensing with time and frequency resolutions of 10.24 us and 97.6 kHz, respectively. Compared to Searchlight, a representative energy-based method, RISE achieves 20.51x lower latency and 22.31% higher IoU. Compared to machine learning baselines, RISE improves IoU by 56.02% over DeepRadar while meeting the real-time deadline, which a GPU-accelerated U-Net exceeds by 213.38x.

</details>


### [120] [Fluid Antenna Networks Beyond Beamforming: An AI-Native Control Paradigm for 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20484)
*Ian F. Akyildiz,Tuğçe Bilen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向6G系统的AI原生流体天线网络架构，通过闭环控制将天线位置调整与无线资源管理联合优化，利用多智能体强化学习实现多小区环境下的分布式控制，证明了智能天线适应能显著提升小区边缘性能并减少小区间干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流体天线系统（FAS）研究主要关注物理层增益，而对网络运营的更广泛影响尚未充分探索。当天线成为可重构实体时，天线定位自然成为网络控制问题的一部分，而非独立的优化任务。

Method: 提出AI原生的闭环控制架构，将天线适应与传统无线资源管理（RRM）功能联合管理。该框架将实时网络观测转化为协调的天线和资源配置决策，响应于用户移动性、流量需求和演进的干扰条件。针对多小区环境的复杂性，采用多智能体强化学习（MARL）方法实现跨基站的分布式自适应控制。

Result: 实验结果表明，智能天线适应能够产生一致的性能增益，特别是在小区边缘区域，同时减少小区间干扰。

Conclusion: 流体天线系统的真正潜力不仅在于可重构硬件，更在于能够有效利用这一额外空间自由度的智能网络控制架构。

Abstract: Fluid Antenna Systems (FAS) introduce a new degree of freedom for wireless networks by enabling the physical antenna position to adapt dynamically to changing radio conditions. While existing studies primarily emphasize physical-layer gains, their broader implications for network operation remain largely unexplored. Once antennas become reconfigurable entities, antenna positioning naturally becomes part of the network control problem rather than a standalone optimization task. This article presents an AI-native perspective on fluid antenna networks for future 6G systems. Instead of treating antenna repositioning as an isolated operation, we consider a closed-loop control architecture in which antenna adaptation is jointly managed with conventional radio resource management (RRM) functions. Within this framework, real-time network observations are translated into coordinated antenna and resource configuration decisions that respond to user mobility, traffic demand, and evolving interference conditions. To address the complexity of multi-cell environments, we explore a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) approach that enables distributed and adaptive control across base stations. Illustrative results show that intelligent antenna adaptation yields consistent performance gains, particularly at the cell edge, while also reducing inter-cell interference. These findings suggest that the true potential of fluid antenna systems lies not only in reconfigurable hardware, but in intelligent network control architectures that can effectively exploit this additional spatial degree of freedom.

</details>


### [121] [immUNITY: Detecting and Mitigating Low Volume & Slow Attacks with Programmable Switches and SmartNICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20573)
*Cuidi Wei,Shaoyu Tu,Daiki Hata,Toru Hasegawa,Yuki Koizumi,K. K. Ramakrishnan,Junji Takemasa,Timothy Wood*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了immUNITY系统，通过协同可编程交换机和SmartNIC，利用过滤数据结构、机器学习分类器和入侵检测规则，实现了对低容量慢速网络攻击（如端口扫描）的高精度实时检测与缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流记录的网络攻击检测主要关注大流量攻击，而实际网络中高达71%的流表现出可疑的低容量攻击行为（如端口扫描）。由于此类攻击流数据包极少，传统实时检测方法难以有效识别，亟需一种能够在数据平面实时区分异常流量的新方法。

Method: 扩展传统大流量检测方法，设计在可编程交换机上部署的高效过滤数据结构，快速将标记为良性的流量转发至目的端系统，将有限的可编程交换机存储与流水线资源与SmartNIC资源相结合。剩余可疑流量在SmartNIC上通过机器学习分类器和入侵检测规则进行分析，识别恶意源IP并触发详细取证以实现攻击缓解。同时，设计了基于数据平面的协议，用于在交换机与SmartNIC间快速协调数据结构更新。

Result: 在包含Tofino v1交换机与Bluefield 3 SmartNIC的实验平台上实现了immUNITY系统。实验结果表明，该系统在保证高检测准确率的同时，显著减少了需要在交换机外部进行深入分析的流量规模，实现了对低容量慢速攻击的实时有效识别。

Conclusion: immUNITY证明了通过协同可编程交换机与SmartNIC，利用过滤与智能分析相结合的架构，能够有效克服低容量攻击实时检测的难题，为网络入侵检测提供了新的思路。该系统在真实网络环境下具有高准确率和实时响应能力，具有较好的实际部署价值。

Abstract: Our analysis of recent Internet traces shows that up to 71% of flows contain suspicious behaviors indicative of low-volume network attacks such as port scans. However, distinguishing anomalous traffic in real time is challenging as each attack flow may comprise only a few packets.
  We extend prior work that tracks heavy hitter flows to also detect low-volume and slow attacks by combining the capabilities of both switches and SmartNICs. We flip the usual design approach by proposing an efficient filter data structure used to quickly route traffic marked as benign towards destination end-systems. We make careful use of limited programmable switch memory and pipeline stages, and complement them with SmartNIC resources to analyze the remaining traffic that may be anomalous. Using machine learning classifiers and intrusion detection rules deployed on the SmartNIC, we identify malicious source IPs, which then undergo more detailed forensics for attack mitigation. Finally, we develop a dataplane based protocol to rapidly coordinate data structure updates between these devices. We implement immUNITY in a testbed with Tofino v1 switch and Bluefield 3 SmartNIC, demonstrating its high accuracy, while minimizing traffic that's analyzed outside the switch.

</details>


### [122] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Next Generation of Multi-AP Coordinated Spatial Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20647)
*Ziru Chen,Salvatore Talarico,Qing Xia,Xihan Peng,Xing Hao,Lin X. Cai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对下一代Wi-Fi网络中的多接入点协调（MAPC）场景，提出了一种两层多臂老虎机（MAB）算法，用于优化协调空间复用（C-SR）网络，在提升总吞吐量的同时增强了公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在下一代Wi-Fi网络中，多接入点协调（MAPC）通过高级协调方案减少AP间的竞争和拥塞来提升网络性能。然而，当采用协调空间复用（C-SR）时，协调的AP需要相互调整调度策略、功率控制和链路自适应以满足特定的服务质量（QoS）要求。传统方法需要跨AP进行协商，导致高开销，并且需要复杂的解决方案来优化网络中的所有参数。

Method: 提出了一种两层多臂老虎机（MAB）算法来优化此类网络，同时保持所有节点之间资源的公平使用。该算法能够协调多AP间的调度策略、功率控制和链路自适应等参数。

Result: 系统级仿真验证了该方法的有效性。结果表明，所提出的算法不仅在网络总吞吐量方面有所提升，而且还能够增强公平性。

Conclusion: 所提出的两层MAB算法为下一代Wi-Fi网络提供了一个鲁棒的解决方案，能够有效优化多AP协调网络，在提升网络性能的同时保证资源使用的公平性，避免了传统方法的高开销和复杂性问题。

Abstract: In next generation of Wi-Fi networks Multiple Access Point Coordination (MAPC) is poised to significantly enhance the network performance by enabling a set of Access Points (APs) to coordinate with each other through advanced coordinating schemes so that to reduce inter-AP contention and congestion. This paper focuses on defining a framework to facilitate the coordination across multi-APs when these employ Coordinated Spatial Reuse (C-SR). In this case, the coordinating APs may need to reciprocally adjust their scheduling strategy, power control and link adaptation to meet specific Quality of Service (QoS) requirements, which by using classical approaches leads to high overhead due to negotiations needed across APs, and requires complex solutions in order to properly optimize the network across all the parameters in play. In this matter, a two layer Multi-Armed Bandit (MAB) algorithm has been proposed to optimize such a network while preserving the fair use of resources across all nodes. The validity of this holistic approach is confirmed by system level simulations, which show that the proposed algorithm not only improves the network in terms of sum-throughput, but also allows to enhance fairness, making this a robust solution for next-generation of Wi-Fi networks.

</details>


### [123] [NextSense: A Semi-Synthetic Sensing Data generation Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20789)
*David Rico Menéndez,Pablo Picazo-Martínez,Antonio de la Oliva,Carlos Jesús Bernardos,Chathura Sarathchandra,Alain Mourad*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了NextSense，一个开源、模块化的半合成数据生成平台，旨在为集成感知与通信(ISAC)应用解决真实数据采集成本高昂且难以获取的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的集成感知与通信(ISAC)应用需要海量数据，但在真实网络中采集此类数据集成本极高、耗时且往往因底层测量权限受限而不可行。

Method: 设计并实现NextSense平台，该平台由5G协议栈、信道仿真器和UE仿真器构成，支持用户通过API和GUI全自定义无线电配置、信道与移动性及流量特征，并输出符号级IQ样本、协议追踪和关键性能指标(KPI)等多视角数据。

Result: 详细描述了NextSense的系统架构，并验证了其作为真实测量数据忠实代理的能力，证明其能够有效支持感知用例。

Conclusion: NextSense平台为ISAC研究提供了一种低成本、高可定制的数据生成解决方案，能够替代难以获取的真实网络测量数据，促进相关技术的发展。

Abstract: Emerging integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) applications require large volumes of data, but collecting such datasets in real networks is costly, time consuming, and often infeasible due to limited access to low level measurements. In this paper we present NextSense, an open and modular semi-synthetic data generation platform that consists of a 5G stack, a channel emulator, and an UE emulator. The platform allows users full customization on radio configuration, channel and mobility, and traffic profiles through an API and GUI, and produces multi-perspective outputs that combine symbol-level IQ samples, protocol traces, and key performance indicators across UE, RAN, and CN. This paper describes the NextSense's architecture, and validates its ability to act as a faithful proxy for real measurements in sensing use cases.

</details>


### [124] [CollabORAN: A Collaborative rApp-xApp-dApp Control Architecture for Fairness-Adaptive Resource Sharing in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20805)
*Anastasios Giannopoulos,Sotirios Spantideas,Panagiotis Trakadas*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CollabORAN，一个协同的rApp-xApp-dApp分层框架，通过跨时间尺度的协调优化实现O-RAN系统中的动态频谱共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有O-RAN解决方案通常依赖孤立的控制环路，无法跨多个时间尺度联合优化端到端目标，同时在遵守控制环路延迟规范的情况下，将优化算法按时间尺度划分到O-RAN各层仍是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出CollabORAN分层框架，采用嵌套控制结构：rApp执行流量感知的策略生成，xApp通过基于超图的PRB着色执行干扰感知频谱分配，DU级dApp通过快速调度强制实现时间公平性，实现跨分钟、秒和毫秒时间尺度的协调优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，CollabORAN在密集和动态网络环境中显著改善了服务公平性，减少了用户饥饿现象，同时保持了高效的频谱复用。

Conclusion: CollabORAN通过跨时间尺度的端到端闭环设计，成功实现了O-RAN系统中的动态和公平频谱共享，为解决多时间尺度协调优化问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The evolution of Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) enables programmable and intelligent control of radio resources through disaggregated architectures and open interfaces. However, existing solutions typically rely on isolated control loops and fail to jointly address end-to-end optimization objectives across multiple timescales. Thus, it remains a key challenge to functionally split optimization algorithms across timescale-specific O-RAN layers while complying with control loop latency specifications. This article proposes CollabORAN, a collaborative rApp-xApp-dApp hierarchical framework for dynamic and equitable spectrum sharing in O-RAN systems. CollabORAN leverages a nested control structure in which the rApp performs traffic-aware policy generation, the xApp executes interference-aware spectrum allocation via hypergraph-based PRB coloring, and the DU-level dApp enforces temporal fairness through fast scheduling. The proposed end-to-end closed-loop design enables coordinated optimization across minutes, seconds, and millisecond time scales. Simulation results demonstrate that CollabORAN significantly improves service fairness and reduces user starvation while maintaining efficient spectrum reuse in dense and dynamic network environments.

</details>


### [125] [FLEX: Joint UL/DL and QoS-Aware Scheduling for Dynamic TDD in Industrial 5G and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20971)
*Leonard Kleinberger,Michael Gundall,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLEX，一种面向工业5G TDD网络的QoS感知调度器，通过动态调整UL/DL资源比例并引入下行缓冲区状态估计机制，在满足非对称流量需求和多样化QoS要求的同时，实现了与现有调度方案相近的吞吐量以及极低的时延开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工业5G TDD网络调度器依赖静态的UL/DL资源配置比例，无法适应工业4.0场景中动态且非对称的流量需求以及异构的QoS要求，导致资源分配效率低下。

Method: 提出FLEX调度器，在灵活TDD时隙中动态调整UL/DL比例；引入下行缓冲区状态估计机制，利用工业流量的确定性特征预测高优先级下行流量需求，防止下行流量饥饿并确保双向QoS优先级。

Result: 基于5G LENA和ns-3的仿真表明，FLEX在吞吐量上与现有调度方案相当，能够正确执行双向流量QoS优先级；对于确定性流量，FLEX保持极低的时延开销（小于一个时隙时长）。

Conclusion: FLEX调度器有效解决了工业5G TDD网络中静态资源配置的局限性，能够适应动态非对称流量并保障QoS，且时延极低，非常适合工业自动化应用场景。

Abstract: Industrial 5G deployments using Time Division Duplex (TDD) networks face a critical challenge: existing schedulers rely on static configuration of Uplink (UL) to Downlink (DL) resource ratios, failing to adapt to dynamic asymmetric traffic demands. This limitation is particularly problematic in Industry 4.0 scenarios where traffic patterns exhibit significant asymmetry between directions and heterogeneous Quality of Service (QoS) requirements. We present FLEX, a novel QoS-aware scheduler that dynamically adjusts the UL/DL ratio in flexible TDD slots while respecting diverse QoS requirements. FLEX introduces DL buffer state estimation to prevent starvation of high-priority DL traffic, exploiting the deterministic nature of industrial traffic patterns for accurate predictions. Through extensive simulations of industrial scenarios using 5G LENA and ns-3, we demonstrate that FLEX achieves similar throughput compared to established scheduling while correctly enforcing QoS priorities in both traffic directions. For deterministic traffic patterns, FLEX maintains minimal latency overhead (less than 1 slot duration), making it particularly suitable for industrial automation applications.

</details>


### [126] [OrbitStream: Training-Free Adaptive 360-degree Video Streaming via Semantic Potential Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20999)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Zhangfei Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OrbitStream，一种无需训练的360°视频流媒体框架，通过结合语义场景理解和控制理论，在保证可解释性的前提下实现了与深度学习方法相媲美的QoE性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的360°视频流媒体方法（数据驱动和深度强化学习）虽然能获得较高的QoE，但存在“黑盒”性质和对训练数据的依赖，限制了其在安全关键系统（如远程操作）中的部署。需要一种既保证高性能又具有可解释性和无需训练的方法。

Method: 提出OrbitStream框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 将视口预测建模为引力视口预测问题，利用语义物体生成势场来吸引用户注视；(2) 采用基于饱和的比例-微分控制器进行缓冲区调节，结合了语义场景理解和鲁棒控制理论。

Result: 在富含物体的远程操作轨迹上，OrbitStream实现了94.7%的零样本视口预测准确率（接近轨迹外推基线的98.5%）；在3600次蒙特卡洛仿真中，平均QoE为2.71，在12种算法中排名第二（接近最优BOLA-E的2.80，优于FastMPC的1.84）；平均决策延迟1.01毫秒，重缓冲事件极少。

Conclusion: OrbitStream证明了基于物理的控制方法结合语义建模能够为远程操作中的360°流媒体提供实用解决方案，在无需训练开销的情况下实现了具有竞争力的QoE和良好的可解释性。

Abstract: Adaptive 360° video streaming for teleoperation faces dual challenges: viewport prediction under uncertain gaze patterns and bitrate adaptation over volatile wireless channels. While data-driven and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) methods achieve high Quality of Experience (QoE), their "black-box" nature and reliance on training data can limit deployment in safety-critical systems. To address this, we propose OrbitStream, a training-free framework that combines semantic scene understanding with robust control theory. We formulate viewport prediction as a Gravitational Viewport Prediction (GVP) problem, where semantic objects generate potential fields that attract user gaze. Furthermore, we employ a Saturation-Based Proportional-Derivative (PD) Controller for buffer regulation. On object-rich teleoperation traces, OrbitStream achieves a 94.7\% zero-shot viewport prediction accuracy without user-specific profiling, approaching trajectory-extrapolation baselines ($\sim$98.5\%). Across 3,600 Monte Carlo simulations on diverse network traces, OrbitStream yields a mean QoE of 2.71. It ranks second among 12 evaluated algorithms, close to the top-performing BOLA-E (2.80) while outperforming FastMPC (1.84). The system exhibits an average decision latency of 1.01 ms with minimal rebuffering events. By providing competitive QoE with interpretability and zero training overhead, OrbitStream demonstrates that physics-based control, combined with semantic modeling, offers a practical solution for 360° streaming in teleoperation.

</details>


### [127] [AnyPro: Preference-Preserving Anycast Optimization based on Strategic AS-Path Prepending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21082)
*Minyuan Zhou,Yuning Chen,Jiaqi Zheng,Yifei Xu,Pan Hu,Yongping Tang,Wendong Yin,Jie Lin,Qingyan Yu,Yuanchao Su,Guihai Chen,Wanchun Dou,Songwu Lu,Wan Du*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AnyPro系统，这是首个利用AS路径预置（ASPP）技术优化大规模任播网络的系统，通过识别ASPP敏感客户端并构建约束优化问题，实现了客户端到站点的最优映射，将90分位延迟降低了37.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模任播网络运营中，由于域间路由的不透明性，客户端到站点的映射常常与运营商的预期不符，导致性能次优，现有方案难以有效解决这一问题。

Method: AnyPro首先采用高效的轮询机制识别所有对ASPP敏感的客户端，通过分析过程中的路由变化推导出一组ASPP约束条件，然后将任播优化问题形式化为基于约束的程序，计算全局最优的ASPP配置。

Result: 在包含20个PoP的全球测试平台上进行广泛评估，AnyPro相比无ASPP的基准配置将90分位延迟降低了37.7%，并且可以与PoP级别的任播优化技术集成以获得额外的性能提升。

Conclusion: AnyPro成功解锁了AS路径预置技术的全部潜力，能够大规模地将客户端引导至性能最优的站点，证明了ASPP技术在任播网络优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Operating large-scale anycast networks is challenging because client-to-site mappings often misalign with operator's expectation due to opaque inter-domain routing. We present AnyPro, the first system to unlock the full potential of AS-path prepending (ASPP), efficiently deriving globally optimal configurations to steer clients toward performance-optimal sites at scale. AnyPro first employs an efficient polling mechanism to identify all clients sensitive to ASPP. By analyzing the routing changes during the process, the system derives a set of ASPP constraints that guide client traffic toward the desired sites. We then formulate the anycast optimization problem as a constraint-based program and compute optimal ASPP configurations. Extensive evaluation on a global testbed with 20 PoPs demonstrates the effectiveness of AnyPro: it reduces the 90th percentile latency by 37.7% compared to baseline configurations without ASPP. Furthermore, we show that AnyPro can be integrated with PoP-level anycast optimization techniques to achieve additional performance gains.

</details>


### [128] [Generative Artificial Intelligence Assisted Multi-modal Semantic Extraction for NOMA-based Image Transmissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21092)
*Songhan Zhao,Shimin Gong,Bo Gu,Hongyang Du,Xidong Mu,Zehui Xiong,Yuming Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种生成式人工智能辅助的NOMA图像语义通信框架，通过联合优化语义特征选择与传输控制，并开发了重要性感知的模型驱动PPO框架，显著提升了语义恢复精度和传输时延性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语义通信系统中，语义特征提取与传输控制共同影响语义恢复性能和传输开销。如何联合优化语义层面的特征选择和传输层面的波束成形与解码顺序，以最大化传输时延和语义恢复准确性的加权和性能，是亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 提出了一种重要性感知与模型驱动的近端策略优化（IM-PPO）框架。该方法量化并保留高重要性语义特征以提升PPO学习效率，同时利用基于模型的优化方法自适应调整传输控制变量（接收波束成形和NOMA解码顺序），实现了语义特征选择与传输控制的联合优化。

Result: 数值实验结果表明，语义特征选择与传输控制的联合调整显著提升了语义恢复准确性和传输时延性能。与基准方法相比，IM-PPO框架有效利用了模型信息，显著提高了学习效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的GAI辅助语义通信框架及IM-PPO优化方法能够有效平衡语义恢复质量与传输效率，为语义通信系统的设计与优化提供了新的解决思路，具有重要的理论价值和应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a generative artificial intelligence (GAI)-assisted semantic communication framework for non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-based image transmissions. Semantic users (SUs) extract cross-modal semantic features from the raw images, which are then used for image recovery by leveraging a GAI model. The GAI enhances the generalization and recovery of semantic image transmissions, while NOMA efficiently allocates transmission capacities to SUs based on their traffic demands. Thus, the semantic extraction and transmission control jointly affect both semantic recovery performance and transmission overhead. We maximize a weighted performance of transmission latency and semantic recovery accuracy by jointly optimizing the semantic feature selection at the semantic level, as well as the receive beamforming and NOMA decoding order at the transmission level. To reduce potential redundancy in semantic features and improve optimization efficiency, we develop an importance-aware and model-driven proximal policy optimization (IM-PPO) framework. Specifically, we quantify and retain high-importance semantic features to enhance the learning efficiency of PPO, while model-based optimization methods are used to adapt the transmission control variables. Numerical results validate that the joint adjustment of the semantic feature selection and the transmission control significantly improves the semantic recovery accuracy and the transmission latency performance. Moreover, the IM-PPO framework effectively leverages the model information to improve the learning efficiency compared to benchmark methods.

</details>


### [129] [DRL-driven Online Optimization for Joint Traffic Reshaping and Channel Reconfiguration in RIS-assisted Semantic NOMA Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21093)
*Songhan Zhao,Shimin Gong,Bo Gu,Zehui Xiong,Ping Wang,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了RIS辅助的语义感知无线网络，提出了一种可延迟语义提取方案和DRL驱动的在线优化框架，通过联合优化RIS波束成形、语义提取和NOMA解码顺序，显著提升了长期能效并降低了计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RIS辅助的语义感知无线网络中，多个语义用户通过NOMA方式传输语义信息。由于涉及多个耦合控制变量（RIS被动波束成形、语义提取、NOMA解码顺序），在时变网络环境中进行长期资源分配面临巨大的计算开销，需要低复杂度的在线优化方案。

Method: 提出可延迟语义提取方案，将语义提取任务分散到多个时隙；建立长期能效最大化问题，联合优化RIS被动波束成形、语义用户语义提取和NOMA解码顺序；开发深度强化学习(DRL)驱动的在线优化框架，根据动态网络状态自适应选择和优化流量重塑、信道重配置或NOMA解码顺序分配。

Result: 数值结果表明，可延迟语义提取方案显著提升了长期能效；DRL驱动的在线优化框架在保持优越学习性能的同时，相比最先进方法有效减少了运行时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的方案成功解决了RIS辅助语义感知网络中长期资源分配的计算复杂度问题，实现了能效提升和低复杂度在线优化的双重目标，为语义通信与智能反射面技术的融合应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper explores a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted and semantic-aware wireless network, where multiple semantic users (SUs) transmit semantic information to an access point (AP) using the non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) method. The RIS reconfigures channel conditions, while semantic extraction reshapes traffic demands, providing enhanced control flexibility for NOMA transmissions. To enable efficient long-term resource allocation, we propose a deferrable semantic extraction scheme that can distribute the semantic extraction tasks across multiple time slots. We formulate a long-term energy efficiency maximization problem by jointly optimizing the RIS's passive beamforming, the SUs' semantic extraction, and the NOMA decoding order. Note that this problem involves multiple and coupled control variables, which can incur significant computational overhead in time-varying network environments. To support low-complexity online optimization, a deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-driven online optimization framework is developed. Specifically, the DRL module facilitates the adaptive selection and optimization of the most suitable option from traffic reshaping, channel reconfiguration, or NOMA decoding order assignment based on the dynamic network status. Numerical results demonstrate that the deferrable semantic extraction scheme significantly improves the long-term energy efficiency. Meanwhile, the DRL-driven online optimization framework effectively reduces the running time while maintaining superior learning performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [130] [Learning to Optimize Joint Source and RIS-assisted Channel Encoding for Multi-User Semantic Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21097)
*Haidong Wang,Songhan Zhao,Bo Gu,Shimin Gong,Hongyang Du,Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出JSRE框架和T-DRL框架，通过联合优化用户调度、RIS相移和语义压缩比，并引入语义相似度估计器和语义模型缓存机制，显著提升了多用户语义通信系统的能量效率和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多用户语义通信系统中，需要最大化整体能量效率，但传统的深度强化学习方法在评估语义相似度时依赖大量真实环境交互，导致训练计算开销过大。此外，用户调度策略与语义模型配置紧密耦合，存在冗余重训问题，亟需高效解决方案。

Method: 提出联合信源与RIS辅助信道编码（JSRE）框架，利用DNN提取语义特征，RIS提供信道正交性。针对计算开销问题，提出截断深度强化学习（T-DRL）框架，开发DNN语义相似度估计器快速估计相似度分数。进一步提出语义模型缓存机制，存储并复用不同调度决策对应的微调语义模型，并在DRL框架中采用Transformer演员网络动态生成条件动作空间。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提JSRE框架在系统能量效率方面显著优于基准方法；T-DRL框架通过训练更少的语义模型，显著提升了学习效率，加速了收敛过程。

Conclusion: 该研究为多用户语义通信提供了一种高效节能的统一语义编码-解码设计范式，所提方法有效降低了训练开销，推动了语义通信在实际应用中的落地。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore a joint source and reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted channel encoding (JSRE) framework for multi-user semantic communications, where a deep neural network (DNN) extracts semantic features for all users and the RIS provides channel orthogonality, enabling a unified semantic encoding-decoding design. We aim to maximize the overall energy efficiency of semantic communications across all users by jointly optimizing the user scheduling, the RIS's phase shifts, and the semantic compression ratio. Although this joint optimization problem can be addressed using conventional deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods, evaluating semantic similarity typically relies on extensive real environment interactions, which can incur heavy computational overhead during training. To address this challenge, we propose a truncated DRL (T-DRL) framework, where a DNN-based semantic similarity estimator is developed to rapidly estimate the similarity score. Moreover, the user scheduling strategy is tightly coupled with the semantic model configuration. To exploit this relationship, we further propose a semantic model caching mechanism that stores and reuses fine-tuned semantic models corresponding to different scheduling decisions. A Transformer-based actor network is employed within the DRL framework to dynamically generate action space conditioned on the current caching state. This avoids redundant retraining and further accelerates the convergence of the learning process. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed JSRE framework significantly improves the system energy efficiency compared with the baseline methods. By training fewer semantic models, the proposed T-DRL framework significantly enhances the learning efficiency.

</details>


### [131] [A lightweight Outlier Detection for Characterizing Radio- and Environment-Specific Link Quality Fluctuation in Low-Power Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21107)
*Zegeye Mekasha Kidane,Waltenegus Dargie*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实地实验，系统分析了外部环境和内部制造缺陷对低功耗无线传感网络链路质量的影响，并提出了一种轻量级的统计异常检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低功耗无线传感网络的性能受外部环境因素和内部制造公差影响，理解这些影响的条件和程度对于实现高性能、高能效以及进行环境和无线电特定配置至关重要。

Method: 在超过15种不同的物理环境（包括河流、湖泊、桥梁、森林和花园）中进行广泛的实际部署和实验，测试四种广泛使用的异构低功耗无线电设备。提出了一种轻量级的统计异常检测技术，其所有参数都根据原始和预测的链路质量指标（RSSI）的统计特性进行定义。

Result: 通过实验揭示了外部和内部因素对低功耗无线传感器网络链路质量的影响程度，验证了所提出的异常检测技术的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为理解和改善低功耗无线传感网络在不同环境条件下的链路质量提供了重要见解，所提出的轻量级异常检测方法为实际部署中的链路质量监控提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The performance of low-power wireless sensing networks can be influenced by both external environmental factors and internal imperfections which often arise due to manufacturing tolerance during mass production. Understanding the conditions and extent of these influences is important not only to achieve high performance and high energy efficiency, but also to carry our environment and radio specific configurations. In this paper we demonstrate, through extensive practical deployments and experiments, the extent to which external and internal factors affect the link quality of low-power wireless sensor networks. Moreover, we propose a lightweight statistical outlier detection technique and define all the parameter thereof in terms of the statistics of both the raw and the predicted link quality metrics (RSSI). Our study considers more than 15 different physical environments consisting of rivers, lakes, bridges, forests, and gardens, as well as four widely employed heterogeneous low-power radios.

</details>


### [132] [WirelessBench: A Tolerance-Aware LLM Agent Benchmark for Wireless Network Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21251)
*Jingwen Tong,Fang Liu,Linkai Xv,Shiliang Lu,Kangqi Li,Yiqian Zhang,Yijie Song,Zeyang Xue,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了首个面向LLM无线网络代理的容错感知、工具集成基准测试WirelessBench，包含三层认知层级共3,392个测试项，通过容错评分、工具必要任务和CoT可追溯设计，揭示了传统评估方法无法发现的级联失败和灾难性单位混淆错误（占错误的23%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线网络基准测试仅评估单一孤立能力且对所有错误同等对待，忽略了级联链式失败和灾难性单位混淆错误（如dB与dBm混淆），无法反映真实工程风险，迫切需要能够可靠部署LLM代理的基准测试。

Method: 构建三层认知层级基准：领域知识推理(WCHW, 1,392项)、意图驱动资源分配(WCNS, 1,000项)、移动场景下前瞻性多步决策(WCMSA, 1,000项)；采用三大设计原则：(i)容错感知评分与灾难性错误检测；(ii)需要3GPP合规射线追踪查询的工具必要任务；(iii)每个测试项配备完整CoT轨迹以实现细粒度诊断。

Result: 直接提示模型GPT-4o得分68%，工具集成代理得分84.64%（领先16.64个百分点）；23%的错误为精确匹配指标无法识别的灾难性失败；层级结构将错误分解为四个可操作的诊断类别，揭示了平坦评估无法展现的问题。

Conclusion: WirelessBench成功填补了无线网络LLM代理可靠部署评估的空白，其容错感知和层级设计能够揭示传统评估遗漏的关键工程风险，为LLM代理在无线网络管理中的实际部署提供了可靠的评估框架和诊断工具。

Abstract: LLM agents are emerging as a key enabler for autonomous wireless network management. Reliably deploying them, however, demands benchmarks that reflect real engineering risk. Existing wireless benchmarks evaluate single isolated capabilities and treat all errors uniformly, missing both cascaded-chain failures and catastrophic unit confusions (\textit{e.g.}, dB vs.\ dBm). We present \wb{}, the first tolerance-aware, tool-integrated benchmark for LLM-based wireless agents. \wb{} is organized as a three-tier cognitive hierarchy: domain knowledge reasoning (WCHW, 1{,}392 items), intent-driven resource allocation (WCNS, 1{,}000 items), and proactive multi-step decisions under mobility (WCMSA, 1{,}000 items). Moreover, \wb{} is established on three design principles: \emph{(i)}~tolerance-aware scoring with catastrophic-error detection; \emph{(ii)}~tool-necessary tasks requiring a 3GPP-compliant ray-tracing query for channel quality; and \emph{(iii)}~Chain-of-Thought (CoT)-traceable items, where every benchmark item ships with a complete CoT trajectory enabling fine-grained diagnosis of where in the reasoning chain an agent fails. Our numerical results show that the direct-prompting model (GPT-4o) scores $68\%$, trailing a tool-integrated agent ($84.64\%$) by $16.64$\,pp; $23\%$ of errors are catastrophic failures invisible to exact-match metrics. More importantly, the hierarchy decomposes errors into four actionable diagnostic categories that flat evaluation cannot reveal. Code and data: https://wirelessbench.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [GateANN: I/O-Efficient Filtered Vector Search on SSDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21466)
*Nakyung Lee,Soobin Cho,Jiwoong Park,Gyuyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出了GateANN，一个高效的SSD图ANNS系统，支持在未修改的图索引上进行过滤向量搜索，通过解耦图遍历与向量检索，引入图隧道技术，减少SSD读取最多10倍，吞吐量提升最多7.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SSD图向量检索系统在支持过滤查询时存在两难：后过滤造成大量无效I/O浪费；支持过滤感知则需要昂贵的索引重建。如何在无需重建索引的前提下，高效支持过滤向量搜索是核心动机。

Method: GateANN将图遍历与向量检索解耦。核心观察是遍历节点仅需邻居列表和近似距离，不依赖全精度向量。提出图隧道（graph tunneling）技术：在内存中检查节点过滤谓词，仅在匹配时发起I/O读取向量，对不匹配节点在内存中直接路由绕过，保持图连通性的同时避免无效SSD读取。

Result: 实验表明，GateANN相比现有方法可减少SSD读取最多10倍，系统吞吐量提升最多7.6倍。

Conclusion: GateANN成功实现了在未修改图索引上高效支持过滤向量搜索，通过解耦遍历与检索、引入图隧道技术，有效解决了I/O浪费与索引重建的矛盾，显著提升了SSD图ANNS系统的性能。

Abstract: We present GateANN, an I/O-efficient SSD-based graph ANNS system that supports filtered vector search on an unmodified graph index. Existing SSD-based systems either waste I/O by post-filtering, or require expensive filter-aware index rebuilds. GateANN avoids both by decoupling graph traversal from vector retrieval. Our key insight is that traversing a node requires only its neighbor list and an approximate distance, neither of which needs the full-precision vector on SSD. Based on this, GateANN introduces graph tunneling. It checks each node's filter predicate in memory before issuing I/O and routes through non-matching nodes entirely in memory, preserving graph connectivity without any SSD read for non-matching nodes. Our experimental results show that it reduces SSD reads by up to 10x and improves throughput by up to 7.6x.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [JointFM-0.1: A Foundation Model for Multi-Target Joint Distributional Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20266)
*Stefan Hackmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了JointFM，这是首个用于耦合时间序列分布预测的基础模型，通过在合成SDE数据上训练，无需针对特定任务进行校准或微调，在零样本设置下将能量损失降低了14.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工智能快速发展，随机微分方程（SDE）仍是建模不确定性系统的标准范式，但在实际应用中面临建模风险高、校准脆弱、高保真仿真计算成本高等挑战。

Method: 反转传统范式，不再将SDE拟合到数据，而是通过采样无限的合成SDE流来训练一个通用模型，直接预测未来的联合概率分布。

Result: JointFM作为首个耦合时间序列分布预测的基础模型，无需针对特定任务校准或微调，在纯零样本设置下，恢复未见合成SDE生成的oracle联合分布时，能量损失相比最强基线降低14.2%。

Conclusion: JointFM成功建立了分布预测的新范式，证明了基础模型在时间序列联合分布预测任务中的有效性，为解决SDE实际应用中的挑战提供了新思路。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancements in Artificial Intelligence (AI), Stochastic Differential Equations (SDEs) remain the gold-standard formalism for modeling systems under uncertainty. However, applying SDEs in practice is fraught with challenges: modeling risk is high, calibration is often brittle, and high-fidelity simulations are computationally expensive. This technical report introduces JointFM, a foundation model that inverts this paradigm. Instead of fitting SDEs to data, we sample an infinite stream of synthetic SDEs to train a generic model to predict future joint probability distributions directly. This approach establishes JointFM as the first foundation model for distributional predictions of coupled time series - requiring no task-specific calibration or finetuning. Despite operating in a purely zero-shot setting, JointFM reduces the energy loss by 14.2% relative to the strongest baseline when recovering oracle joint distributions generated by unseen synthetic SDEs.

</details>


### [135] [MARLIN: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Incremental DAG Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20295)
*Dong Li,Zhengzhang Chen,Xujiang Zhao,Linlin Yu,Zhong Chen,Yi He,Haifeng Chen,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARLIN，一种基于多智能体强化学习的高效增量DAG学习方法，通过创新的策略设计和框架结构，在保持有效性的同时显著提升了因果结构发现的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的因果结构发现方法虽然能够从观测数据中识别有向无环图(DAG)，但普遍存在效率不足的问题，难以满足在线应用的需求，因此迫切需要开发更高效的因果发现方法。

Method: 提出MARLIN框架，采用DAG生成策略将连续实值空间映射到DAG空间作为批次内策略；引入两个强化学习智能体（状态特定和状态不变）来揭示因果关系；将其整合到增量学习框架中；利用分解动作空间来提高并行化效率。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，MARLIN在效率和有效性两个维度上均优于现有最先进的方法。

Conclusion: MARLIN成功地将多智能体强化学习与增量学习相结合，通过创新的策略设计和框架优化，实现了高效且有效的因果结构发现，为在线因果发现应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Uncovering causal structures from observational data is crucial for understanding complex systems and making informed decisions. While reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in identifying these structures in the form of a directed acyclic graph (DAG), existing methods often lack efficiency, making them unsuitable for online applications. In this paper, we propose MARLIN, an efficient multi agent RL based approach for incremental DAG learning. MARLIN uses a DAG generation policy that maps a continuous real valued space to the DAG space as an intra batch strategy, then incorporates two RL agents state specific and state invariant to uncover causal relationships and integrates these agents into an incremental learning framework. Furthermore, the framework leverages a factored action space to enhance parallelization efficiency. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that MARLIN outperforms state of the art methods in terms of both efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [136] [Collaborative Adaptive Curriculum for Progressive Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20296)
*Jing Liu,Zhenchao Ma,Han Yu,Bobo Ju,Wenliang Yang,Chengfang Li,Bo Hu,Liang Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAPD框架，通过基于PCA的知识分层和基于共识的课程进阶机制，解决了联邦蒸馏中教师知识复杂度与客户端能力不匹配的问题，实现了更高的精度和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的协同知识蒸馏方法在资源受限的分布式场景中面临一个根本性矛盾：教师模型的高维知识复杂度与异构客户端的学习能力不匹配，这限制了其在边缘视觉分析系统中的部署。

Method: 提出联邦自适应渐进蒸馏（FAPD）框架。首先，利用PCA对教师特征进行分层结构化分解，按方差贡献排序建立视觉知识层级；随后，客户端通过维度自适应投影矩阵逐步接收复杂度递增的知识；同时，服务器通过时间共识窗口监测全网学习稳定性，仅在达成集体共识时推进课程维度。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明：FAPD在CIFAR-10上相比FedAvg精度提升3.64%，收敛速度提升2倍；在极端数据异构（α=0.1）条件下，性能超越基线模型4.5%以上。

Conclusion: FAPD成功实现了知识转移节奏的自适应调节，在保证收敛性能的同时有效应对了客户端能力异构问题，为边缘场景下的联邦蒸馏提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in collaborative knowledge distillation have demonstrated cutting-edge performance for resource-constrained distributed multimedia learning scenarios. However, achieving such competitiveness requires addressing a fundamental mismatch: high-dimensional teacher knowledge complexity versus heterogeneous client learning capacities, which currently prohibits deployment in edge-based visual analytics systems. Drawing inspiration from curriculum learning principles, we introduce Federated Adaptive Progressive Distillation (FAPD), a consensus-driven framework that orchestrates adaptive knowledge transfer. FAPD hierarchically decomposes teacher features via PCA-based structuring, extracting principal components ordered by variance contribution to establish a natural visual knowledge hierarchy. Clients progressively receive knowledge of increasing complexity through dimension-adaptive projection matrices. Meanwhile, the server monitors network-wide learning stability by tracking global accuracy fluctuations across a temporal consensus window, advancing curriculum dimensionality only when collective consensus emerges. Consequently, FAPD provably adapts knowledge transfer pace while achieving superior convergence over fixed-complexity approaches. Extensive experiments on three datasets validate FAPD's effectiveness: it attains 3.64% accuracy improvement over FedAvg on CIFAR-10, demonstrates 2x faster convergence, and maintains robust performance under extreme data heterogeneity (α=0.1), outperforming baselines by over 4.5%.

</details>


### [137] [Transformer-Based Predictive Maintenance for Risk-Aware Instrument Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20297)
*Adithya Parthasarathy,Aswathnarayan Muthukrishnan Kirubakaran,Akshay Deshpande,Ram Sekhar Bodala,Suhas Malempati,Nachiappan Chockalingam,Vinoth Punniyamoorthy,Seema Gangaiah Aarella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将仪器校准调度建模为预测性维护问题，通过预测漂移时间（TTD）来实现主动干预。基于改进的NASA C-MAPSS数据集，Transformer模型在TTD预测上表现最佳，结合不确定性感知的策略能显著降低违规风险和校准成本，证明了基于状态的校准优于传统的固定周期校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的固定周期校准计划易于管理，但忽略了仪器在不同工况下漂移速率的差异，导致效率低下或存在风险。本文旨在解决如何利用传感器历史数据预测仪器漂移时间（TTD），从而实现基于状态的预测性校准调度，在违规发生前进行干预，以提高校准的准确性和经济性。

Method: 1. 将NASA C-MAPSS基准数据集适配为校准场景，筛选对漂移敏感的传感器，定义虚拟校准阈值，并插入合成重置事件以模拟重复校准过程。
2. 比较多种模型用于TTD预测，包括经典回归器、循环神经网络（RNN）、卷积序列模型以及一个紧凑型Transformer。
3. 引入基于分位数的不确定性模型，以在漂移行为噪声较大时支持保守的调度决策。
4. 在违反感知成本模型下，评估预测性调度策略相对于被动和固定策略的经济效益。

Result: 1. Transformer模型在主要数据子集FD001上提供了最强的点预测性能，并在更复杂的FD002-FD004子集上保持竞争力。
2. 在考虑违规惩罚的成本模型下，预测性调度比被动和固定策略成本更低。
3. 当点预测可靠性降低时，不确定性感知的触发机制能大幅减少违规事件的发生。

Conclusion: 基于状态的校准可以有效地被构建为一个联合预测与决策问题。将序列模型（特别是Transformer）与风险感知决策策略相结合，是实现更智能、更经济且更可靠的校准规划的有效途径。

Abstract: Accurate calibration is essential for instruments whose measurements must remain traceable, reliable, and compliant over long operating periods. Fixed-interval programs are easy to administer, but they ignore that instruments drift at different rates under different conditions. This paper studies calibration scheduling as a predictive maintenance problem: given recent sensor histories, estimate time-to-drift (TTD) and intervene before a violation occurs. We adapt the NASA C-MAPSS benchmark into a calibration setting by selecting drift-sensitive sensors, defining virtual calibration thresholds, and inserting synthetic reset events that emulate repeated recalibration. We then compare classical regressors, recurrent and convolutional sequence models, and a compact Transformer for TTD prediction. The Transformer provides the strongest point forecasts on the primary FD001 split and remains competitive on the harder FD002--FD004 splits, while a quantile-based uncertainty model supports conservative scheduling when drift behavior is noisier. Under a violation-aware cost model, predictive scheduling lowers cost relative to reactive and fixed policies, and uncertainty-aware triggers sharply reduce violations when point forecasts are less reliable. The results show that condition-based calibration can be framed as a joint forecasting and decision problem, and that combining sequence models with risk-aware policies is a practical route toward smarter calibration planning.

</details>


### [138] [Rolling-Origin Validation Reverses Model Rankings in Multi-Step PM10 Forecasting: XGBoost, SARIMA, and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20315)
*Federico Garcia Crespi,Eduardo Yubero Funes,Marina Alfosea Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 静态评估会高估机器学习模型的实际预测能力；在滚动更新评估下，XGBoost在短中期预测中并不优于持久性基准，而SARIMA表现更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多空气质量预测研究声称机器学习优于传统方法，但评估通常采用静态时间划分并忽略持久性基准，导致在常规更新的实际操作环境中，机器学习方法的真实增益不明确。

Method: 基于南欧某城市背景监测站2017至2024年间2350个日PM10观测数据，对比XGBoost与SARIMA模型。采用两种评估协议：静态划分与滚动原点协议（逐月更新），以持久性模型为基准，报告不同预测时效的技能分数及可预测性视界。

Result: 静态评估显示XGBoost在1至7天预测时效表现良好。但滚动原点评估改变了排名：XGBoost在短期和中期预测上并不始终优于持久性基准；SARIMA则在全预测时效范围内均保持正向技能，表现更加稳健可靠。

Conclusion: 静态时间划分评估可能夸大模型的操作价值并改变方法排名。对研究者而言，应采用滚动原点评估以反映真实性能；对实践者而言，滚动原点且参考持久性的技能分布图能清晰展示各方法在不同预测时效的可靠性。

Abstract: (a) Many air quality forecasting studies report gains from machine learning, but evaluations often use static chronological splits and omit persistence baselines, so the operational added value under routine updating is unclear.
  (b) Using 2,350 daily PM10 observations from 2017 to 2024 at an urban background monitoring station in southern Europe, we compare XGBoost and SARIMA against persistence under a static split and a rolling-origin protocol with monthly updates. We report horizon-specific skill and the predictability horizon, defined as the maximum horizon with positive persistence-relative skill. Static evaluation suggests XGBoost performs well from one to seven days ahead, but rolling-origin evaluation reverses rankings: XGBoost is not consistently better than persistence at short and intermediate horizons, whereas SARIMA remains positively skilled across the full range.
  (c) For researchers, static splits can overstate operational usefulness and change rankings. For practitioners, rolling-origin, persistence-referenced skill profiles show which methods stay reliable at each lead time.

</details>


### [139] [Probing the Latent World: Emergent Discrete Symbols and Physical Structure in Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20327)
*Liu hung ming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIM框架作为被动量化探针，将V-JEPA 2的连续潜在向量转换为离散符号序列，证明冻结的JEPA潜在空间中存在可发现的结构化符号流形，且动作类别共享公共表示核心，语义差异以分级分布变化而非类别边界编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于联合嵌入预测架构的视频世界模型通过预测潜在空间的掩码区域而非重建像素来获取丰富的时空表征，但这移除了生成模型的视觉验证路径，造成了结构可解释性鸿沟：编码器学习到的物理结构无法以任何可检查的形式获取。现有探测方法要么在没有结构化中间层的连续空间中操作，要么附加生成组件，其参数会混淆对编码器行为的归因。

Method: 提出AI Mother Tongue (AIM)框架作为被动量化探针：一种轻量级、无词汇表的探针，将V-JEPA 2连续潜在向量转换为离散符号序列，无需任务特定监督或修改编码器。编码器保持完全冻结，因此AIM码本中的任何符号结构完全归因于V-JEPA 2预训练表征，而非探针本身。

Result: 在Kinetics-mini数据集上进行类别对比实验，评估三个物理维度：抓取角度、物体几何形状和运动时序结构。AIM符号分布在三个实验中均存在显著差异（卡方检验p<10^-4；互信息0.036-0.117比特，标准化互信息为3比特最大值的1.2-3.9%；JSD高达0.342；码本活跃率62.5%）。实验揭示V-JEPA 2潜在空间显著紧凑：不同动作类别共享公共表示核心，语义差异以分级分布变化而非类别边界编码。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了通向动作条件符号世界模型四阶段路线图的第一阶段，证明了结构化符号流形是冻结JEPA潜在空间的可发现属性。

Abstract: Video world models trained with Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) acquire rich spatiotemporal representations by predicting masked regions in latent space rather than reconstructing pixels. This removes the visual verification pathway of generative models, creating a structural interpretability gap: the encoder has learned physical structure inaccessible in any inspectable form. Existing probing methods either operate in continuous space without a structured intermediate layer, or attach generative components whose parameters confound attribution of behavior to the encoder.
  We propose the AI Mother Tongue (AIM) framework as a passive quantization probe: a lightweight, vocabulary-free probe that converts V-JEPA 2 continuous latent vectors into discrete symbol sequences without task-specific supervision or modifying the encoder. Because the encoder is kept completely frozen, any symbolic structure in the AIM codebook is attributable entirely to V-JEPA 2 pre-trained representations -- not to the probe.
  We evaluate through category-contrast experiments on Kinetics-mini along three physical dimensions: grasp angle, object geometry, and motion temporal structure. AIM symbol distributions differ significantly across all three experiments (chi^2 p < 10^{-4}; MI 0.036--0.117 bits, NMI 1.2--3.9% of the 3-bit maximum; JSD up to 0.342; codebook active ratio 62.5%). The experiments reveal that V-JEPA 2 latent space is markedly compact: diverse action categories share a common representational core, with semantic differences encoded as graded distributional variations rather than categorical boundaries. These results establish Stage 1 of a four-stage roadmap toward an action-conditioned symbolic world model, demonstrating that structured symbolic manifolds are discoverable properties of frozen JEPA latent spaces.

</details>


### [140] [Bounded Coupled AI Learning Dynamics in Tri-Hierarchical Drone Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20333)
*Oleksii Bychkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对三层异构时序多智能体系统（Hebbian在线学习、MARL、元学习），给出了确保系统总误差有界、表征漂移受限、元层兼容以及误差不累积的四个形式化定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自主多智能体系统融合了不同时间尺度的异构学习机制，这些机制耦合后能否被形式化保证其动力学始终处于可允许的运行域内，仍是一个未解的问题。本文旨在解决这一开放性问题。

Method: 研究一个三层层级化的群体学习系统：个体层面的Hebbian在线学习（快时标）、群体层面的多智能体强化学习MARL（中时标）、策略层面的元学习MAML（慢时标），在契约式学习率约束、层级间映射的Lipschitz连续性及权重稳定化条件下，分析其耦合动力学。

Result: 证明了四个定理：(1) 有界总误差定理：在给定条件下，总次优性具有与时间无关的分量上界；(2) 有界表征漂移定理：给出了Hebbian更新在一个MARL周期内对协调层嵌入影响的最坏估计；(3) 元层兼容性定理：给出了策略适应保持下层不变量的充分条件；(4) 非累积定理：证明了误差不会随时间无界增长。

Conclusion: 本文为多时间尺度异构学习机制的耦合系统建立了形式化安全边界，证明在适当条件下系统可维持在可允许运行域内，为复杂自主系统的理论分析与设计提供了基础保障。

Abstract: Modern autonomous multi-agent systems combine heterogeneous learning mechanisms operating at different timescales. An open question remains: can one formally guarantee that coupled dynamics of such mechanisms stay within the admissible operational regime? This paper studies a tri-hierarchical swarm learning system where three mechanisms act simultaneously: (1) local Hebbian online learning at individual agent level (fast timescale, 10-100 ms); (2) multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) for tactical group coordination (medium timescale, 1-10 s); (3) meta-learning (MAML) for strategic adaptation (slow timescale, 10-100 s). Four results are established. The Bounded Total Error Theorem shows that under contractual constraints on learning rates, Lipschitz continuity of inter-level mappings, and weight stabilization, total suboptimality admits a component-wise upper bound uniform in time. The Bounded Representation Drift Theorem gives a worst-case estimate of how Hebbian updates affect coordination-level embeddings during one MARL cycle. The Meta-Level Compatibility Theorem provides sufficient conditions under which strategic adaptation preserves lower-level invariants. The Non-Accumulation Theorem proves that error does not grow unboundedly over time.

</details>


### [141] [Hybrid Autoencoder-Isolation Forest approach for time series anomaly detection in C70XP cyclotron operation data at ARRONAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20335)
*F Basbous,F Poirier,F Haddad,D Mateus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合自编码器（AE）和孤立森林（IF）的混合方法，用于加速器传感器数据的早期异常检测，有效解决了传统IF难以检测细微异常的问题，实验验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用于医用放射性同位素生产的C70XP回旋加速器系统复杂且昂贵，容易发生故障导致运行中断，因此需要开发一种能够实现早期异常检测的方法以提升系统性能和可靠性。

Method: 提出一种混合方法，将全连接自编码器（AE）与孤立森林（IF）相结合。具体而言，利用AE重构传感器数据并计算平均立方误差（MCE），将其作为IF模型的输入特征，从而增强对细微异常（特别是位于正常数据均值附近的异常）的检测能力。

Result: 在质子束强度时间序列数据上的验证结果表明，所提出的AE-IF混合方法在异常检测性能上相比传统IF方法有显著提升，能够有效检测出细微异常。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了传统孤立森林因依赖轴平行分割而难以检测细微异常的问题，为回旋加速器等复杂系统的早期异常检测提供了一种有效的机器学习解决方案。

Abstract: The Interest Public Group ARRONAX's C70XP cyclotron, used for radioisotope production for medical and research applications, relies on complex and costly systems that are prone to failures, leading to operational disruptions. In this context, this study aims to develop a machine learning-based method for early anomaly detection, from sensor measurements over a temporal window, to enhance system performance. One of the most widely recognized methods for anomaly detection is Isolation Forest (IF), known for its effectiveness and scalability. However, its reliance on axis-parallel splits limits its ability to detect subtle anomalies, especially those occurring near the mean of normal data. This study proposes a hybrid approach that combines a fully connected Autoencoder (AE) with IF to enhance the detection of subtle anomalies. In particular, the Mean Cubic Error (MCE) of the sensor data reconstructed by the AE is used as input to the IF model. Validated on proton beam intensity time series data, the proposed method demonstrates a clear improvement in detection performance, as confirmed by the experimental results.

</details>


### [142] [Graph-Aware Text-Only Backdoor Poisoning for Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20339)
*Qi Luo,Minghui Xu,Dongxiao Yu,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TAGBD，一种针对文本属性图的纯文本后门攻击方法。攻击者仅修改节点文本即可在训练数据中植入后门，实验表明该攻击高效、可迁移且能抵抗常见防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图学习系统常使用包含文本属性的节点数据（如论文摘要、用户帖子），这些文本多来自开放平台，易被攻击者进行数据投毒。现有研究多关注图结构攻击，忽略了仅修改节点文本这一隐蔽且现实的攻击向量，因此需要研究文本属性图在纯文本修改场景下的安全风险。

Method: 提出TAGBD（Text-Attributed Graph Backdoor Attack）攻击框架：首先识别易受影响的训练节点；然后利用影子图模型生成外观自然的触发器文本；最后通过替换原文本或追加短语的方式注入触发器，整个过程不改变图的拓扑结构。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明：该攻击具有极高的攻击成功率；攻击可在不同图神经网络模型间迁移；在常见防御机制下仍保持较强的攻击效果。

Conclusion: 仅修改节点文本是图学习系统中一个可行且高效的攻击渠道，现有防御措施不足。未来的防御策略需要同时审查图链接和节点内容，以应对此类文本后门攻击。

Abstract: Many learning systems now use graph data in which each node also contains text, such as papers with abstracts or users with posts. Because these texts often come from open platforms, an attacker may be able to quietly poison a small part of the training data and later make the model produce wrong predictions on demand. This paper studies that risk in a realistic setting where the attacker edits only node text and does not change the graph structure. We propose TAGBD, a text-only backdoor attack for text-attributed graphs. TAGBD first finds training nodes that are easier to influence, then generates natural-looking trigger text with the help of a shadow graph model, and finally injects the trigger by either replacing the original text or appending a short phrase. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that the attack is highly effective, transfers across different graph models, and remains strong under common defenses. These results demonstrate that text alone is a practical attack channel in graph learning systems and suggest that future defenses should inspect both graph links and node content.

</details>


### [143] [Interpretable Multiple Myeloma Prognosis with Observational Medical Outcomes Partnership Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20341)
*Salma Rachidi,Aso Bozorgpanah,Eric Fey,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种新型正则化技术，通过约束模型预测与可解释基线的一致性，在多发性骨髓瘤患者五年生存率预测任务中实现了可解释性与性能的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在医疗决策中的不透明行为限制了其在临床实践中的采用。尽管机器学习有望改善临床决策，但缺乏可解释性阻碍了其在医疗健康领域的应用。

Method: 针对多发性骨髓瘤患者的五年生存率预测任务，提出两种正则化惩罚项构造方法：第一种惩罚模型预测与仅含两个人工选择特征的可解释逻辑回归模型的预测偏差；第二种要求模型预测与修订国际分期系统（R-ISS）保持一致性。使用赫尔辛基大学医院的812名患者临床数据进行实验验证。

Result: 在测试集上达到最高0.721的准确率；SHAP值分析表明模型确实依赖于所选择的重要特征，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的正则化技术能够有效保证机器学习模型的可解释性，同时维持较好的预测性能，为医疗健康领域中可解释机器学习的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) promises better clinical decision-making, yet opaque model behavior limits the adoption in healthcare. We propose two novel regularization techniques for ensuring the interpretability of ML models trained on real-world data. In particular, we consider the prediction of five-year survival for multiple myeloma patients using clinical data from Helsinki University Hospital. To ensure the interpretability of the trained models, we use two alternative constructions for a penalty term used for regularization. The first one penalizes deviations from the predictions obtained from an interpretable logistic regression method with two manually chosen features. The second construction requires consistency of model predictions with the revised international staging system (R-ISS). We verify the usefulness of the proposed regularization techniques in numerical experiments using data from 812 patients. They achieve an accuracy up to 0.721 on a test set and SHAP values show that the models rely on the selected important features.

</details>


### [144] [The Multiverse of Time Series Machine Learning: an Archive for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20352)
*Matthew Middlehurst,Aiden Rushbrooke,Ali Ismail-Fawaz,Maxime Devanne,Germain Forestier,Angus Dempster,Geoffrey I. Webb,Christopher Holder,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将UEA多变量时间序列分类数据集从30个扩展到133个（含预处理版本共147个），重新命名为Multiverse archive，并提供MV-core子集与基准算法评估，为TSML研究提供统一资源平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列机器学习（TSML）领域的发展依赖于基准数据集的支撑。2018年发布的包含30个多变量时间序列分类数据集的UEA archive已成为数百篇论文引用的核心资源。然而，随着该领域和更广泛社区的快速发展，现有数据集规模已无法满足研究需求，亟需扩充以支持更全面、多样化的算法评估与比较研究。

Method: 作者对原UEA archive进行了大规模扩展，新增数据集至133个分类问题，并对包含缺失值或不等长序列的数据集进行预处理，使总数据集达147个。该数据集（更名为Multiverse archive）整合了多个来源的数据集，形成统一的数据仓库。为降低计算成本，提出推荐子集MV-core用于初步探索。作者提供详细的使用指南，并构建了兼容aeon和scikit-learn框架的专属代码仓库，包含可复现性工具、发表结果记录与交互式接口。
