The results suggest that dynActivation variants tend to linearize deep layers while maintaining high performance, which can improve training efficiency by up to $+54\%$ over ReLU.
  On CIFAR-10, dynActivation(Mish) improves over static Mish by up to $+14.02\%$ on AttentionCNN with an average improvment by $+6.00\%$, with a $24\%$ convergence-AUC reduction relative to Mish (2120 vs. 2785). In a 1-to-75-layer MNIST depth-scaling study, dynActivation never drops below $95\%$ test accuracy ($95.3$--$99.3\%$), while ReLU collapses below $80\%$ at 25 layers. Under FGSM at $\varepsilon{=}0.08$, dynActivation(Mish) incurs a $55.39\%$ accuracy drop versus $62.79\%$ for ReLU ($7.40\%$ advantage). Transferred to language modeling, a new proposed dynActGLU-variant achieves a $10.3\%$ relative perplexity reduction over SwiGLU at 5620 steps (4.047 vs. 4.514), though the gap vanishes at 34300 steps.

</details>


### [292] [RAMPAGE: RAndomized Mid-Point for debiAsed Gradient Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22155)
*Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了经典Extragradient (EG)方法在非线性向量场上存在离散化偏差，并提出了两种无偏方法RAMPAGE和RAMPAGE+，后者利用对偶采样消除方差中的内部一阶项，在多种变分不等式问题中实现了O(1/k)收敛率，且在部分场景下获得了确定性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Extragradient (EG)方法可视为标准的离散时间积分方案，但在应用于非线性向量场（保守或非保守）时存在离散化偏差问题。作者旨在揭示这一缺陷并设计无偏的方法来解决该问题。

Method: 提出了RAndomized Mid-Point for debiAsed Gradient Extrapolation (RAMPAGE)方法及其方差缩减版本RAMPAGE+。RAMPAGE+利用对偶采样(antithetic sampling)和负相关技术，作为一个无偏的几何路径积分器，完全消除了方差中的内部一阶项。同时引入了对称缩放变体以将结果扩展到约束变分不等式问题。

Result: 两种方法在包括共强制、共亚单调和广义Lipschitz条件下的根寻找问题等多种场景中都具有可证明的O(1/k)收敛保证。RAMPAGE+相比RAMPAGE有显著改进。尽管是随机化方法，RAMPAGE+在多个研究场景中获得了纯确定性界限。方法还扩展到了约束变分不等式，并为随机和确定性光滑凸凹博弈提供了收敛保证。

Conclusion: 成功识别并解决了EG方法的离散化偏差问题，提出的RAMPAGE系列方法实现了无偏性，在理论上和实验上都证明了其有效性，为变分不等式问题提供了更优的求解方案。

Abstract: A celebrated method for Variational Inequalities (VIs) is Extragradient (EG), which can be viewed as a standard discrete-time integration scheme. With this view in mind, in this paper we show that EG may suffer from discretization bias when applied to non-linear vector fields, conservative or otherwise. To resolve this discretization shortcoming, we introduce RAndomized Mid-Point for debiAsed Gradient Extrapolation (RAMPAGE) and its variance-reduced counterpart, RAMPAGE+ which leverages antithetic sampling. In contrast with EG, both methods are unbiased. Furthermore, leveraging negative correlation, RAMPAGE+ acts as an unbiased, geometric path-integrator that completely removes internal first-order terms from the variance, provably improving upon RAMPAGE. We further demonstrate that both methods enjoy provable $\mathcal{O}(1/k)$ convergence guarantees for a range of problems including root finding under co-coercive, co-hypomonotone, and generalized Lipschitzness regimes. Furthermore, we introduce symmetrically scaled variants to extend our results to constrained VIs. Finally, we provide convergence guarantees of both methods for stochastic and deterministic smooth convex-concave games. Somewhat interestingly, despite being a randomized method, RAMPAGE+ attains purely deterministic bounds for a number of the studied settings.

</details>


### [293] [Multimodal Survival Analysis with Locally Deployable Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22158)
*Moritz Gögl,Christopher Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种可在本地部署的轻量级大语言模型方法，用于整合临床文本、表格协变量和基因组数据的多模态生存分析，在保证隐私安全的同时提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多医疗机构面临计算资源紧张和隐私保护的双重限制，无法使用云端大语言模型，因此需要开发轻量级、可本地部署的多模态生存分析方法。

Method: 采用教师-学生蒸馏技术和有原则的多模态融合方法，联合估计校准后的生存概率，并生成简洁、基于证据的预后文本。

Result: 在TCGA队列上的实验表明，该方法优于标准基线方法，同时避免了对云端服务的依赖和相关的隐私问题，并降低了基础大语言模型可能出现的幻觉或校准错误风险。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在本地部署环境下进行多模态生存分析的可行性，为资源受限的医疗机构提供了一种既保护隐私又具有良好预测性能的解决方案。

Abstract: We study multimodal survival analysis integrating clinical text, tabular covariates, and genomic profiles using locally deployable large language models (LLMs). As many institutions face tight computational and privacy constraints, this setting motivates the use of lightweight, on-premises models. Our approach jointly estimates calibrated survival probabilities and generates concise, evidence-grounded prognosis text via teacher-student distillation and principled multimodal fusion. On a TCGA cohort, it outperforms standard baselines, avoids reliance on cloud services and associated privacy concerns, and reduces the risk of hallucinated or miscalibrated estimates that can be observed in base LLMs.

</details>


### [294] [Calibeating Made Simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22167)
*Yurong Chen,Zhiyi Huang,Michael I. Jordan,Haipeng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将校准打击（calibeating）问题转化为在线学习技术，建立了其与后悔最小化和专家问题的极小极大等价性，从而获得了多种损失函数下的最优校准打击速率，并首次提出了同时实现校准和最优校准打击速率的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作仅针对特定损失函数和特定参数分析校准打击问题，缺乏统一的理论框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过建立校准打击与现有在线学习技术之间的联系，为一般适当损失函数提供通用的解决方案。

Method: （1）证明了校准打击与后悔最小化在极小极大意义下等价；（2）证明了多校准打击与校准打击及经典专家问题的组合在极小极大意义下等价；（3）利用这些等价性，将在线学习中的成熟技术和分析工具应用于校准打击问题。

Result: （1）恢复了Foster和 Hart [FH23]在Brier损失和对数损失下的O(log T)校准打击速率及其最优性；（2）获得了可混合损失和一般有界损失的新最优校准打击速率；（3）得到了可混合损失（包括Brier和对数损失）及一般有界损失的新最优多校准打击速率；（4）针对Brier损失，获得了同时实现校准和校准打击的新界。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立校准打击与经典在线学习问题之间的等价关系，成功将丰富的在线学习工具应用于校准打击领域，不仅统一和推广了已有结果，还为该领域开辟了新的研究方向，特别是针对二元预测，首次实现了同时满足校准性质和最优校准打击速率的算法。

Abstract: We study calibeating, the problem of post-processing external forecasts online to minimize cumulative losses and match an informativeness-based benchmark. Unlike prior work, which analyzed calibeating for specific losses with specific arguments, we reduce calibeating to existing online learning techniques and obtain results for general proper losses. More concretely, we first show that calibeating is minimax-equivalent to regret minimization. This recovers the $O(\log T)$ calibeating rate of Foster and Hart [FH23] for the Brier and log losses and its optimality, and yields new optimal calibeating rates for mixable losses and general bounded losses. Second, we prove that multi-calibeating is minimax-equivalent to the combination of calibeating and the classical expert problem. This yields new optimal multi-calibeating rates for mixable losses, including Brier and log losses, and general bounded losses. Finally, we obtain new bounds for achieving calibeating and calibration simultaneously for the Brier loss. For binary predictions, our result gives the first calibrated algorithm that at the same time also achieves the optimal $O(\log T)$ calibeating rate.

</details>


### [295] [Causal Evidence that Language Models use Confidence to Drive Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22161)
*Dharshan Kumaran,Nathaniel Daw,Simon Osindero,Petar Velickovic,Viorica Patraucean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过四阶段实验范式，证实了大语言模型（LLM）具备类似生物系统的两阶段元认知控制能力，即弃权行为源于内部置信度表征与阈值策略的共同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知可从LLM输出中提取置信度，但模型是否像生物体一样主动利用这些内部信号来调节自身行为（如决定作答或弃权）仍是一个根本性问题。探究此问题对于理解LLM的认知能力及其作为自主智能体的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 采用四阶段弃权范式：第一阶段建立无弃权选项时的内部置信度基线；第二阶段分析LLM在决定作答或弃权时是否应用隐式阈值；第三阶段通过激活引导（activation steering）因果性地操纵内部置信度信号以观察弃权率变化；第四阶段验证模型能否根据指令设定的阈值系统性地调整弃权策略。

Result: 实验发现LLM在决策时应用隐式阈值，且置信度是预测行为的主导因素，其效应量比知识检索可及性（RAG分数）或表面语义特征大一个数量级。因果操纵证实，改变内部置信度信号会相应改变弃权率，且模型能根据指令系统性地调整弃权策略。

Conclusion: LLM的弃权行为源于内部置信度表征与基于阈值的策略的共同作用，这种机制反映了在生物系统中发现的两阶段元认知控制。这种能力对于LLM转变为能够识别自身不确定性并决定何时行动或寻求帮助的自主智能体具有重要意义。

Abstract: Metacognition -- the ability to assess one's own cognitive performance -- is documented across species, with internal confidence estimates serving as a key signal for adaptive behavior. While confidence can be extracted from Large Language Model (LLM) outputs, whether models actively use these signals to regulate behavior remains a fundamental question. We investigate this through a four-phase abstention paradigm.Phase 1 established internal confidence estimates in the absence of an abstention option. Phase 2 revealed that LLMs apply implicit thresholds to these estimates when deciding to answer or abstain. Confidence emerged as the dominant predictor of behavior, with effect sizes an order of magnitude larger than knowledge retrieval accessibility (RAG scores) or surface-level semantic features. Phase 3 provided causal evidence through activation steering: manipulating internal confidence signals correspondingly shifted abstention rates. Finally, Phase 4 demonstrated that models can systematically vary abstention policies based on instructed thresholds.Our findings indicate that abstention arises from the joint operation of internal confidence representations and threshold-based policies, mirroring the two-stage metacognitive control found in biological systems. This capacity is essential as LLMs transition into autonomous agents that must recognize their own uncertainty to decide when to act or seek help.

</details>


### [296] [SPA: A Simple but Tough-to-Beat Baseline for Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22213)
*Kexian Tang,Jiani Wang,Shaowen Wang,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPA方法，通过精心设计的提示词生成大规模合成数据用于知识注入，发现其优于多种强基线；揭示了现有方法在扩展性上的局限性，证明了简单的大规模增强结合精细提示设计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然经过海量数据预训练，但在专业且数据稀缺的领域知识覆盖仍不完整。现有合成数据生成方法存在局限性，亟需研究更有效的知识注入方法。

Method: 提出SPA（Scaling Prompt-engineered Augmentation）方法，通过少量精心设计的提示词生成大规模合成数据用于知识注入，并进行了系统性的对比实验分析。

Result: SPA优于多个强基线方法。发现现有方法的两个关键局限：RL方法在小规模时提高效率但大规模时会出现多样性崩溃；多阶段提示方法优势在精细调优后消失。

Conclusion: 对于知识注入任务，精细的提示词设计结合直接的大规模增强方法具有惊人的效果，SPA可作为该领域未来研究的强基线。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are pretrained on massive amounts of data, their knowledge coverage remains incomplete in specialized, data-scarce domains, motivating extensive efforts to study synthetic data generation for knowledge injection. We propose SPA (Scaling Prompt-engineered Augmentation), a simple but tough-to-beat baseline that uses a small set of carefully designed prompts to generate large-scale synthetic data for knowledge injection. Through systematic comparisons, we find that SPA outperforms several strong baselines. Furthermore, we identify two key limitations of prior approaches: (1) while RL-based methods may improve the token efficiency of LLM-based data augmentation at small scale, they suffer from diversity collapse as data scales, leading to diminishing returns; and (2) while multi-stage prompting may outperform simple augmentation methods, their advantages can disappear after careful prompt tuning. Our results suggest that, for knowledge injection, careful prompt design combined with straightforward large-scale augmentation can be surprisingly effective, and we hope SPA can serve as a strong baseline for future studies in this area. Our code is available at https://github.com/Tangkexian/SPA.

</details>


### [297] [Confidence-Based Decoding is Provably Efficient for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22248)
*Changxiao Cai,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为扩散语言模型（DLM）中基于置信度的解码策略建立了理论分析框架，证明了基于熵和的策略可实现采样加速，其迭代复杂度与数据分布熵相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型相比自回归模型具有灵活的生成顺序和并行生成能力，但这种灵活性引入了解码策略选择的挑战。尽管基于置信度的解码方法在实践中表现优异，但理论理解仍然有限，缺乏对其性能和效率的理论保证。

Method: 提出基于熵和的解码策略：在每次迭代中持续揭示（unmask）Token，直到累积熵超过预设阈值。作者建立了理论分析框架，证明该方法能在KL散度下实现ε精度采样，预期迭代次数为O(H(X₀)/ε)，其中H(X₀)为目标数据分布的熵。

Result: 证明了基于熵和的解码策略具有理论保证：1）可实现ε精度采样；2）迭代复杂度为O(H(X₀)/ε)；3）当数据分布相对于序列长度具有低熵时，能实现显著加速；4）能自动适应数据内在复杂度，无需先验知识或超参数调整。

Conclusion: 本研究为DLM中基于置信度的解码策略提供了首个理论分析框架，证明了基于熵和策略的有效性和效率优势，为未来设计更高效的DLM解码策略奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) models for language modeling, allowing flexible generation order and parallel generation of multiple tokens. However, this flexibility introduces a challenge absent in AR models: the \emph{decoding strategy} -- which determines the order and number of tokens generated at each iteration -- critically affects sampling efficiency. Among decoding strategies explored in practice, confidence-based methods, which adaptively select which and how many tokens to unmask based on prediction confidence, have shown strong empirical performance. Despite this success, our theoretical understanding of confidence-based decoding remains limited.
  In this work, we develop the first theoretical analysis framework for confidence-based decoding in DLMs. We focus on an entropy sum-based strategy that continues unmasking tokens within each iteration until the cumulative entropy exceeds a threshold, and show that it achieves $\varepsilon$-accurate sampling in KL divergence with an expected number of iterations $\widetilde O(H(X_0)/\varepsilon)$, where $H(X_0)$ denotes the entropy of the target data distribution. Notably, this strategy yields substantial sampling acceleration when the data distribution has low entropy relative to the sequence length, while automatically adapting to the intrinsic complexity of data without requiring prior knowledge or hyperparameter tuning. Overall, our results provide a theoretical foundation for confidence-based decoding and may inform the design of more efficient decoding strategies for DLMs.

</details>


### [298] [Revisiting Quantum Code Generation: Where Should Domain Knowledge Live?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22184)
*Oscar Novo,Oscar Bastidas-Jossa,Alberto Calvo,Antonio Peris,Carlos Kuchkovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，在Qiskit量子代码生成任务中，具备检索增强（RAG）和执行反馈代理的现代通用大模型显著优于领域专用微调模型，其中最强模型准确率达85%，较基线提升超35%，证明了推理时增强比参数微调更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子软件开发等快速演进的领域，如何将领域知识融入LLM助手同时保持库更新时的可维护性是一个核心问题。研究旨在探索是否需要领域专用微调，或可通过其他方式实现更好的代码生成效果。

Method: 基于Qiskit-HumanEval基准测试，系统比较了参数专用微调基线模型与多种增强策略的通用LLM：包括零样本设置、检索增强生成（RAG）、以及结合执行反馈的代理推理方法。

Result: 通用模型零样本和RAG设置下达到60-65% pass@1；结合迭代执行反馈代理的最强模型达85%，较零样本提升超20%，较微调基线（47%）提升超35%。代理执行反馈改进最稳定（运行成本较高），RAG效果适中且依赖模型。

Conclusion: 无需领域特定微调，通过推理时增强（特别是执行反馈代理）即可显著提升量子代码生成性能，这种方法更具灵活性和可维护性，更适合快速演进的软件生态系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled the automation of an increasing number of programming tasks, including code generation for scientific and engineering domains. In rapidly evolving software ecosystems such as quantum software development, where frameworks expose complex abstractions, a central question is how best to incorporate domain knowledge into LLM-based assistants while preserving maintainability as libraries evolve.
  In this work, we study specialization strategies for Qiskit code generation using the Qiskit-HumanEval benchmark. We compare a parameter-specialized fine-tuned baseline introduced in prior work against a range of recent general-purpose LLMs enhanced with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and agent-based inference with execution feedback.
  Our results show that modern general-purpose LLMs consistently outperform the parameter-specialized baseline. While the fine-tuned model achieves approximately 47% pass@1 on Qiskit-HumanEval, recent general-purpose models reach 60-65% under zero-shot and retrieval-augmented settings, and up to 85% for the strongest evaluated model when combined with iterative execution-feedback agents -representing an improvement of more than 20% over zero-shot general-purpose performance and more than 35% over the parameter-specialized baseline.
  Agentic execution feedback yields the most consistent improvements, albeit at increased runtime cost, while RAG provides modest and model-dependent gains. These findings indicate that performance gains can be achieved without domain-specific fine-tuning, instead relying on inference-time augmentation, thereby enabling a more flexible and maintainable approach to LLM-assisted quantum software development.

</details>


### [299] [Chimera: Latency- and Performance-Aware Multi-agent Serving for Heterogeneous LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22206)
*Kangqi Ni,Wenyue Hua,Xiaoxiang Shi,Jiang Guo,Shiyu Chang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Chimera系统，通过预测性调度在异构LLM集群上优化多智能体工作流服务，实现了比vLLM等基线方法延迟降低1.2-2.4倍且任务性能提升8.0-9.5个百分点的双重收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统主要面向同构集群设计，忽视了异构部署在延迟与性能权衡上的潜力。多智能体应用常以多阶段工作流形式执行任务，引入异构性带来了跨模型吞吐量和性能差异的调度新挑战。

Method: 设计预测性调度系统Chimera：应用语义路由估计每个请求的模型置信度分数；预测工作流剩余输出总长度；利用在途预测token量估计各模型拥塞情况进行负载均衡。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理的代表性智能体工作流上，使用多种异构LLM配置评估，相比vLLM等竞争基线，在相当设置下端到端延迟降低1.2-2.4倍，任务性能平均提升8.0-9.5个百分点，追踪最佳延迟-性能前沿。

Conclusion: Chimera验证了在异构LLM集群上采用预测性调度策略能够有效解决多智能体工作流服务中的调度难题，在保持或提升任务性能的同时显著降低了端到端延迟。

Abstract: Multi-agent applications often execute complex tasks as multi-stage workflows, where each stage is an LLM call whose output becomes part of context for subsequent steps. Existing LLM serving systems largely assume homogeneous clusters with identical model replicas. This design overlooks the potential of heterogeneous deployments, where models of different sizes and capabilities enable finer trade-offs between latency and performance. However, heterogeneity introduces new challenges in scheduling across models with diverse throughput and performance. We present Chimera, a predictive scheduling system for multi-agent workflow serving on heterogeneous LLM clusters that jointly improves end-to-end latency and task performance. Chimera applies semantic routing to estimate per-model confidence scores for each request, predicts the total remaining output length of the workflow, and estimates per-model congestion using in-flight predicted token volumes for load balancing. We evaluate Chimera on representative agentic workflows for code generation and math reasoning using multiple heterogeneous LLM configurations. Across comparable settings, Chimera traces the best latency-performance frontier, reducing end-to-end latency by 1.2--2.4$\times$ and improving task performance by 8.0-9.5 percentage points on average over competitive baselines including vLLM.

</details>


### [300] [Noise Titration: Exact Distributional Benchmarking for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22219)
*Qilin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于干预主义和精确统计基准测试的时间序列预测评估新范式，通过向已知动力学系统中注入高斯噪声将预测转化为精确分布推断任务。实验表明，现有的基础模型在非平稳条件下系统性失效，而改进的Fern架构能够保持结构保真度和统计校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代时间序列预测评估完全依赖于对单一历史轨迹的被动观测，导致关于模型对非平稳性鲁棒性的声明根本无法证伪。缺乏精确统计基准使得难以判断模型的真正泛化能力。

Method: 提出干预主义评估范式：向已知混沌和随机动力学系统注入校准高斯观测噪声，将预测从黑盒序列匹配转化为精确分布推断任务。由于数据生成过程和噪声方差的数学表达式明确，评估可依赖精确负对数似然和校准分布测试。同时将Fern架构扩展为概率生成模型，原生参数化对称正定(SPD)锥，输出校准的联合协方差结构。

Result: 最先进的零样本基础模型表现出上下文鹦鹉机制，在非平稳区域转移和噪声升高时系统性失效。相比之下，Fern显式捕获底层动力学的不变测度和多变量几何，在大型序列匹配模型崩溃的条件下维持结构保真度和统计锐利校准。

Conclusion: 干预主义精确统计基准测试能够有效揭示现有预测模型的真实局限性，为时间序列预测提供了一个可证伪、数学严谨的评估框架，推动了从启发式近似向精确统计推断的转变。

Abstract: Modern time series forecasting is evaluated almost entirely through passive observation of single historical trajectories, rendering claims about a model's robustness to non-stationarity fundamentally unfalsifiable. We propose a paradigm shift toward interventionist, exact-statistical benchmarking. By systematically titrating calibrated Gaussian observation noise into known chaotic and stochastic dynamical systems, we transform forecasting from a black-box sequence matching game into an exact distributional inference task. Because the underlying data-generating process and noise variance are mathematically explicit, evaluation can rely on exact negative log-likelihoods and calibrated distributional tests rather than heuristic approximations. To fully leverage this framework, we extend the Fern architecture into a probabilistic generative model that natively parameterizes the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) cone, outputting calibrated joint covariance structures without the computational bottleneck of generic Jacobian modeling. Under this rigorous evaluation, we find that state-of-the-art zero-shot foundation models behave consistently with the context-parroting mechanism, failing systematically under non-stationary regime shifts and elevated noise. In contrast, Fern explicitly captures the invariant measure and multivariate geometry of the underlying dynamics, maintaining structural fidelity and statistically sharp calibration precisely where massive sequence-matching models collapse.

</details>


### [301] [Decoupling Exploration and Policy Optimization: Uncertainty Guided Tree Search for Hard Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22273)
*Zakaria Mhammedi,James Cohan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将探索与利用显式分离的新范式，在探索阶段绕过强化学习，使用树搜索策略和认知不确定性度量，实现了比标准内在动机基线高一个数量级的探索效率，并在多个困难任务上达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主探索方法主要使用强化学习训练具有内在动机的智能体，通过最大化外在和内在奖励的复合目标来解决问题。作者认为这种方法存在不必要的开销：虽然策略优化对于精确的任务执行是必要的，但仅为了扩展状态覆盖而使用这种机制可能效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种显式分离探索与利用的新范式，在探索阶段绕过强化学习。该方法使用受Go-With-The-Winner算法启发的树搜索策略，结合认知不确定性度量来系统性地驱动探索。发现的轨迹随后通过现有的监督反向学习算法蒸馏为可部署的策略。

Result: 在困难Atari基准测试上，探索效率比标准内在动机基线高一个数量级；在Montezuma's Revenge、Pitfall!和Venture上以显著优势达到了最先进分数；在高维连续动作空间中，直接从图像观察解决了MuJoCo Adroit灵巧操作和AntMaze任务，无需专家演示或离线数据集。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离探索与利用，并使用树搜索代替强化学习进行探索，可以显著提高探索效率。该方法具有通用性，能够解决稀疏奖励设置下的高维连续动作空间问题，这是首次实现此类成果。

Abstract: The process of discovery requires active exploration -- the act of collecting new and informative data. However, efficient autonomous exploration remains a major unsolved problem. The dominant paradigm addresses this challenge by using Reinforcement Learning (RL) to train agents with intrinsic motivation, maximizing a composite objective of extrinsic and intrinsic rewards. We suggest that this approach incurs unnecessary overhead: while policy optimization is necessary for precise task execution, employing such machinery solely to expand state coverage may be inefficient. In this paper, we propose a new paradigm that explicitly separates exploration from exploitation and bypasses RL during the exploration phase. Our method uses a tree-search strategy inspired by the Go-With-The-Winner algorithm, paired with a measure of epistemic uncertainty to systematically drive exploration. By removing the overhead of policy optimization, our approach explores an order of magnitude more efficiently than standard intrinsic motivation baselines on hard Atari benchmarks. Further, we demonstrate that the discovered trajectories can be distilled into deployable policies using existing supervised backward learning algorithms, achieving state-of-the-art scores by a wide margin on Montezuma's Revenge, Pitfall!, and Venture without relying on domain-specific knowledge. Finally, we demonstrate the generality of our framework in high-dimensional continuous action spaces by solving the MuJoCo Adroit dexterous manipulation and AntMaze tasks in a sparse-reward setting, directly from image observations and without expert demonstrations or offline datasets. To the best of our knowledge, this has not been achieved before.

</details>


### [302] [Scaling DoRA: High-Rank Adaptation via Factored Norms and Fused Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22276)
*Alexandra Zelenin,Alexandra Zhuravlyova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对高秩DoRA的高效系统优化方案，通过因式分解范数计算和融合Triton内核，在保持数值精度的同时实现了1.5-2.7倍加速并显著降低显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DoRA通过解耦权重幅度和方向扩展了LoRA，但其前向传播需要对W + sBA进行逐行范数计算，现有框架通过具体化稠密[d_out, d_in]矩阵乘积BA来实现。当d_in = 8192、秩r = 384时，单个模块的范数计算在bf16下需要约512 MB瞬态工作内存，使得高秩DoRA在涉及数百个适配模块和检查点的常见单GPU设置上成本高昂且常常不可行。

Method: 提出了两项系统贡献：（1）因式分解范数：将平方范数分解为基项、交叉项和Gram项，可通过O(d_out r + r²)中间量计算，消除了稠密矩阵乘积；（2）融合Triton内核：将四内核DoRA组合折叠为单次通过，减少约4倍内存流量，并采用数值稳定形式避免在接近单位重缩放区域（幅度尺度实际集中的区域）的灾难性抵消。

Result: 在六种8-32B视觉语言模型、三种NVIDIA GPU（RTX 6000 PRO、H200、B200）、秩r = 384、bf16精度设置下，融合实现相比Hugging Face PEFT的DoRA实现：推理速度提升1.5-2.0倍，梯度计算速度提升1.5-1.9倍（不含优化器步骤），峰值显存降低高达7 GB。跨四代架构的六种GPU微基准测试证实组合内核加速1.5-2.7倍。最终逻辑余弦相似度在所有模型/GPU配对中超过0.9999，多种子训练曲线在2000步内每步损失差值在7.1×10⁻⁴以内匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作成功解决了高秩DoRA的内存瓶颈和计算效率问题，使得原本在单GPU上不可行的高秩适配变得可行，同时完全保持了数值精度和训练稳定性，为大规模模型的高效参数微调提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA) extends LoRA by decoupling weight magnitude from direction, but its forward pass requires the row-wise norm of W + sBA, a computation that every major framework we surveyed implements by materializing the dense [d_out, d_in] product BA. At d_in = 8192 and rank r = 384, a single module's norm requires about 512 MB of transient working memory in bf16, making high-rank DoRA costly and often infeasible on common single-GPU setups once hundreds of adapted modules and checkpointing are involved.
  We present two systems contributions. A factored norm decomposes the squared norm into base, cross, and Gram terms computable through O(d_out r + r^2) intermediates, eliminating the dense product. Fused Triton kernels collapse the four-kernel DoRA composition into a single pass, reducing memory traffic by about 4x and using a numerically stable form that avoids catastrophic cancellation in the near-unity rescaling regime where magnitude scales concentrate in practice.
  Across six 8-32B vision-language models (VLMs) on three NVIDIA GPUs (RTX 6000 PRO, H200, B200) at r = 384 in bf16, the fused implementation is 1.5-2.0x faster than Hugging Face PEFT's DoRA implementation for inference and 1.5-1.9x faster for gradient computation (optimizer step excluded), with up to 7 GB lower peak VRAM. Microbenchmarks on six GPUs spanning four architecture generations (L40S, A100, RTX 6000 PRO, H200, B200, B300) confirm 1.5-2.7x compose-kernel speedup. Final-logit cosine similarity exceeds 0.9999 across all model/GPU pairs, and multi-seed training curves match within 7.1 x 10^-4 mean per-step loss delta over 2000 steps.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [303] [COmPOSER: Circuit Optimization of mm-wave/RF circuits with Performance-Oriented Synthesis for Efficient Realizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20486)
*Subhadip Ghosh,Surya Srikar Peri,Ramprasath S.,Sosina A. Berhan,Endalk Y. Gebru,Ramesh Harjani,Sachin S. Sapatnekar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了COmPOSER，一个开源的端到端RF/毫米波设计自动化框架，能将目标规格转化为优化电路及版图，实现100-300倍的生产力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频/毫米波电路设计通常依赖专家手工完成，流程耗时且效率低下。为降低设计门槛、提升设计效率并实现从规格到版图的自动化，有必要开发统一的自动化设计框架。

Method: 提出COmPOSER框架，统一了原理图综合、有源/无源器件版图生成以及布局布线；融合了基于物理的方程与机器学习驱动的电磁模型进行建模与优化。

Result: 在商用65nm工艺上，对多个工作频率高达60GHz的低噪声放大器（LNA）和功率放大器（PA）进行后版图验证，结果显示COmPOSER能够达到性能目标，其结果可与专家手工设计相媲美，同时实现100-300倍的生产力提升。

Conclusion: COmPOSER证明了将物理方程与机器学习模型相结合实现RF/毫米波电路端到端自动设计的可行性，在保证性能的同时显著提升设计效率，为集成电路设计自动化提供了有力工具。

Abstract: This work presents COmPOSER, an open-source, end-to-end framework for RF/mm-wave design automation that translates target specifications into optimized circuits with layouts. It unifies schematic synthesis, layout generation for actives and passives, and placement/routing, incorporating physics-based equations and machine-learning-driven electromagnetic models. Based on post-layout validation on multiple LNAs and PAs operating at up to 60GHz in a commercial 65nm process-kit, COmPOSER meets performance targets, comparable to expert manual designs, while delivering a 100-300x productivity gain.

</details>


### [304] [MINISA: Minimal Instruction Set Architecture for Next-gen Reconfigurable Inference Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20623)
*Jianming Tong,Devansh Jain,Yujie Li,Charith Mendis,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出MINISA指令集，通过虚拟神经元（VN）粒度的粗粒度控制，在保持硬件灵活性的同时避免不必要的控制开销，将指令流量减少35x至4x10^5倍，实现最高31.6倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代可重构AI加速器依赖丰富的映射和数据布局灵活性来维持高利用率，但通过细粒度微控制暴露这种灵活性会导致从片外存储器获取配置比特的控制开销过高（占96.9%的微指令周期），形成指令获取停顿的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出MINISA最小指令集：(1)改进FEATHER+，消除运行时数据流/布局协同切换所需的冗余片内复制，支持执行前输入权重数据不可用的动态场景；(2)将FEATHER+的控制抽象为三条布局设置指令（分别针对输入、权重、输出虚拟神经元）和一条映射指令（设置数据流），在保留合法映射和布局空间的同时减少控制和指令足迹。

Result: 在涵盖AI（GPT-oss）、FHE和ZKP的50个GEMM工作负载下，MINISA将几何平均片外指令流量减少35x至4x10^5倍，消除了消耗96.9%微指令周期的指令获取停顿，对16x256 FEATHER+实现高达31.6倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: MINISA通过虚拟神经元粒度的最小指令集设计，成功解决了可重构加速器细粒度控制带来的控制开销问题，在保持映射和布局灵活性的同时大幅降低了指令流量，为AI及新兴应用的高效加速提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern reconfigurable AI accelerators rely on rich mapping and data-layout flexibility to sustain high utilization across matrix multiplication, convolution, and emerging applications beyond AI. However, exposing this flexibility through fine-grained micro-control results in prohibitive control overhead of fetching configuration bits from off-chip memory. This paper presents MINISA, a minimal instruction set that programs a reconfigurable accelerator at the granularity of Virtual Neurons (VNs), the coarsest control granularity that retains flexibility of hardware and the finest granularity that avoids unnecessary control costs. First, we introduce FEATHER+, a modest refinement of FEATHER, that eliminates redundant on-chip replication needed for runtime dataflow/layout co-switching and supports dynamic cases where input and weight data are unavailable before execution for offline layout manipulation. MINISA then abstracts control of FEATHER+ into three layout-setting instructions for input, weight, and output VNs and a single mapping instruction for setting dataflow. This reduces the control and instruction footprint while preserving the legal mapping and layout space supported by the FEATHER+. Our results show that MINISA reduces geometric mean off-chip instruction traffic by factors ranging from 35x to (4x10^5)x under various sizes under 50 GEMM workloads spanning AI (GPT-oss), FHE, and ZKP. This eliminates instruction-fetch stalls that consume 96.9% of micro-instruction cycles, yielding up to 31.6x end-to-end speedup for 16x256 FEATHER+. Our code: https://github.com/maeri-project/FEATHER/tree/main/minisa.

</details>


### [305] [PC2IM: An Efficient In-Memory Computing Accelerator for 3D Point Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21167)
*Dengfeng Wang,Shunqin Cai,Yanan Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出PC2IM，一种面向3D点云神经网络的高效SRAM存内计算加速器，通过优化数据预处理和特征计算流程，实现1.5倍加速和2.7倍能效提升（对比SOTA），相比GPU实现3.5倍加速和1518.9倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D点云神经网络虽然提升了移动智能系统的感知能力，但在数据预处理阶段存在大量内存访问，特征计算负载重，导致高能耗和高延迟，存在内存访问瓶颈。

Method: 1) 提出定制化CIM引擎驱动的数据预处理模块，融入内存高效数据流；2) 设计近似距离SRAM-CIM（APD-CIM），消除空间分割点云的重复片上内存访问，减少临时距离数据量；3) 基于APD-CIM引入两级Ping-Pong-MAX CAM，自适应更新临时距离并原位搜索最大值；4) 提出split-concatenate SRAM-CIM特征计算引擎，降低高精度输入MLP的计算延迟。

Result: 实验表明，相比最先进点云加速器，PC2IM实现1.5倍加速和2.7倍能效提升；相比GPU实现，达到3.5倍加速和1518.9倍能效提升。

Conclusion: PC2IM通过SRAM-CIM架构创新有效缓解了点云网络的内存访问瓶颈，在保持高面积和能效的同时显著降低了延迟，为资源受限的移动智能系统提供了高效的点云处理解决方案。

Abstract: 3D point cloud neural networks have significantly enhanced the perceptual capabilities of resource-limited mobile intelligent systems. However, despite the transformative impact, the point cloud algorithm suffers from substantial memory access during data preprocessing and imposes a burdensome workload on feature computing, resulting in high energy consumption and latency. In this paper, an efficient SRAM-based computing-in-memory (SRAM-CIM) accelerator (PC2IM), is proposed to alleviate memory access bottlenecks in point-based 3D point cloud networks. A data preprocessing module driven by the customized CIM engines is proposed and incorporated into a memory-efficient data flow. Specifically, an approximate distance SRAM-CIM (APD-CIM) is introduced to eliminate the repetitive on-chip memory access for point clouds that are spatially partitioned by the median and reduce the volume of temporary distance data. Building on the APD-CIM, a two-level Ping-Pong-MAX Content Addressable Memory (Ping-Pong-MAX CAM) is introduced to adaptively update temporary distances and perform in-situ search for the maximum, further reducing memory access. Additionally, an efficient CIM-based feature computing engine, named split-concatenate SRAM-CIM, is presented to minimize computation latency in multi-layer perceptron with high-precision input, while maintaining high area and energy efficiency. Experiment results show that the proposed PC2IM demonstrates 1.5x speedup and 2.7x enhanced energy efficiency compared to state-of-the-art point cloud accelerator. Moreover, PC2IM achieves 3.5x speedup and 1518.9x enhanced energy efficiency compared to GPU implementations.

</details>


### [306] [DS2SC-Agent: A Multi-Agent Automated Pipeline for Rapid Chiplet Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21190)
*Yiwei Wu,Yifan Wu,Yunhao Xiong,Dengwei Zhao,Jiaxuan Shen,Jianfei Jiang,Guanghui He,Shikui Tu,Yanan Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了DS2SC-Agent，这是首个将原始数据手册直接转换为SystemC小芯片模型的端到端全自动生成流水线，通过多智能体协作框架解决了现有LLM辅助设计框架在处理非结构化长文档时的上下文消失和逻辑幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建行为级芯片模型（如SystemC）对早期异构架构探索至关重要，但传统手工建模耗时且易出错。现有LLM辅助设计框架主要针对高度结构化或定义良好的设计规范，而实际工程中的原始数据手册通常包含冗长、复杂且高度非结构化的信息，直接从这些原始数据手册生成可靠代码面临上下文消失和逻辑幻觉等严峻挑战。

Method: 提出DS2SC-Agent系统，建立一个高效的多智能体协作框架。通过解耦复杂的建模任务，该流水线编排了一个全自动工作流程，包括非结构化长文档解析、SystemC核心代码构建、测试平台激励生成和自适应闭环调试。

Result: 在模拟、数字和射频领域的代表性单功能小芯片上全面评估了该框架，具体包括限幅放大器（LA）、快速傅里叶变换（FFT）模块和功率放大器（PA）。评估结果表明，该流水线能够无缝处理复杂的真实世界数据手册，持续生成功能正确的SystemC模型。

Conclusion: 该工作为敏捷模型库构建提供了一个高效可靠的范式，同时大幅减少了人工干预，成功实现了从原始数据手册到SystemC模型的端到端全自动生成。

Abstract: Constructing behavioral-level chiplet models (e.g., SystemC) is crucial for early-stage heterogeneous architecture exploration. Traditional manual modeling is notoriously time-consuming and error-prone. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated immense potential in automating hardware code generation. However, existing LLM-assisted design frameworks predominantly target highly structured or well-defined design specifications. In practical engineering scenarios, raw datasheets typically encompass lengthy, complex, and highly unstructured information. Consequently, reliable code generation directly from these raw datasheets suffers from severe challenges, including context vanishing and logical hallucinations.To overcome this critical bottleneck, this paper proposes DS2SC-Agent(Datasheet-to-SystemC-Agent): the first end-to-end, fully automated generation pipeline that translates raw datasheets directly into SystemC chiplet models. This system establishes a highly efficient multi-agent collaborative framework. By decoupling the intricate modeling tasks, the proposed pipeline orchestrates a fully automated workflow encompassing unstructured long-document parsing, SystemC core code construction, testbench stimulus generation, and adaptive closed-loop debugging. We comprehensively evaluate the proposed framework on representative single-function chiplets across the analog, digital, and radio frequency (RF) domains--specifically, a Limiting Amplifier (LA), a Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) module, and a Power Amplifier (PA). The evaluation demonstrates that our pipeline seamlessly processes complex real-world datasheets to consistently generate functionally correct SystemC models. This provides a highly efficient and reliable paradigm for agile model library construction while drastically minimizing manual intervention.

</details>


### [307] [IMMSched: Interruptible Multi-DNN Scheduling via Parallel Multi-Particle Optimizing Subgraph Isomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21659)
*Boran Zhao,Hetian Liu,Zihang Yuan,Yanbin Hu,Wenzhe Zhao,Tian Xia,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了IMMSched，一种面向边缘加速器的中断式调度框架，通过并行子图同构方法解决了多DNN任务不可预测到达时的实时调度难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘加速器上多DNN工作负载的不可预测任务到达时间对中断式调度提出了需求，但现有抢占式框架假设任务到达时间已知且依赖CPU离线调度，运行时开销大且难以处理不可预测任务到达；此外，多DNN调度需要在有限时间内求解大规模有向无环图上的NP难子图同构问题，极具挑战性。

Method: 提出IMMSched方法：1) 结合多粒子优化与Ullmann算法的并行子图同构方法，基于概率连续松弛方案消除串行数据依赖；2) 采用量化调度方案和硬件架构中的全局控制器，结合多粒子结果进行共识引导探索。

Result: IMMSched在调度延迟和能耗方面实现了数量级的降低，使边缘加速器能够实时执行不可预测的DNN任务。

Conclusion: IMMSched成功解决了边缘加速器上不可预测DNN任务的实时调度问题，通过并行化方法和硬件协同设计显著提升了调度效率和能效，为边缘计算场景下的多DNN工作负载调度提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The growing demand for multi-DNN workloads with unpredictable task arrival times has highlighted the need for interruptible scheduling on edge accelerators. However, existing preemptive frameworks typically assume known task arrival times and rely on CPU-based offline scheduling, which incurs heavy runtime overhead and struggles to handle unpredictable task arrivals. Even worse, prior studies have shown that multi-DNN scheduling requires solving an NP-hard subgraph isomorphism problem on large directed acyclic graphs within limited time, which is extremely challenging. To tackle this, we propose IMMSched, a parallel subgraph isomorphism method that combines Multi-Particle Optimization with the Ullmann algorithm based on a probabilistic continuous-relaxation scheme, eliminating the serial data dependencies of previous works. Finally, a quantized scheduling scheme and a global controller in the hardware architecture further combine multi-particle results for consensus-guided exploration. Evaluations demonstrate that IMMSched achieves orders-of-magnitude reductions in scheduling latency and energy consumption, enabling real-time execution of unpredictable DNN tasks on edge accelerators.

</details>


### [308] [Quantifying Uncertainty in FMEDA Safety Metrics: An Error Propagation Approach for Enhanced ASIC Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21770)
*Antonino Armato,Christian Kehl,Sebastian Fischer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出将误差传播理论引入FMEDA安全指标计算中，通过量化SPFM和LFM的最大偏差和置信区间来衡量分析质量，并引入误差重要性标识符（EII）来识别不确定性的主要来源，从而提高了ASIC功能安全验证的透明度和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的FMEDA安全指标（如SPFM和LFM）依赖于失效模式分布（FMD）和诊断覆盖率（DC）估计的精确度，这种依赖性往往导致显著的、未量化的不确定性，并且依赖于专家判断，从而影响了安全分析的质量，这是功能安全领域长期存在的开放性问题。

Method: 将误差传播理论引入FMEDA安全指标的计算中，量化SPFM和LFM的最大偏差并提供置信区间；同时引入误差重要性标识符（EII）来精确定位不确定性的主要来源，指导有针对性的改进措施。

Result: 该方法能够直接衡量安全分析的质量，提供量化的置信区间，并能准确识别不确定性来源，从而显著提高了FMEDA分析的透明度和可信度，为ASIC安全验证提供了更稳健的保障。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了功能安全领域长期存在的FMEDA指标不确定性量化问题，为ISO 26262合规性验证提供了更可靠的技术手段，使得ASIC功能安全验证更加透明、可信和稳健。

Abstract: Accurate and reliable safety metrics are paramount for functional safety verification of ASICs in automotive systems. Traditional FMEDA (Failure Modes, Effects, and Diagnostic Analysis) metrics, such as SPFM (Single Point Fault Metric) and LFM (Latent Fault Metric), depend on the precision of failure mode distribution (FMD) and diagnostic coverage (DC) estimations. This reliance can often leads to significant, unquantified uncertainties and a dependency on expert judgment, compromising the quality of the safety analysis. This paper proposes a novel approach that introduces error propagation theory into the calculation of FMEDA safety metrics. By quantifying the maximum deviation and providing confidence intervals for SPFM and LFM, our method offers a direct measure of analysis quality. Furthermore, we introduce an Error Importance Identifier (EII) to pinpoint the primary sources of uncertainty, guiding targeted improvements. This approach significantly enhances the transparency and trustworthiness of FMEDA, enabling more robust ASIC safety verification for ISO 26262 compliance, addressing a longstanding open question in the functional safety community.

</details>


### [309] [Convolutions Predictable Offloading to an Accelerator: Formalization and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21792)
*Benjamin Husson,Mohammed Belcaïd,Thomas Carle,Claire Pagetti*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化方法来解决CNN在专用加速器上因片上内存不足而需要分步执行的问题，通过约束编码寻找最优卸载策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络需要大量乘累加操作，专用加速器的片上内存通常不足以存储CNN层计算所需的全部数据，因此必须分步执行计算，需要优化这些卸载步骤以实现实时性约束。

Method: 作者形式化了卸载步骤序列，将形式化方法应用于最先进的卷积分解技术，并使用一组约束条件对问题进行编码，以寻找在执行时长方面最优的策略，同时开发了基于Python的模拟器进行深入分析。

Result: 建立了一套形式化框架来描述CNN在内存受限加速器上的分步执行过程，并通过约束求解方法找到了最优卸载策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的形式化方法和约束编码方法能够有效分析并计算出在片上内存受限情况下的最优CNN执行策略，为CNN加速器设计提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) require a large number of multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations. To meet real-time constraints, they often need to be executed on specialized accelerators composed of an on-chip memory and a processing unit. However, the on-chip memory is often insufficient to store all the data required to compute a CNN layer. Thus, the computation must be performed in several offloading steps. We formalise such sequences of steps and apply our formalism to a state of the art decomposition of convolutions. In order to find optimal strategies in terms of duration, we encode the problem with a set of constraints. A Python-based simulator allows to analyse in-depth computed strategies.

</details>
